Disoriented\nWithout a compass, we cannot orient our maps!=ZH=迷失方向\n沒有指南針，我們無法繪製地圖！

Mage=ZH=法師
Undead=ZH=亡靈
Demon=ZH=惡魔
Stone=ZH=石化
Magic User=ZH=法師
Warrior=ZH=戰士
The base fighter-type in The Bard's Tale, warriors can use nearly every weapon there is. For every 4 levels of experience after the 1st, Warriors get an extra attack ability in combat.=ZH=戰士是《冰城傳奇》中的基礎戰鬥型職業，幾乎能使用所有武器。從第1級之後，每提升4級經驗等級，戰士在戰鬥中就會獲得一次額外攻擊能力。
Paladin=ZH=聖騎士
Paladins are fighters who have sworn to abstain from all evil and to uphold honor and purity in all places. They can use most weapons and some that no other fighters can. They get multiple attacks at higher levels. They also have a greatly increased resistance to evil magic.=ZH=聖騎士是宣誓摒棄一切邪惡、在所有地方堅守榮譽與純潔的戰士。他們能使用大多數武器，甚至包括其他戰士無法使用的某些武器。在高等級時可獲得多次攻擊。他們對邪惡魔法也有極高的抗性。
Rogue=ZH=盜賊
A professional thief with so-so combat ability, the rogue can hide in shadows, and leap out at distant enemies to land critical hits. They can also search for traps and disarm them. Without a rogue your party will pay very dearly for the booty it wins.=ZH=盜賊是戰鬥能力平平的專業小偷，能隱藏在陰影中，並躍出攻擊遠處的敵人造成暴擊。他們還能搜尋陷阱並將其解除。沒有盜賊，你的隊伍將為獲得的戰利品付出極其慘痛的代價。
Bard=ZH=吟遊詩人
The Bard is a wandering minstrel. You'll see them with a tankard of ale in front of them in the less reputable taverns -- the rowdier the better. Their song magic can help your party immensely in and out of combat.=ZH=吟遊詩人是流浪的樂手。你會在名聲較差的酒館裡看到他們，面前擺著一杯麥芽酒——越吵鬧的地方越好。他們的歌曲魔法能在戰鬥內外極大地幫助你的隊伍。
Hunter=ZH=獵人
An assassin, a mercenary, a ninja. The hunter can use most weapons, and has the ability (which grows with experience) to do critical hits in combat (i.e., to attack a nerve center or other vital area and instantly kill an opponent). A good skill.=ZH=刺客、傭兵、忍者。獵人能使用大多數武器，並擁有隨經驗成長的能力，可在戰鬥中造成暴擊（即攻擊神經中樞或其他要害部位，瞬間殺死對手）。這是個好技能。
Monk=ZH=武僧
A martial artist, an almost inhuman fighting machine trained to fight without weapons or armor. The monk can use them, but, at higher levels particularly, often does better without.=ZH=武僧是武術家，一種近乎非人的戰鬥機器，受過訓練能在不攜帶武器或盔甲的情況下戰鬥。武僧可以使用它們，但在高等級時，通常不裝備反而表現更好。
Conjurer=ZH=咒術師
Conjurers deal in the physical creation and manifestation of real things (like fire, light, healing).=ZH=咒術師擅長實際創造與顯化真實事物（例如火焰、光線、治療）。
Magician=ZH=幻術師
Magicians deal with magic as it affects physical objects (i.e., enchanting a sword, making armor stronger, making a dungeon wall disappear).=ZH=幻術師處理影響實體物品的魔法（例如附魔劍、強化盔甲、讓地下城牆壁消失）。
Sorcerer=ZH=術士
Sorcerers are magic users who deal with the creation and manipulation of illusion. Due to the power of sorcerer spells, this class is not available to newly created characters.=ZH=術士是擅長創造與操控幻象的魔法使用者。由於術士法術的強大力量，這個職業不開放給新創建的角色。
Wizard=ZH=巫師
Wizards are magic users who are dedicated to the summoning and binding of various supernatural creatures. These creatures are not friendly to humankind and trying to control them is extremely hazardous. The Wizard-class is not available to new characters either.=ZH=巫師是專注於召喚與束縛各種超自然生物的魔法使用者。這些生物對人類並不友善，試圖控制它們極其危險。巫師職業也不開放給新角色。
Archmage=ZH=大法師
These magic users have learned at least three levels for each of the previous four magic user classes. The Archmage is one of the most powerful and important characters in The Destiny Knight because an Archmage can do almost anything. Remember, Lagoth Zanta is an Archmage... fight fire with fire.=ZH=這些魔法使用者已精通前四個魔法職業類別中每個至少三級。大法師是《天命勇士》中最強大且最重要的角色之一，因為大法師幾乎無所不能。記住，Lagoth Zanta 就是一位大法師……以火攻火。
Chronomancer=ZH=時空術士
Chronomancers are the travel experts of the magic users. Before becoming one, a magic user must master all the spells of any three mage classes. And once you become a Chronomancer, you lose the power to use all the spells you previously learned. It may sound unfair, but the Chronomancer needs to focus all of his or her attention on the important dimension teleport spells. Don't think you can get around without this spellcaster, because you just can't.=ZH=時空術士是法師當中的旅行專家。在成為時空術士之前，法師必須精通任三種法師職業的所有法術。而一旦成為時空術士，你將失去使用先前所學所有法術的能力。這聽起來或許不公平，但時空術士必須將全部心力專注於重要的次元傳送法術。別以為少了這個施法者也能過關，因為你絕對辦不到。
Geomancer=ZH=大地術士
Fighters who want to convert to a life of a magic can become Geomancers. Once they choose to change, they can still use the armor, weapons, and magic items that the fighter classes use. Beware: Bards lose their songs, Warriors lose their multiple attacks, Hunters lose their critical hit ability, and Monks lose their armor class bonus and multiple attacks. It's part of the trade off. To become a Geomancer, you must find the one special location that performs the rite of passage to this mage class.=ZH=想要轉換成魔法生活的戰士可以成為大地術士。一旦他們選擇轉變，仍然可以使用戰士職業適用的護甲、武器與魔法物品。請注意：吟遊詩人會失去他們的歌曲，戰士會失去多重攻擊，獵人會失去暴擊能力，武僧則會失去防禦等級加值與多重攻擊。這是取捨的一部分。要成為大地術士，你必須找到某個特殊地點，進行晉升此法師職業的入門儀式。
Monster=ZH=怪物
Illusion=ZH=幻象
Human=ZH=人類
While possibly being of hardier stock, this character is nonetheless like you or me.=ZH=雖然體格可能較為強健，但這個角色終究與你我無異。
Elf=ZH=精靈
Patterned after the Tolkien elf, the elf is slight of build, frequently taller and nimbler than a human, and very inclined to magic.=ZH=以托爾金筆下的精靈為藍本，精靈身形纖細，通常比人類更高挑、更敏捷，且極具魔法天賦。
Dwarf=ZH=矮人
The Dwarven people are short and stout, extremely strong and healthy, but not amazingly intelligent (i.e. excellent fighters).=ZH=矮人族身材矮壯，極其強壯健康，但智力並不特別出眾（也就是說，他們是出色的戰士）。
Hobbit=ZH=哈比人
Hobbits are slightly smaller than Dwarves but are nimble and dexterous. Just the right make-up for a rogue.=ZH=哈比人比矮人略小，但身手矯健靈巧。正是當盜賊的料。
Half-Elf=ZH=半精靈
These crossbreeds are usually blond and fair-skinned, like elves, and get some added size and strength from their human ancestry.=ZH=這些混血兒通常像精靈一樣金髮白膚，並從人類血統中獲得了一些體型和力量上的加成。
Half-Orc=ZH=半獸人
An orc is a large, goblin-like creature often found working for evil wizards. The Half-orc, being half human, is not quite as despicable as his orc parent, but you wouldn't want to date one. They are tough but incredibly stupid.=ZH=獸人是體型龐大、類似哥布林的生物，常為邪惡巫師效力。半獸人因具有一半人類血統，不像其獸人親族那般可憎，但你絕對不會想跟牠約會。牠們強悍但極度愚蠢。
Gnome=ZH=地侏
Gnomes closely resemble dwarves, but have less hair and even shorter tempers. They are also more magically inclined, as a rule. Highly intelligent but not very robust.=ZH=地侏與矮人極為相似，但毛髮較少，脾氣也更暴躁。一般來說，他們也更傾向於魔法。智力極高，但體魄不算強健。
Male=ZH=男性
Female=ZH=女性
Strength=ZH=力量
Strength is physical power and chiefly affects the amount of damage a character can do to an opponent in hand-to-hand combat. Make sure your fighting characters are strong.=ZH=力量代表體能，主要影響角色在近身戰鬥中能對敵人造成的傷害量。請確保你的戰鬥型角色擁有足夠的力量。
Intelligence=ZH=智力
Intelligence is mental power. A high intelligence will enable your magic users to get bonus spell points.=ZH=智力代表心智能力。高智力能讓你的魔法使用者獲得額外的法術點數。
Dexterity=ZH=敏捷
Measures agility & nimbleness. A high score makes your characters harder to hit and helps them strike the first blow in combat.=ZH=衡量靈巧與敏捷度。高敏捷值能讓你的角色更難被擊中，並有助於在戰鬥中搶先出手。
Constitution=ZH=體質
Measures healthiness. It takes more damage to kill a character with a high constitution score. This is reflected in bonus "hit points", the character's life span.=ZH=衡量健康程度。體質高的角色需要承受更多傷害才會死亡。這體現在額外的「生命值」上，也就是角色的壽命。
Luck=ZH=幸運
Luck is an ambiguous attribute, as it has a number of unseen effects on gameplay. For example, lucky characters are more likely to resist evil magic and avoid nasty traps.=ZH=幸運是一個模糊的屬性，對遊戲有許多看不見的影響。例如，幸運的角色更容易抵抗邪惡魔法並避開惱人的陷阱。
Hit Points=ZH=生命值
Hit Points measure the damage a character can take before they are killed. The current status is shown in the characters status bar, followed by their current maximum potential.=ZH=生命值衡量角色在死亡前能承受的傷害。當前狀態顯示在角色狀態列中，後方則為目前的最大潛力值。
Spell Points=ZH=法術點數
This shows current spell point status. Points are used up with each cast of a magic spell. If a Conjurer has 20 spell points and casts a Mage Flame spell at a cost of 2 spell points, his new total would be 18. The characters status line lists both their current and maximum spell points.=ZH=此處顯示目前的法術點數狀態。每次施展法術都會消耗法術點數。如果咒術師有20點法術點數，並施展消耗2點法術點數的「法師火焰」法術，那麼新的總數將變為18。角色的狀態列會同時列出目前與最大法術點數。

Falkentyne's Fury=ZH=法肯泰恩之怒
This tune increases the damage your party will do in combat, by driving them into a berserker rage.=ZH=這首曲調能讓你的隊伍陷入狂戰士之怒，提升戰鬥中的傷害輸出。
Seeker's Ballad=ZH=尋覓者之歌
This song will produce light when exploring, and during combat it will increase the party's chance of hitting a foe with a weapon.=ZH=這首歌能在探索時產生光源，戰鬥時則能提升隊伍以武器命中敵人的機率。
Wayland's Watch=ZH=韋蘭德之守護
This song will soothe your savage foes, making them do less damage in combat.=ZH=這首歌能安撫你兇猛的敵人，使他們在戰鬥中造成的傷害降低。
Badh'r Kilnfest=ZH=Badh'r Kilnfest
This is an ancient Elven melody, which will heal the Bard's wounds during travelling, and heal the party's wounds during combat.=ZH=這是一首古老的精靈旋律，能在旅行時治療吟遊詩人的傷口，並在戰鬥中治療隊伍的傷勢。
Traveller's Tune=ZH=旅者之歌
This melody makes the members of your party more dexterous and agile, and thus more difficult to hit.=ZH=這首旋律能讓你的隊伍成員更加靈巧敏捷，因此更難被擊中。
Lucklaran=ZH=Lucklaran
This song sets up a partial "anti-magic" field, which gives party members some increased protection against spell casting.=ZH=這首歌會建立一個部分「反魔法」力場，為隊伍成員提供額外的法術防護。
Archer's Tune=ZH=射手之歌
Doubles the party's missile damage, and cuts the missile damage inflicted by a foe in half. Missile weapons are those weapons that are thrown or shot such as arrows, spears, and axes.=ZH=使隊伍的遠程傷害加倍，並將敵人造成的遠程傷害減半。遠程武器是指那些投擲或射出的武器，例如箭矢、長矛和飛斧。
Spell Song=ZH=法術之歌
Spells do increased damage to monsters in close range and reduces the damage done by enemy spells.=ZH=法術對近距離的怪物造成更多傷害，並減少敵方法術造成的傷害。
Bonus to saving roll. This means the party is less likely to be damaged by magic and traps.=ZH=豁免檢定加成。這意味著隊伍較不容易受到魔法和陷阱的傷害。
Sanctuary Score=ZH=庇護之曲
Lowers the Armor Class for all party members.=ZH=降低所有隊伍成員的防禦等級。
Melee March=ZH=近戰進行曲
Increases the effectiveness of your party's melee combat, reducing damage taken and also increases the damage points inflicted on enemies.=ZH=提升隊伍近戰的效能，減少受到的傷害，並增加對敵人造成的傷害點數。
Zanduvar Carack=ZH=Zanduvar Carack
Protection from traps when played under normal conditions, but heals during combat.=ZH=在一般情況下演奏可防護陷阱，但在戰鬥中則會治療傷勢。
Rhyme of Duotime=ZH=雙時韻律
Regenerates spell points at twice the normal speed when played under normal conditions, and provides extra attacks during combat.=ZH=在一般情況下演奏時，法術點數恢復速度加倍；在戰鬥中則提供額外攻擊次數。
Watchwood Melody=ZH=守望林旋律
Creates light. May work even in anti-magic zones. The party will be resistant to disorientation, allowing them to automap when they have been teleported, and spinners won't affect them when they are turning.=ZH=產生光源。即使在反魔法區域也可能生效。隊伍將對迷向效果具有抗性，使他們在被傳送後仍能自動繪製地圖，且轉向時不受迷向器影響。
Creates light. May work even in anti-magic zones.=ZH=產生光源。即使在反魔法區域也可能生效。
Sir Robin's Tune=ZH=羅賓爵士之歌
This lets you run away from attackers as long as the combat has not yet begun. During combat, this keeps the monsters from calling for additional help.=ZH=只要戰鬥尚未開始，這首歌能讓你逃離攻擊者。在戰鬥中，這首歌能阻止怪物呼叫更多援軍。
Safety Song=ZH=安全之歌
Sets up an anti-monster aura, so foes won't randomly attack you.=ZH=建立一個反怪物光環，使敵人不會隨機攻擊你。
Bringaround Ballad=ZH=迴旋敘事曲
In non-combat situations, this rejuvenates the Bard's hit points. During combat, this song will affect everyone in your party, including the Bard.=ZH=在非戰鬥情況下，這首歌能恢復吟遊詩人的生命值。在戰鬥中，這首歌會影響隊伍中的每個人，包括吟遊詩人。
Kiel's Overture=ZH=基爾序曲
In non-combat situations, this calls up a compass so you can get your bearings. During combat, this casts the monster-frying Trebuchet spell for one round.=ZH=在非戰鬥情況下，這首歌會召喚一個指南針，讓你能夠辨別方向。在戰鬥中，這首歌會施放一回合的「投石機」法術來轟炸怪物。
Minstrel Shield=ZH=吟遊詩人盾牌
In non-combat situations, this lowers your armor class. During combat, it also partially shields your party so they only take half damage from monster attacks.=ZH=在非戰鬥狀態下，此盾牌能降低你的防禦等級。戰鬥時，它還能部分遮蔽你的隊伍，使他們只承受怪物攻擊的一半傷害。

<color=#ffff80>All</color>\nShow all the spells that this caster currently knows. Filter them further by typing keywords below.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>全部</color>\n顯示施法者目前已知的所有法術。可在下方輸入關鍵字進一步篩選。
<color=#ffff80>Exploration</color>\nSpells that help the party during their travels, such as creating light, detecting the unseen, aid in movement or disarm traps.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>探索</color>\n在旅途中幫助隊伍的法術，例如創造光源、偵測隱藏事物、輔助移動或解除陷阱。
<color=#ffff80>Healing</color>\nSpells that replenish hit points, cure status conditions, remove curses or resurrect the party members.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>治療</color>\n恢復生命值、治癒狀態異常、解除詛咒或復活隊伍成員的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Buffs</color>\nSpells that increase the strength, armor or general battle skills of individuals or the entire the party.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>增益</color>\n提升個人或整個隊伍的力量、防禦或一般戰鬥技能的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Single Target</color>\nSpells that do direct damage to a single enemy.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>單一目標</color>\n對單一敵人造成直接傷害的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Multiple Targets</color>\nSpells that do damage to one or more groups of enemies.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>多重目標</color>\n對一個或多個敵人群組造成傷害的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Curses</color>\nSpells that reduce the combat effectiveness of enemies, or inflict negative status conditions on the enemies such as poison.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>詛咒</color>\n降低敵人戰鬥效能，或對敵人施加中毒等負面狀態的法術。
<color=#ffff80>Summoning</color>\nSpells that summon monsters or allies to join your party.=ZH=<color=#ffff80>召喚</color>\n召喚怪物或盟友加入你隊伍的法術。

Anti-Magic=ZH=反魔法
Causes the ground to absorb a portion of the magical energies cast at the party, frequently allowing the members to escape all damage. Also aids in disbelieving illusions and in turning back magical fire, like a dragon's breath.=ZH=讓地面吸收一部分朝隊伍施放的魔法能量，經常能使隊員完全免於傷害。同時有助於看破幻象，以及抵擋龍息之類的魔法火焰。

Disrupt Illusion=ZH=破除幻象
This spell will destroy any illusion fighting the party, and any new illusions created later in the combat. It will also point out any doppelgangers in the party.=ZH=此法術將摧毀任何與隊伍作戰的幻象，以及在戰鬥中後續產生的新幻象。它也會指出隊伍中的任何變形怪。
Divine Intervention=ZH=神聖干預
This powerful spell earns its name by doing the following: 1) Turns illusionary characters into real characters; 2) Cures characters of all illnesses but age; and 3) Restores all hit points to the party. If you're in combat, it also does the following: 1) Lowers your armor class, saving throw, to hit, and damage by 20 points; 2) Increases your attack by eight points; and 3) Casts Mangar's Mallet.=ZH=這個強大的法術名副其實，效果如下：1) 將幻影角色轉變為真實角色；2) 治癒角色所有疾病（衰老除外）；3) 恢復隊伍所有生命值。若在戰鬥中使用，還會：1) 降低你的防禦等級、豁免檢定、命中與傷害20點；2) 提升你的攻擊力8點；3) 施展「曼加的槌子」。
Dragon Breath=ZH=龍息術
Allows the mage to breathe fire at a group of foes, doing {0}-{1} points of damage to each.=ZH=讓法師能對一群敵人噴吐火焰，對每個目標造成 {0} 至 {1} 點傷害。
DreamSpell=ZH=夢境法術
This powerful spell earns its name by doing the following: 1) Turns illusionary characters into real characters; 2) Cures characters of all illnesses but age; and 3) Restores all hit points to the party. If you're in combat, it also does the following: 1) Lowers your armor class, saving throw, to hit, and damage by 20 points; 2) Increases your attack by eight points; and 3) Casts Mangar's Mallet. When used out of combat, the Dreamspell can be used to escape the current dungeon.=ZH=這個強大的法術名副其實，效果如下：1) 將幻影角色轉變為真實角色；2) 治癒角色所有疾病（衰老除外）；3) 恢復隊伍所有生命值。若在戰鬥中使用，還會：1) 降低你的防禦等級、豁免檢定、命中與傷害20點；2) 提升你的攻擊力8點；3) 施展「曼加的槌子」。若在非戰鬥狀態下使用，夢之法術可用來逃離當前的地下城。

This spell causes the weapon (or hands) of a party member to be covered with a magical field, which causes them to do an additional 2-8 points of damage.=ZH=此法術使一名隊員的武器（或雙手）覆蓋一層魔法力場，使其額外造成 2-8 點傷害。

An electric spell that creates a pyrotechnical storm over a group of monsters, inflicting {0} to {1} damage points.=ZH=一個閃電法術，在一群怪物上方引發一場煙火風暴，造成 {0} 到 {1} 點傷害。
Wall Warp=ZH=牆壁扭曲
Dissolve the dungeon wall that the party if facing. Lasts until the party leaves the dungeon.=ZH=溶解隊伍面對的地下城牆壁。效果持續到隊伍離開地下城為止。

Causes a spray of energy to spring from the caster's extended finger, sizzling a group of opponents for {0}-{1} points of damage.=ZH=從施法者伸出的指尖噴出一道能量束，灼燒一群對手，造成 {0}-{1} 點傷害。

This spell creates an illusionary wolf to join the party. This and other illusions are only effective as long as an enemy "believes" them. Depending on power and location, the monster may see through the illusion, and cause it to vanish.=ZH=此法術創造出一隻幻影狼加入隊伍。此幻影與其他幻術僅在敵人「相信」時有效。根據力量與地點，怪物可能看穿幻影，使其消失。

Any foe at whom this spell is cast is likely to be turned old, thus reducing their ability to attack and defend in combat.=ZH=任何被施以此法術的敵人很可能變得衰老，從而降低其在戰鬥中攻擊與防禦的能力。

"When your party is ready, set out into town. Be sure to visit Garth's Shoppe to the north and equip yourself for battle. He has the finest weapons and armour in Skara Brae, and indeed is the only shoppe brave enough to open these days!"=ZH=「當你的隊伍準備好之後，就出發進城吧。記得去北邊的加斯商店，為戰鬥做好裝備。他那裡有斯卡拉布雷最好的武器與護甲，說真的，現在還敢開門營業的商店也就只剩他這一家了！」
"The city has an abundance of temples that are open night and day to heal your wounds, and I believe Roscoe is still doing good business restoring magic points for your mages."\n\n=ZH=「城裡有許多神廟，日夜開放為你治療傷勢，而且我相信羅斯科還在繼續做他的好生意，幫你的法師恢復法術點數。」\n\n
"And when your bard gets thirsty, be sure to visit one of the many Inns and Taverns. The Scarlet Bard, to the south, has the best selection in town!"=ZH=「而當你的吟遊詩人渴了，記得去拜訪其中一間旅館或酒館。南邊的『緋紅吟遊詩人』，可是全城選擇最豐富的地方！」

You have stepped out into the town of Skara Brae for the first time. Would you like a quick tutorial on the game controls before you proceed?=ZH=你初次踏入了斯卡拉布雷鎮。在繼續前進之前，是否要快速學習遊戲操作教學？
Use W,A,S,D to move around the town, or the cursor keys. You can also left click on the game view to move around.\n\nHold down right mouse on the game view and you can look around to take in the view.=ZH=使用 W、A、S、D 鍵或方向鍵在鎮上移動。你也可以在遊戲畫面上按左鍵來移動。\n\n在遊戲畫面上按住右鍵，即可環顧四周欣賞景色。
There is a Pause button at the top left of the game view that gives access to graphics, audio and game settings, as well as saving or loading the game.\n\nThere is an Actions button on the top right of the game view that will drop down a ribbon of actions to choose from. These actions are not usable during game events.=ZH=遊戲畫面左上角有一個「暫停」按鈕，可用來調整圖形、音效與遊戲設定，以及儲存或載入遊戲進度。\n\n遊戲畫面右上角有一個「行動」按鈕，點擊後會下拉一排行動選項。請注意，這些行動在遊戲事件進行中無法使用。
You can change the speed that combat messages scroll by pressing the 'greater than' and 'less than' keys, or by adjusting the slider in the 'game settings' menu.=ZH=你可以按下「大於」與「小於」鍵，或在「遊戲設定」選單中調整滑桿，來改變戰鬥訊息捲動的速度。
Pressing 1 to 7 lets you view the stats of one of your party members.\n\nIf you left click and drag with your mouse on one of the party member names below, you can change their position in the party. Only the first 4 character slots can be hit by enemy melee attacks.\n\n=ZH=按下 1 到 7 鍵可檢視隊伍中某位成員的狀態。\n\n如果你在下方任一隊員名稱上按住左鍵並拖曳滑鼠，即可改變他們在隊伍中的位置。只有前 4 個角色欄位會受到敵人的近戰攻擊。\n\n
You should try to keep a character slot free so that you can summon creatures or allow roving monsters to join your party.=ZH=你應該保留一個角色空位，這樣才能召喚生物或讓遊蕩的怪物加入你的隊伍。
Below the game view, totals of your party's gold and inventory items are shown.\n\nAny item that is not equipped by a character will go into the communal inventory and is accessible through any player's inventory screen.=ZH=在遊戲畫面下方，會顯示隊伍的總金幣與道具庫存數量。\n\n任何未由角色裝備的物品都會進入共用庫存，並可透過任一角色的道具欄畫面存取。

"Defeating him will not be easy, but I'm sure you will find the help you need in this guild."\n\n=ZH=「擊敗他並不容易，但我相信你們能在這座公會中找到所需的幫助。」\n\n
"When your party is ready, set out into the town, or indeed exit through the city gates and traverse the wilderness to the other five great towns in this region of Caith."=ZH=「當你的隊伍準備就緒時，就出發前往城鎮，或者乾脆穿過城門，橫越荒野，前往凱斯地區的其他五座大城。」
"You'll find Garth's Shoppe not far to the south, where you can equip yourself for battle with a fine assortment of weapons and armor."\n\n=ZH=「往南不遠處就能找到加斯的商店，你可以在那裡用各式精良的武器和護甲裝備自己，準備好迎接戰鬥。」\n\n
"Stop by a temple when you are in need of healing, and Roscoe's Energy Emporium is always willing to exchange your gold for a quick spell recharge."\n\n=ZH=「需要療傷時，記得去一趟神廟；而羅斯柯的能量補給站隨時歡迎你用金幣換取快速的法術充能。」\n\n
"Your bards can wet their whistles if their voices get dry at one of our fine taverns, and if they want to learn some new songs, look around for a man known as the Scarlet Bard.\n\n=ZH=「如果你的吟遊詩人嗓子乾了，可以在我們某間上好的酒館裡潤潤喉；要是他們想學幾首新歌，就去找一位名叫『緋紅吟遊詩人』的人吧。」\n\n
"While you're looking, feel free to drop in to one of our Casinos for a hand of BlackJack. Make sure to use our banks to keep your gold safe, you never know when you'll need to make a hasty exit from a dungeon."=ZH=「在你尋找的途中，不妨順道去我們的賭場玩一局二十一點。記得利用銀行保管好你的金幣，你永遠不知道何時會需要從地下城匆忙逃離。」
"Before I go, if you should come across the King's daughter, tell her the King wishes for her presence immediately. Saradon's Workshop=ZH=薩拉頓的工作坊
A long corridor stretches out before you, through walls stacked to the ceiling with books. You can almost feel the magic in the air.=ZH=一條長廊在你面前延伸開來，兩側牆壁堆滿了直達天花板的書籍。你幾乎能感受到空氣中瀰漫的魔法氣息。
A sign on the wall reads:\n\n"Saradon's Workshop. The uninvited are not welcome."=ZH=牆上掛著一塊牌子，寫著：\n\n「薩拉頓的工作坊。未受邀者謝絕進入。」
You find a scrap of parchment in the handwriting of Saradon. It reads:\n\nIf you're reading this, you have already proven yourself in my maze of teleports. Respect my workshop please, but take what you need. You may find something of use. Farewell and good luck.=ZH=你找到了一張羊皮紙碎片，上面是薩拉頓的筆跡，寫著：\n\n如果你正在讀這段文字，表示你已經在我的傳送迷宮中證明了自己。請尊重我的工作坊，但需要什麼就拿吧。你也許能找到些有用的東西。珍重，祝你好運。
You stumble over an open book on the floor.\n\nIt seems that Saradon maintained a correspondence with Longinus, the mad prophet of Skara Brae. Longinus’ replies seem to have consisted of nothing more than inscrutable rhymes. You pick up one such example and read it...\n\nBy tor and loch by burn and tarn.\nYour fate lies in the lap of Narn.=ZH=你被地上的一本翻開的書絆了一下。\n\n看來薩拉頓與史卡拉布雷的瘋狂先知隆基努斯保持著書信往來。隆基努斯的回覆似乎盡是些難以理解的韻文。你撿起其中一封讀了起來……\n\n越過山丘與湖泊，穿過溪流與池塘。\n你的命運，掌握在納恩的膝上。
More notes from Longinus catch your eye, these just as cryptic as the previous\n\nThe greatest heroes number seven.\nAssume their roles and rise to heaven.=ZH=隆基努斯的更多筆記吸引了你的目光，這些和之前的一樣晦澀難懂：\n\n最偉大的英雄共有七位。\n扮演他們的角色，升天歸位。
On a low table there is yet another piece of nonsensical poetry from the mad prophet:\n\nThough high you fly or low you stoop.\nYou will never be worthy of the soup.=ZH=矮几上又放著一首來自瘋狂先知的無意義詩歌：\n\n任你高飛或低俯。\n你永遠不配那碗湯。
You find what seems to be a longer draft of the letter you received from Saradon.=ZH=你找到了一封似乎是薩拉頓寄給你的信件更長的草稿。
My name is Saradon, a wizard of some fame and little fortune. Though you know me not, and we have never met, I feel that I have known you all my life. For the story of your deeds, your entanglement with Mangar and his evil horde in Skara Brae, flows from the lips of every Bard throughout the Realm.=ZH=吾名薩拉頓，一位小有名氣卻囊中羞澀的巫師。你雖不識我，我們也未曾謀面，但我卻覺得彷彿認識了你一輩子。因為你的事蹟——你在斯卡拉布雷與曼加爾及其邪惡大軍的糾葛——早已隨著王國中每位吟遊詩人的歌謠傳頌開來。
For this reason, for your courage and experience, I have contacted you above all others. For now we face an evil much greater than Mangar's. An evil that threatens not the safety and tranquility of but one city, nae... an evil that threatens the existence of the entire Realm and all the cities and citizens within!=ZH=正因如此，鑑於你的勇氣與經驗，我選擇聯繫你而非他人。如今我們面對的邪惡遠比曼加爾更為強大。這股邪惡威脅的不僅是一座城市的安寧……不，它威脅的是整個王國的存在，以及其中所有的城市與子民！
Recall now the lessons of your childhood. The story of Turin, the Archmage who forged the Destiny Wand in the molten depths of the holy mountain Krontor. The Destiny Wand, the uniting force which has rallied armies to the aid of the Realm, and has maintained peace and prosperity for the last 700 years.=ZH=現在，請回想你兒時聽過的故事。關於大法師圖林的故事——他在聖山克朗托的熔岩深處鍛造了命運之杖。命運之杖，那股團結的力量，曾集結大軍保衛王國，並在過去七百年間維持了和平與繁榮。
But look around you now, where has the power of the Destiny Wand gone? Lawless mercenaries from the neighboring kingdom of Lestradae have invaded the Realm and now run rampant through the streets. Even in Tangramayne, our capital! It is they who have taken the Destiny Wand, with the guidance of an evil Archmage known as Lagoth Zanta.=ZH=但看看你四周，命運之杖的力量如今何在？來自鄰國萊斯特拉迪的無法傭兵已入侵王國，在街頭橫行無忌。甚至在我們的首都坦格拉梅因也是如此！正是他們，在一位名為拉戈斯·贊塔的邪惡大法師指引下，奪走了命運之杖。
Although Lagoth has thwarted each of my attempts to gain knowledge of his powers and location, I have been able to discover that the Destiny Wand lies broken in seven pieces, at seven different locations. Lagoth has placed each fragment of the scepter within a Snare of Death - a puzzle room that will require all the wisdom and cunning at your disposal in order for you to survive.=ZH=儘管拉戈斯挫敗了我每一次探查他力量與藏身之處的嘗試，但我仍設法發現命運權杖已斷裂成七塊碎片，散落在七個不同的地點。拉戈斯將權杖的每一塊碎片都放置在「死亡陷阱」之中——這是一個謎題房間，你必須運用所有的智慧與機智才能存活下來。
Do you believe in legends?\n\nLegend states that, Unimaginable power is bestowed upon the one who reforges the Destiny Wand... Regardless of whether or not you hold faith in the stuff of legend, you must defeat Lagoth Zanta, regain the seven fragments, and reforge the Destiny Wand - it is the only way to save the Realm.=ZH=你相信傳說嗎？\n\n傳說記載，無可想像的力量將賜予重鑄命運權杖之人……無論你是否相信傳說中的事物，你必須擊敗拉戈斯·贊塔，奪回七塊碎片，並重鑄命運權杖——這是拯救王國的唯一方法。
Now I must hurry, for in my attempts to glean knowledge of Lagoth, he has discovered my prying magic and even now his mercenaries approach. I care not for my own safety, but I pray that my magic is still strong enough to deliver this letter into your hands.=ZH=現在我必須趕緊行動，因為在我試圖窺探拉戈斯知識的過程中，他已發現了我的探查魔法，此刻他的傭兵正步步逼近。我並不關心自己的安危，但我祈禱我的魔法仍足夠強大，能將這封信送到你手中。
My workshop contains powerful quest knowledge which I have gathered using magic and other, more physical, methods. Use it to help you during your quest. My only regret is that I cannot personally be there to counsel you during your quest. May fate smile upon you... and upon me.\n\nSaradon.=ZH=我的工坊中藏有強大的任務知識，這些知識是我透過魔法以及其他更為實際的方法所收集而來。請善用它們來協助你的旅程。我唯一的遺憾是無法親自在你冒險途中提供建言。願命運眷顧你……也眷顧我。\n\n——薩拉頓。

Warrior=ZH=戰士
Standing before you is a hearty warrior wearing battle-scarred armor and a big grin. You notice the gray shot through his black hair as he warmly welcomes you.=ZH=站在你面前的是一位強壯的戰士，身穿佈滿戰鬥傷痕的鎧甲，臉上掛著燦爛的笑容。你注意到他黑髮中夾雜的灰白，他熱情地歡迎你。
"Well as I live and breathe! I'd thought I'd never see you again since the time we last parted ways."=ZH=「哎呀，我還活著呢！我以為自從上次我們分道揚鑣之後，就再也見不到你了。」

Outpost=ZH=前哨站
The small hut holds the utter cold at bay, but the wind still whistles through small cracks, and the floor is covered with sparkling snowflakes.\n\n=ZH=這間小屋勉強抵擋住刺骨的嚴寒，但寒風仍從細小的裂縫中呼嘯而入，地板上覆蓋著閃閃發亮的雪花。\n\n
Seated at a desk you see the frozen body of a man, perhaps a half-elf. The weather has not been kind to him, and he is, in effect, mummified so you cannot tell how long he has been here.=ZH=你看到一張書桌前坐著一具凍僵的人類屍體，或許是個半精靈。嚴酷的天氣對他毫不留情，他實際上已經木乃伊化，所以你無法判斷他在這裡待了多久。
Despite that confusion about his body's condition, however, the gold wristlets and circlet he wears are styled after the fashion thought over 1000 years old in your world.\n\n=ZH=儘管他對自己身體狀況感到困惑，但他所戴的金色腕環與頭冠，其風格在你們的世界中已被認為是超過一千年前的時尚。\n\n
On the desk you see, beneath a light dusting of snow, a book. Its pages are covered with a bold, flowing hand that, unfortunately, turned into weakened and trembling writing.=ZH=在桌上，你看到一本書，上面覆蓋著一層薄薄的雪。書頁上原本是粗獷流暢的筆跡，但不幸地，後來變成了虛弱顫抖的字跡。
Even a brief glance at it leaves you with the impression this was the dead man's diary.\n\n=ZH=即使只是匆匆一瞥，你也覺得這是那位死者的日記。\n\n
Shall you read the Diary?\n\n=ZH=你要閱讀這本日記嗎？\n\n

Entry 1 ~\n=ZH=第一篇日記 ~\n
"The battle goes poorly, but that is as I would have predicted it. This time we have no hero like Hawkslayer to bolster our militia.=ZH=「戰況不利，但這正如我所預料。這次我們沒有像弒鷹者那樣的英雄來提振民兵士氣。
The first invasion, as the songs sing, would have succeeded but for Lanatir's vast magics.\n\n=ZH=據歌謠所傳，第一次入侵若非拉納提爾施展浩瀚魔法，本該成功。\n\n
How cruel it is, then, that the invaders would have struck Lanatir down in their first attack. Without hero and god, how can we prevail?"=ZH=然而，何其殘酷的是，入侵者在首次攻擊中便擊倒了拉納提爾。失去英雄與神祇，我們如何能取勝？」
Entry 2 ~\n=ZH=第二篇日記 ~\n
"I agree with my brothers that none of the monsters will penetrate Lanatir's tomb, so his Sphere and the Wand of Power are safe until claimed by those capable of piercing our wards.=ZH=「我同意兄弟們的看法，沒有任何怪物能穿透拉納提爾的墓穴，因此他的法球與權能之杖將安全無虞，直到有能力突破我們結界的人前來取走。
It was difficult in creating those wards, powerful magic created them and only the proper spells can destroy them.\n\n=ZH=創造那些結界並不容易，強大的魔法造就了它們，唯有正確的法術才能將其摧毀。\n\n

You gather around Tarjan's dead body, carefully avoiding the steaming streams of vitriolic blood burning into the stone floor, and reality slowly begins to unravel around you.=ZH=你們圍聚在塔爾揚的屍體旁，小心翼翼地避開那腐蝕石地、冒著蒸氣的酸性血液溪流，而你們周圍的現實開始逐漸崩解。
You look up and discover, where there had only been a dark rock ceiling, a brilliantly lit chimney of stone.=ZH=你抬頭一看，發現原本只有黑暗岩石天花板的地方，出現了一道光芒四射的石頭煙囪。
You slowly drift upward.=ZH=你緩緩向上飄升。
Time and space folds in upon itself and you reach a place made completely and utterly of light.=ZH=時間與空間在自身中摺疊，你抵達了一個完全由光芒構成的所在。
Seated in a glowing throne, a man smiles at you. Though he is youthful and handsome, you recognize him instantly as the old man who sent you upon your quest.=ZH=一位男子坐在發光的王座上，對你微笑。儘管他年輕英俊，你立刻認出他就是派你踏上任務的那位老人。
Beside him stands your friend, Hawkslayer.=ZH=他的身旁站著你的朋友，Hawkslayer。
The enthroned figure pulls out a sword and begins to speak...=ZH=端坐於王座上的身影拔出一柄劍，開始說話……
Looking around, you recognise the site where Tarjan was finally defeated. Now quiet, though still the remnants of Tarjan's beasts wander through this realm of evil.=ZH=環顧四周，你認出這裡正是塔爾揚最終被擊敗的地點。如今雖已平靜，但塔爾揚的殘餘魔獸仍在這邪惡領域中遊蕩。

Volume I=ZH=第一卷
THE LEGEND OF SKARA BRAE\n\n\n\nLong ago in the land of Caith, when magic still prevailed, the evil wizard Mangar the Dark threatened a small but harmonious town called Skara Brae. Overnight, all the town's militiamen disappeared, and Mangar froze the surrounding countryside with a spell of Eternal Winter, totally isolating it from any possible help. \n\n\n\nAs evil creatures oozed into the sewers to join Mangar’s shadow crusade, the future of Skara Brae hung in the balance. And who was left to resist? Only a handful of unproven young Warriors, some junior magic users, a couple of Bards barely old enough to drink, and a few out of work Rogues.\n\n\n\nYou are the leader of this ragtag group of freedom fighters. Luckily you have a Bard with you to sing your glories - if you survive - for this is the stuff of legends. \n\n\n\nAnd thus the story begins...=ZH=斯卡拉布雷的傳說\n\n\n許久以前，在凱斯這片土地上，當魔法仍盛行時，邪惡巫師「黑暗曼加」威脅著一個名為斯卡拉布雷的小鎮，那裡原本和諧安寧。一夜之間，鎮上所有民兵消失無蹤，曼加以永恆寒冬的法術冰封了周圍鄉野，使其完全孤立，無法獲得任何援助。\n\n\n隨著邪惡生物滲入下水道，加入曼加的陰影遠征，斯卡拉布雷的未來岌岌可危。而留下來抵抗的又是誰呢？只有一群未經考驗的年輕戰士、幾名初階法師、幾位年紀尚輕的吟遊詩人，以及一些失業的盜賊。\n\n\n你就是這群烏合之眾自由鬥士的領袖。幸運的是，你身邊有一位吟遊詩人，歌頌你的榮耀——如果你能存活下來的話——因為這正是傳奇的素材。\n\n\n於是，故事就此展開……
"The song I sing\nwill tell the tale\nof a cold and wintery day"=ZH=「我所吟詠的樂章，\n將訴說一段往事——\n那段冰封刺骨的凜冬歲月。」

A LETTER RECEIVED\n\n\n\nGreetings, heroes! My name is Saradon, a wizard of some fame and little fortune. Every bard in Caith sings of your victory over Mangar and his vile horde in Skara Brae. Thus, even though we have never met, I am seeking you out.\n\n\n\nOur land now faces a danger even greater than Mangar - an evil that could leave it open to invasion and bloody conquest. The Destiny Wand, which has kept us safe for seven hundred years, has been shattered into seven fragments by the Archmage Lagoth Zanta, and those fragments hidden all across the realm. Until you recover them, reforge the wand, and defeat Lagoth Zanta, Caith is in peril. Save her, I beg you.\n\n\n\nNow I must hurry, for while I was attempting to glean knowledge of Zanta, he sensed my prying magic, and even now his Lestradae mercenaries approach. I care not for my own safety, but I pray my magic will keep this letter safe until it reaches your hands. May fate smile upon you... and me.=ZH=一封收到的信\n\n\n致意，英雄們！我的名字是薩拉東，一位小有名氣卻財運不佳的巫師。凱斯境內每位吟遊詩人都傳唱著你們在斯卡拉布雷擊敗曼加及其邪惡大軍的勝利。因此，即使我們素未謀面，我仍設法尋找你們。\n\n\n我們這片土地如今面臨比曼加更巨大的危險——一種可能讓國門洞開、遭受入侵與血腥征服的邪惡。守護我們七百年之久的命運之杖，已被大法師拉戈斯·贊塔擊碎成七塊碎片，並藏匿於王國各處。在你們尋回碎片、重鑄法杖、擊敗拉戈斯·贊塔之前，凱斯將危在旦夕。我懇求你們，拯救她吧。\n\n\n我必須趕緊動身了，因為當我試圖窺探贊塔的知識時，他察覺到了我的探查魔法，此刻他的萊斯特拉德傭兵正步步逼近。我不在乎自身安危，但我祈禱我的魔法能保護這封信，直到它送達你們手中。願命運眷顧你們……也眷顧我。
Volume III=ZH=第三卷
As I lay dying...\n\n\n\nI leave this record so those who come after will know what horrors that the Mad God Tarjan has perpetrated, and so that they will not be lulled into believing that Skara Brae was his sole target.\n\n\n\nWe held a great festival to celebrate the victory over Mangar. Then Mangar's Master, The Mad God Tarjan, arrived... His shadow ravaged the city and foul creatures burst forth to assault fair Skara Brae. People ran, but Tarjan's minions left none untouched.\n\n Tarjan gloated that Skara Brae was merely one in a series of conquests that would complete his revenge. He laughed when I promised that the legendary Hawkslayer, and those who defeated Mangar, would return again to triumph. As payment for my threat, he gave a swift twist of his jewelled sword into my belly. \n\n\n\nTarjan must be stopped, for upon this rests the fate of more than Skara Brae, more than Caith. Failure will mean the extinction of all life - save for the wretched Tarjan and his servile parasites. Pray for success... and life renewed.=ZH=在我垂死之際……\n\n\n我留下這份記錄，好讓後來者知曉瘋狂之神塔爾揚犯下的恐怖罪行，也讓他們不會誤以為斯卡拉布雷是他唯一的目標。\n\n\n我們舉辦了一場盛大的慶典，慶祝擊敗曼加的勝利。然後，曼加的主人——瘋狂之神塔爾降臨了……他的陰影蹂躪了城市，邪惡的生物蜂擁而出，襲擊美麗的斯卡拉布雷。人們四處奔逃，但塔爾揚的爪牙無一放過。\n\n\n塔爾揚得意洋洋地宣稱，斯卡拉布雷只是他完成復仇的一系列征服中的一環。當我誓言傳奇獵鷹殺手以及那些擊敗曼加的人終將歸來、再次獲勝時，他放聲大笑。作為對我這番威脅的報償，他迅速轉動鑲滿寶石的長劍，刺入我的腹部。\n\n\n必須阻止塔爾揚，因為這關係到的不只是斯卡拉布雷的命運，也不只是凱斯的命運。失敗將意味著所有生命的滅絕——除了可悲的塔爾揚與他那些奴性的寄生蟲。祈求成功……祈求生命重生。
Come hear the tale of Skara Brae -\n\nA god returned to have his way.\n\nCreatures of darkness, spawn of night,\n\nThe Mad One's kin destroyed the site.=ZH=且聽斯卡拉布雷的故事——\n\n一位歸來的神祇，欲遂其意。\n\n黑暗的造物，黑夜的孽種，\n\n狂人之親族，摧毀了此地。

"Welcome, brave heroes. You have succeeded in destroying\n\nthe threat to all reality. As you know, to do this, you slipped\n\nthe bonds of time, and traveled forbidden routes through that\n\nwhich has forever been. You pressed your struggle forward\n\ndespite danger and death, and you accomplished that which\n\nthe gods themselves were unable to do."=ZH=「歡迎，勇敢的英雄們。你們成功摧毀了威脅整個現實的危機。如你們所知，為此你們掙脫了時間的束縛，穿越了永恆存在的禁忌路徑。你們不顧危險與死亡，奮力向前推進，完成了眾神本身也無能為力的事。」
His praise washes over you like a warm ocean wave, and\n\nyou feel strength infuse your body. "In doing what you have\n\ndone, you have proven yourself worthy of nothing less than\n\nthe ultimate reward." Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave outside of the Guild while Legacy Mode Save-At-Guild is active in Tales of the Unknown or The Destiny Knight. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在離開將會遺失所有未存檔的進度，因為在《未知傳奇》或《天命勇士》中啟用「公會存檔傳承模式」時，無法在公會外自動存檔。你確定要離開嗎？
Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave during an active death snare event. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在離開將會遺失所有未存檔的進度，因為在死亡陷阱事件進行中無法自動存檔。你確定要離開嗎？
Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave during combat. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在離開將會遺失所有未存檔的進度，因為在戰鬥中無法自動存檔。你確定要離開嗎？
Quitting now will lose any unsaved progress because you cannot autosave during special events, or shops. Do you wish to quit anyway?=ZH=現在離開將會遺失所有未存檔的進度，因為在特殊事件或商店中無法自動存檔。你確定要離開嗎？
You cannot save during a death snare! They are the ultimate challenge...=ZH=死亡陷阱進行中無法存檔！這是最終極的挑戰……

You cannot save during special events, or shops=ZH=特殊事件或商店中無法存檔
Each character has their own inventory and gold to manage. Gold and items will need to be pooled and traded between characters. Each character can hold up to 16 items at a time, less if Misc Game Differences is enabled.=ZH=每個角色都有自己的道具欄與金幣需要管理。金幣與物品需要在角色之間集中分配與交易。每個角色最多可攜帶 16 件物品，若啟用「雜項遊戲差異」則數量更少。
With Save At Guild Only enabled, saving the game will only be possible when the party are in an adventurers guild or camp. Autosave will only fire when entering a guild. With Misc Game Differences also enabled, BT3 will still allow saving anywhere, since the original game introduced a save game option.=ZH=啟用「僅在公會存檔」後，只有在冒險者公會或營地時才能儲存遊戲。自動存檔僅在進入公會時觸發。若同時啟用「各代遊戲差異」，BT3 仍允許在任何地方存檔，因為原版遊戲已提供存檔選項。
Enables differences between each game. These include:\n\n- SPECIAL SLOT for monsters only in BT1.\n\n- No archery in BT1.\n\n- No item charges in BT1, instead items have a small chance to disappear on each use.\n\n- 8 slot inventory in BT1 and 10 slots in BT2.\n\n- Wine Skins only available in BT3.\n\n- Rogue identifying items only available in BT3.\n\n- Items and Spells adjusted to match each game.=ZH=啟用各代遊戲之間的差異。包括：\n\n- BT1 中怪物專用的特殊欄位。\n\n- BT1 中無弓箭。\n\n- BT1 中物品無充能次數，改為每次使用有低機率消失。\n\n- BT1 道具欄為 8 格，BT2 為 10 格。\n\n- 酒囊僅在 BT3 中可用。\n\n- 盜賊鑑定物品僅在 BT3 中可用。\n\n- 物品與法術已調整以符合各代遊戲。
Enable the full XP requirements from the originals, and also some exploits that helped with levelling.\n\n- Up to twice the XP needed for each level.\n\n- Allows party attack to be initiated anywhere.\n\n- Enabled shop buy and sell any charged item.\n\n- All game events and battles are now redoable.=ZH=啟用原始版本完整的經驗值需求，以及一些有助於升級的漏洞。\n\n- 每個等級所需的經驗值最多增加兩倍。\n\n- 允許在任何地點發起隊伍攻擊。\n\n- 啟用商店買賣任何已充能的物品。\n\n- 所有遊戲事件與戰鬥現在皆可重複進行。
Grab some grid paper and map the levels yourself. This was a big part of the 1980s experience and why all the spinners and teleports in dungeons exist. For those that have the time and temperament, this can be a rewarding experience. If Misc Game Differences is also enabled, then the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> will still allow for a simplified journal, just showing map walls and doors, as the original game introduced an automap feature.=ZH=拿些方格紙，親手繪製地圖吧。這是1980年代體驗的重要一環，也是地下城中存在這麼多迷向器和傳送門的原因。對於有時間和耐心的玩家來說，這會是一段很有成就感的體驗。如果同時啟用「雜項遊戲差異」，那麼<b>《命運之賊》(BT3)</b>仍會提供簡化的日誌，僅顯示地圖牆壁和門，因為原始遊戲已引入了自動地圖功能。
Enabling this setting restores the way song magic used to work in the originals.\n\nSongs in <b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b> and <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b> revert to having their strength vary with dungeon difficulty. Songs in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> have their strength vary with level of the bard that is singing.=ZH=啟用此設定會恢復歌曲魔法在原始版本中的運作方式。\n\n在<b>《未知傳奇》(BT1)</b>和<b>《天命勇士》(BT2)</b>中，歌曲的強度會隨地下城難度而變化。在<b>《命運之賊》(BT3)</b>中，歌曲的強度則隨吟唱詩人的等級而變化。
On some platforms, such as the Commodore 64 (C64), it was possible to use items, that were not equipped, in combat. This has an especially large impact on bards, choosing to blow horns, or magic users in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> using harmonic gems. Enabling this option also removes the ability to equip or unequip items during combat.=ZH=在某些平台上，例如Commodore 64 (C64)，可以在戰鬥中使用未裝備的物品。這對選擇吹響號角的吟遊詩人，或在<b>《命運之賊》(BT3)</b>中使用和諧寶石的魔法使用者影響尤其巨大。啟用此選項也會移除在戰鬥中裝備或卸下物品的能力。
The remaster adds some hints and random gear rewards to be found by searching the empty houses in cities. Ticking this option will remove those and empty houses will just be for finding random groups of enemies to fight, or the occasional dungeon entrance. This makes gaining the first few character levels just a little harder.=ZH=重製版在城市中搜索空屋時，會加入一些提示和隨機裝備獎勵。勾選此選項將移除這些獎勵，空屋只會用來尋找隨機的敵人群戰鬥，或偶爾發現地下城入口。這會讓前幾個角色等級的升級過程稍微困難一些。

Your first three dungeons should be the sewers, the catacombs and the castle (in that order). Each is progressively tougher.=ZH=你最初的三個地下城應該是下水道、墓穴與城堡（依此順序）。每個都比前一個更難。
You can adjust the speed that messages scroll in combat in gameplay settings.=ZH=你可以在遊戲設定中調整戰鬥中訊息滾動的速度。
Avoid traps. Higher level rogues are excellent at opening chests, but when in doubt use the 'Trapzap' spell.=ZH=小心陷阱。高等級的盜賊非常擅長開啟寶箱，但若有疑慮，請使用「陷阱轟炸」法術。
Be sure that your party is fully healed before entering a new dungeon.=ZH=進入新地下城前，務必確保你的隊伍完全恢復生命值。
Remember that each succeeding level goes UP in a tower or castle and DOWN in a dungeon.=ZH=請記住，在塔或城堡中，每往上一層就是「向上」；在地下城中，每往下一層就是「向下」。
Good maps will show you the logical spots for secret doors and secret rooms.=ZH=好的地圖會顯示出秘密門與秘密房間的合理位置。
Use your spells and Bard songs to lower the armor class of your entire party. The lower the better.=ZH=使用你的法術與吟遊詩人之歌來降低整個隊伍的防禦等級。越低越好。
You can minimize damage by killing off all but one monster in either of the first two attacking groups.=ZH=你可以透過殺死前兩組攻擊怪物中其中一組的所有怪物，只留下一隻，來將傷害降到最低。
Treat the undead with respect. In other words, kill them quickly.=ZH=對待亡靈要心存敬畏。換句話說，快點殺了它們。
Be prepared to die a lot with level 1 and 2 characters. Especially at night. Especially when you don't have any weapons and are just walking to Garth's Shoppe.=ZH=做好準備，等級 1 和 2 的角色會經常死亡。尤其是在夜間。尤其是當你沒有任何武器，只是走去加斯的商店時。
Monsters are smart. They usually attack your most vulnerable characters first, unless one of your members represents a significant threat.=ZH=怪物很聰明。它們通常會先攻擊你最脆弱的角色，除非你的某個成員構成重大威脅。
Beware the Doppelganger monster. It enters your party and looks just like one of your members.=ZH=當心變身怪。它會混入你的隊伍，看起來就像你的其中一個成員。
Develop a Sorcerer fast. They are very useful in dungeons.=ZH=快點培養一名術士。他們在地下城中非常有用。
Don't venture too far into any dungeon without your maximum spell points. A good rule is to leave a dungeon when you are down to one quarter of your maximum spell points.=ZH=在法術點數未滿的情況下，不要深入任何地下城。一個好規則是：當你的法術點數降到最大值四分之一時，就該離開地下城。
Manage your spell points carefully. Don't waste a spell if you can accomplish the same thing without magic.=ZH=謹慎管理你的法術點數。如果能不用魔法就達成同樣效果，就不要浪費法術。
Locate traps, second sight and sorcerer sight spells all identify traps within 30 feet.=ZH=「偵測陷阱」、「第二視界」和「術士視界」法術都能識別 30 呎內的陷阱。
Cast a long lasting Bard spell right before entering a tavern. It's like getting a free spell.=ZH=進入酒館前先施放一個持續時間長的吟遊詩人法術，就像免費獲得一個法術一樣。
Generally, the more expensive the item is, the better it works. Almost like real life.=ZH=一般來說，物品越昂貴，效果越好。這幾乎跟現實生活一樣。
There are no cursed or bad items, unlike real life.=ZH=這裡沒有被詛咒或劣質的物品，跟現實生活不一樣。
Don't be stingy. Equip your fighters with the best armor, weapon, shield, helmet and gauntlets money can buy. After all, they can't take it with them.=ZH=別吝嗇。用金錢能買到最好的盔甲、武器、盾牌、頭盔和護手來裝備你的戰士。畢竟，他們死後也帶不走。
Experiment with all items you find to determine their capabilities. Magical items are key to success.=ZH=嘗試使用你找到的所有物品，以了解它們的能力。魔法物品是成功的關鍵。
The tavern on Rakhir Street is the only tavern that serves wine. And that wine "goes down" easily. Good luck!=ZH=拉基爾街上的酒館是唯一供應葡萄酒的酒館。而且那種葡萄酒很容易「下肚」。祝好運！
If your spell points aren't recharging fast enough, go see Roscoe. But be prepared for electrifyingly high fees.=ZH=如果你的法術點數恢復得不夠快，就去找羅斯柯。但要做好準備，他的收費高得嚇人。
Temples are divine places of resurrection and complete healing. In fact, this is the only place to cure characters who have been withered or drained of experience.=ZH=神廟是進行復活和完全治療的神聖場所。事實上，這是唯一能治癒衰老或經驗耗盡角色的地方。
Gold you receive in battles is pooled for the party and can be used to buy weapons, heal your wounds or regenerate your spell points.=ZH=戰鬥中獲得的金幣會歸入隊伍共有，可用來購買武器、治療傷口或恢復法術點數。
After battles you may find items which go into a pool for anyone to use. Sell or drop items if your inventory gets too full.=ZH=戰鬥後你可能會找到物品，這些物品會進入共用庫存，供任何人使用。如果你的道具欄太滿，就賣掉或丟棄物品。
Every mage should know where Roscoe's Energy Emporium is. He will regenerate your magic if the price is right.=ZH=每個法師都應該知道羅斯柯的能量商店在哪裡。只要價格合適，他就會幫你恢復魔力。
If you cannot kill off all the magic-using monsters, you may want to cast anti-magic spells to protect against illusions, possessions and other spells.=ZH=如果你無法殺光所有會使用魔法的怪物，你可能需要施放反魔法法術來抵禦幻象、附身和其他法術。
It might be a good idea to stay close to a Temple at night, so you can quickly heal any wounds.=ZH=晚上最好待在神廟附近，這樣可以快速治療任何傷口。
Wizards are extremely important in advanced levels; they can summon demons, extremely powerful specials.=ZH=巫師在高級階段極其重要；他們可以召喚惡魔，這是極其強大的特殊能力。
Items that appear in the inventory in red cannot be used by that character class.=ZH=道具欄中以紅色顯示的物品，該角色職業無法使用。
Don't be shy about using magic in combat. When in doubt, blast 'em with magic.=ZH=在戰鬥中不要吝於使用魔法。當你猶豫不決時，就用魔法轟炸他們。
Trapzap spell disarms all traps within 30 feet.=ZH=「陷阱解除」法術能解除 30 呎內的所有陷阱。
You may wish to drop by a tavern to wet the whistle of your Bard.=ZH=你可能想順道去酒館，讓你的吟遊詩人潤潤喉。
Deeper dungeons require a higher Hide-In-Shadows and Critical Strike skill=ZH=更深層的地下城需要更高的「陰影躲藏」和「暴擊」技能。
There's dungeons deep underneath Skara Brae... sewers, catacombs, the castle and more, each more dangerous than the one before.=ZH=斯卡拉布雷地下深處有地下城……下水道、地下墓穴、城堡等等，一個比一個危險。
Explore and map every square in every maze. There are 'Magic Mouths' that give hints. There are 1-of-a-kind magic items and spell regeneration zones. Good maps will show you the logical spots for secret doors and secret rooms, too.=ZH=探索並繪製每個迷宮中每一格的地圖。有些「魔法之口」會提供提示。還有獨一無二的魔法物品和法術恢復區域。好的地圖也會顯示出暗門和密室可能存在的合理位置。
Avoid traps. Higher level rogues are excellent at opening chests, but when in doubt use the 'Trapzap' spell.=ZH=避開陷阱。高等級的盜賊非常擅長開啟寶箱，但如果不確定，就使用「陷阱解除」法術。
You can use 'Trapzap' to disarm any traps you encounter.=ZH=你可以使用「陷阱解除」來解除你遇到的任何陷阱。
Be sure that your party is fully healed before entering a new dungeon.=ZH=在進入新的地下城之前，務必確保你的隊伍完全恢復。
When you are finding your bearings in a maze, remember that each succeeding level goes UP in a tower or castle and DOWN in a dungeon.=ZH=當你在迷宮中尋找方向時，請記住，在塔樓或城堡中，每下一層是往上走；在地下城中，每下一層是往下走。
Your highest dexterity character will attack earliest in combat. Target particularly nasty monsters with spells and attacks from high dexterity characters first. Your less dexterous characters may not survive long enough to get in a first strike.=ZH=你敏捷度最高的角色會在戰鬥中最先攻擊。優先讓高敏捷度的角色用法術和攻擊鎖定特別難纏的怪物。你的低敏捷度角色可能撐不到發動先手攻擊的時候。
Use your spells and Bard songs to lower the armor class of your entire party. The lower the better.=ZH=使用你的法術和吟遊詩人之歌來降低整個隊伍的防禦等級。越低越好。
If you are attacked by more than 2 groups of monsters, concentrate on magic users first. If you cannot kill off all the magic-using monsters, you may want to cast anti-magic spells to protect against illusions, possessions and other spells.=ZH=如果你被超過兩組怪物攻擊，優先集中攻擊會使用魔法的敵人。如果你無法殺光所有會使用魔法的怪物，你可能需要施放反魔法法術來抵禦幻象、附身和其他法術。
You can minimize damage by killing off monsters in melee range first. Non-magic users, further than 10' away, will need to advance before they can attack.=ZH=你可以先消滅近戰範圍內的怪物，以將傷害降到最低。距離超過 10 呎的非魔法使用者，需要前進才能發動攻擊。
Many of the undead monsters are capable of draining experience levels, turning characters old and gray or even turning characters to stone. Treat the undead with respect. In other words, kill them quickly.=ZH=許多亡靈怪物能夠吸取經驗等級，讓角色變得衰老灰白，甚至將角色石化。請對待亡靈保持敬意。換句話說，快點幹掉牠們。
Be prepared to die a lot with level 1 and 2 characters. Especially at night. Especially when you don't have any weapons and are just walking to Garth's Shoppe. It might even be a good idea to stay close to a Temple at night, so you can quickly heal any wounds.=ZH=做好準備，1級和2級的角色會經常陣亡。尤其是在夜間。尤其是當你沒有任何武器，只是步行前往 Garth 的商店時。夜間最好待在神廟附近，這樣就能快速治療任何傷勢。
Monsters are smart. They usually attack your most vulnerable characters first, unless one of your members represents a significant threat.=ZH=怪物很聰明。牠們通常會先攻擊你最脆弱的角色，除非你的其中一名成員構成重大威脅。
Beware the Doppelganger monster. It enters your party and looks just like one of your members.=ZH=小心變身怪。牠會潛入你的隊伍，看起來就跟你的其中一名成員一模一樣。
Develop a Sorcerer fast. They are very useful in dungeons. Wizards are extremely important in advanced levels; they can summon demons, extremely powerful specials. Develop an Archmage, too. You'll need one; you'll want more.=ZH=盡快培養一名術士。他們在地下城中非常有用。巫師在高級階段極其重要；他們可以召喚惡魔，這些是極其強大的特殊能力。也培養一名大法師吧。你會需要一個；而且你會想要更多。
Don't venture too far into any dungeon without your maximum spell points. A good rule is to leave a dungeon when you are down to one quarter of your maximum spell points.=ZH=在法術點數未達最大值時，不要冒險深入任何地下城太遠。一個好規則是：當你的法術點數降到最大值四分之一時，就該離開地下城。
Manage your spell points carefully. Don't waste a spell if you can accomplish the same thing without magic. Torches, for example, often work as well as magic light spells. On the other hand, don't be shy about using magic in combat. When in doubt, blast 'em with magic.=ZH=謹慎管理你的法術點數。如果不用魔法也能達成同樣效果，就不要浪費法術。例如，火把通常和魔法照明法術一樣好用。另一方面，在戰鬥中也不要吝於使用魔法。如果猶豫不決，就用魔法轟炸他們。
Locate traps, second sight and sorcerer sight spells all identify traps within 30 feet. Trapzap spell disarms all traps within 30 feet.=ZH=「偵測陷阱」、「真知術」和「術士之眼」法術都能在 30 呎內辨識陷阱。「陷阱解除」法術能解除 30 呎內的所有陷阱。
Cast a long lasting Bard spell right before entering a tavern. It's like getting a free spell.=ZH=在進入酒館前，先施放一個持續時間長的吟遊詩人法術。這就像免費獲得一個法術一樣。
Generally, the more expensive the item is, the better it works.=ZH=一般來說，物品越昂貴，效果就越好。
There are no cursed or bad items.=ZH=這裡沒有詛咒或不良的物品。
Don't be stingy. Equip your fighters with the best armor, weapon, shield, helmet and gauntlets money can buy. After all, they can't take it with them.=ZH=別吝嗇。用金錢能買到的最好盔甲、武器、盾牌、頭盔和護手來裝備你的戰士。畢竟，他們死後也帶不走。
Experiment with all items you find to determine their capabilities. Magical items are key to success. Remember that an item may only be usable for certain characters and classes, so trade items around if you have to.=ZH=嘗試使用你找到的所有物品，以確定它們的能力。魔法物品是成功的關鍵。請記住，物品可能只對特定角色和職業可用，所以必要時請互相交換物品。
Seek out the Review Board. You need to visit there to be awarded new levels and spells.=ZH=尋找仲裁委員會。你需要去那裡才能獲得新的等級和法術。
By the way, the tavern on Rakhir Street is the only tavern that serves wine. And that wine "goes down" easily. You may wish to drop by to wet the whistle of your Bard. Good luck!=ZH=順便一提，拉基爾街上的那間酒館是唯一供應葡萄酒的酒館。而且那種酒「很容易下肚」。你可能想順道去那裡，讓你的吟遊詩人潤潤喉。祝好運！
If your spell points aren't recharging fast enough, go see Roscoe. But be prepared for electrifyingly high fees.=ZH=如果你的法術點數恢復得不夠快，就去找羅斯柯。但要做好準備，他會收取高得嚇人的費用。
Temples are divine places of resurrection and complete healing. In fact, this is the only place to cure characters who have been withered or turned to stone.=ZH=神廟是進行復活和完全治療的神聖場所。事實上，這裡是唯一能治癒衰老或石化角色的地方。
Gold you receive in battles is pooled for the party and can be used to buy weapons, heal your wounds or regenerate your spell points.=ZH=你在戰鬥中獲得的金幣會匯入隊伍的共用資金，可以用來購買武器、治療傷口或恢復法術點數。
After battles you may find items which go into a pool for anyone to use. Sell or drop items if your party inventory gets too full.=ZH=戰鬥後你可能會找到物品，這些物品會進入一個任何人都能使用的共用庫存。如果你的隊伍庫存太滿，就賣掉或丟棄物品。
Every mage should know where Roscoe's Energy Emporium is. He will regenerate your magic if the price is right.=ZH=每個法師都應該知道羅斯柯的能量商店在哪裡。只要價格合適，他就會幫你恢復魔力。
The temples can heal your wounds, cure your illnesses and even bring back the dead for the right donation.=ZH=神廟可以治療你的傷口、治癒你的疾病，甚至只要捐獻足夠的香油錢，就能讓死者復活。
Deeper dungeons require a higher hide-in-shadows skill. Keep upgrading your skill to 100 and beyond or you will be discovered more often.=ZH=更深層的地下城需要更高的「藏身陰影」技能。持續將你的技能提升到 100 或更高，否則你將更常被發現。
Search all the buildings in all the towns. There is a dungeon to be found in every town, starting with Tangramayne.=ZH=搜索所有城鎮中的所有建築物。從坦格拉梅恩開始，每個城鎮都有一個地下城可以探索。
The forests of the Realm hide a variety of interesting buildings, huts and strongholds (as well as a host of evildoers). Crypts can also be found in the wilderness and are worth investigating if you can gain access.=ZH=王國的森林中隱藏著各種有趣的建築物、小屋和據點（以及一大群邪惡之徒）。在荒野中也能找到墓穴，如果你能設法進入，這些地方值得調查。
Be sure to tip the bartender. He has valuable information to give you. Try giving him more money when you can afford it.=ZH=記得給酒保小費。他有寶貴的情報可以提供給你。當你負擔得起時，試著給他更多錢。
Searching the room, you discover some parchment from the Adventurer's Guild Handbook. It reads:\n\n=ZH=搜索房間時，你發現了一些來自冒險者公會手冊的羊皮紙。上面寫著：\n\n

Mangar protects himself using a riddle with 7 answers, and you found them all! Your skills are without limit. You will surely be able to rid us of this evil soon!=ZH=曼加爾用一個有七個答案的謎語保護自己，而你找到了所有答案！你的技藝無可限量。你一定能很快為我們除去這個邪惡！
You found the Mad God and defeated him?! That's amazing. Our faith in you was not wasted!=ZH=你找到了瘋狂之神並擊敗了他？！太驚人了。我們對你的信任沒有白費！
I see you've found an entrance into the catacombs. Your progress is impressive!=ZH=我看見你找到了一個進入地下墓穴的入口。你的進展令人印象深刻！
What is this? The statue turned into a Samurai and attacked you? This must be Mangar's evil magic. There are guardian statues throughout the town and some are undoubtedly even tougher to defeat!=ZH=這是怎麼回事？雕像變成了一個武士並攻擊了你？這一定是曼加爾的邪惡魔法。城鎮各處都有守護雕像，有些無疑更難擊敗！
An evil wizard Mangar is threatening the town. He's brought down a blizzard upon us and all manner of evil creatures are wandering about.\n\nTake a party out into town, explore everywhere for clues. You MUST find a way to stop him!=ZH=邪惡巫師曼加爾正威脅著這座城鎮。他給我們帶來了暴風雪，各種邪惡生物四處遊蕩。\n\n帶一支隊伍到鎮上去，四處探索尋找線索。你必須找到阻止他的方法！

Seek out Brilhasti ap Tarj beneath the Mad God's Temple!=ZH=前往瘋狂之神神殿下方，尋找 Brilhasti ap Tarj！
The old man in the Review Board scratches his head. "Yes, you are the prophesied ones, but you have come too early. No matter."=ZH=仲裁委員會的老人搔了搔頭。「沒錯，你們就是預言中的人，但你們來得太早了。不過沒關係。」
"Beneath Skara Brae you will find one of Tarjan's devotees. Brilhasti ap Tarj is a foul Necromancer, and his life impedes my efforts to stave off disaster."=ZH=「在 Skara Brae 地下，你們會找到 Tarjan 的一名信徒。Brilhasti ap Tarj 是個邪惡的死靈法師，他的存在阻礙了我阻止災難的努力。」
"You may enter the Catacombs under the Mad God's Temple by uttering his master's name... 'Tarjan'"=ZH=「只要說出他主人的名字……『Tarjan』，你們就能進入瘋狂之神神殿下的墓穴。」

"Go to Arboria and retrieve for me Valarian's Bow and The Arrows of Life. The Chronomancer spell, Apport Arboria, cast at the Twilight Copse will get you there. The spell, Reditus Arboria, cast at the point where you arrived, will return you to Caith."=ZH=「前往 Arboria，為我取回瓦拉里安之弓與生命之箭。在暮光林地施展時空術士法術『傳送 Arboria』即可抵達該處。在你抵達的地點施展『回歸 Arboria』法術，則會將你送回 Caith。」
"Welcome ye children of the prophecy. Upon your shoulders falls a great weight, for you must embark on what will be your greatest adventure ever."=ZH=「歡迎你們，預言的子民。一副重擔將落在你們肩上，因為你們必須踏上此生最偉大的冒險。」
"That which has laid waste to Skara Brae is an ancient evil recently released. It threatens to destroy all reality and time as it has wrought havoc on Skara Brae."=ZH=「那對斯卡拉布雷造成浩劫的，是最近釋放出的遠古邪惡。它已在斯卡拉布雷肆虐，並威脅要毀滅所有的現實與時間。」
"If you cannot stop it, it will consume the universe. If you do stop it, you will be rewarded beyond all your dreams."=ZH=「如果你們無法阻止它，它將吞噬整個宇宙。如果你們成功阻止了它，你們將獲得超越一切夢想的獎賞。」
"Prepare thyselves, and hasten to the place of trees, for it is most like the first dimension you must sojourn in to blunt the evil."=ZH=「準備好你們自己，趕往樹木之地，因為那裡最像你們必須首先停留以削弱邪惡的第一個次元。」
"Arboria, the home of Valarian the bold, is reached through using powerful magic that only a Chronomancer can control."=ZH=「阿博利亞，勇敢者瓦拉利安的家鄉，必須透過只有時空術士才能操控的強大魔法才能抵達。」
"Bring to me Valarian's Bow and The Arrows of Life if Valarian will not return here with you."=ZH=「如果瓦拉利安不願隨你們回來，就把瓦拉利安之弓和生命之箭帶回來給我。」
"Yes, and be on the lookout for an ally, for you are not the first I have sent on this quest. Though your paths are different, they may cross, and you will do well together."=ZH=「是的，並留意尋找一位盟友，因為你們並非我派去執行此任務的第一批人。雖然你們的道路不同，但可能會交會，而你們聯手將會大有可為。」
"Use Apport Gelidia at Cold Peak to travel to Gelidia and retrieve for me the Wand of Power and the Sphere of Lanatir."=ZH=「在冷峰使用『阿波特·格利迪亞』前往格利迪亞，為我取回力量之杖與拉納提爾之球。」
The old man takes the news of Valarian's death hard, but he summons a smile to his lips and breathes deeply.=ZH=老人聽聞瓦拉利安的死訊後顯得難以承受，但他強擠出一抹微笑掛在唇邊，深深吸了一口氣。
"You have done well, children, and I take well the news that Hawkslayer has survived this long. Now you are bound for a place far distant."=ZH=「你們做得很好，孩子們，而『弒鷹者』能存活至今的消息，我也欣然接受。現在，你們將前往一個遙遠的地方。」
"It is the dimension of magic. It is known as Gelidia and if your Chronomancer is able, I will share the spells to get you there and back again."=ZH=「那是魔法的次元，被稱為格利迪亞。如果你們的時空術士有能力，我將分享讓你們往返那裡的咒語。」
"I shall teach there Apport Gelidia and Reditus Gelidia. Seek out Cold Peak to the north west, as they will only work there."=ZH=「我將在那裡傳授『阿波特·格利迪亞』與『雷迪圖斯·格利迪亞』。前往西北方的冷峰，因為這些法術只在那裡有效。」
"Bring Lanatir with you, or, if you cannot convince him to come, coax from him the Wand of Power and the Sphere of Lanatir."=ZH=「帶著拉納提爾一起來，或者，如果你們無法說服他前來，就從他那裡誘出力量之杖與拉納提爾之球。」
Seek out Alliria in Lucencia. The Crystal Spring in the east offers passage to Lucencia using Apport Lucencia. Recover the Crown of Truth and the Belt of Alliria!"=ZH=「在盧森西亞尋找阿利里亞。東方的水晶泉提供使用『阿波特·盧森西亞』前往盧森西亞的通道。取回真理之冠與阿利里亞之腰帶！」
The old man appears visibly shaken by the news of Lanatir's death. After a long silence he stares off into space and mumbles, "Is it to be that way then?"=ZH=老人聽到拉納提爾的死訊後，明顯地動搖了。經過長時間的沉默，他凝視著遠方，喃喃自語：「事情非得如此嗎？」
His eyes focus upon you again, and a grim look washes over his features. "Now you are bound for Lucencia."=ZH=他的目光再次聚焦在你們身上，一抹嚴峻的神色掠過他的面容。「現在，你們將前往盧森西亞。」
"To this end, I will teach thee Apport Lucencia and Reditus Lucencia which you must use at the Crystal Spring to the north east."=ZH=「為此，我將教導你們『阿波特·盧森西亞』與『雷迪圖斯·盧森西亞』，你們必須在東北方的水晶泉使用它們。」
"I dare not hope Alliria lives, so I want you to recover the Crown of Truth and the Belt of Alliria. Beware, for she had a jealous consort, and he will protect her as best he can."=ZH=「我不敢奢望阿利里亞還活著，所以我希望你們取回真理之冠與阿利里亞之腰帶。小心，因為她有一位善妒的伴侶，他會盡其所能地保護她。」
"Get thee to Kinestia and recover The Hammer of Wrath and Ferofist's Helm. Your path begins in the Dwarven Mines to the south west!"=ZH=「前往基內斯蒂亞，取回憤怒之錘與費羅菲斯特之頭盔。你們的道路始於西南方的矮人礦坑！」
The old man's look of grim determination withstands news of Alliria's death, but the information saps some of his strength.=ZH=老人臉上堅毅決絕的神情承受住了阿利里亞死訊的打擊，但這個消息仍削弱了他的一些力量。
"Quickly then, my children, to distant Kinestia!"=ZH=「那麼，快去吧，我的孩子們，前往遙遠的基內斯蒂亞！」
"Learn now, Apport Kinestia and Reditus Kinestia, which will take you to Ferofist in Kinestia if cast from the Dwarven Mines in the south west."=ZH=「現在學會『阿波特·基內斯蒂亞』與『雷迪圖斯·基內斯蒂亞』，若在西南方的矮人礦坑施放，它們將帶你們前往基內斯蒂亞的費羅菲斯特那裡。」

"Hurry, the pace quickens, and the outlook is horrible if you fail!"=ZH=「快點，節奏正在加快，如果你們失敗，前景將不堪設想！」
"Get to Tenebrosia! Bring me the Helm of Justice and Sceadu's Cloak. Seek the Shadow Rock!"=ZH=「前往泰尼布羅西亞！為我帶來正義之盔與斯卡杜之斗篷。尋找暗影之岩！」
The old man's eyes narrow as you tell of Ferofist's end. "An alliance with the dark one, did he say? It is well he realized his folly in the end."=ZH=當你們訴說費羅菲斯特的結局時，老人瞇起了眼睛。「與黑暗者結盟，他是這麼說的嗎？他最終能認清自己的愚行，這很好。」
He pauses a second, and you see utter weariness send a tremor through him. "Now to Tenebrosia for all of you."=ZH=他停頓了一秒，你們看見極度的疲憊讓他渾身一顫。「現在，你們所有人都要去泰尼布羅西亞。」
I endow thee with the spells to travel there and return: Apport Tenebrosia and Reditus Tenebrosia. The spell must be cast at Shadow Rock, which can be seen towering in the east."=ZH=「我授予你們往返該地的咒語：『阿波特·泰尼布羅西亞』與『雷迪圖斯·泰尼布羅西亞』。此咒語必須在暗影之岩施放，那塊岩石高聳於東方，清晰可見。」
"Remember, in the land where night is day and day is night, nothing is as you know it to be. Paradox lives there - trust no one but yourselves."=ZH=「記住，在那個夜晚是白天、白天是夜晚的土地上，一切都與你們所知的不同。矛盾存在於那裡——除了你們自己，誰也別相信。」

"No!" the old man cries out, "not Sceadu as well. The dark one is utterly mad. Still," the old man smiles, "he burns the chaff while I sow the seed."=ZH=「不！」老人喊道，「連 Sceadu 也……黑暗者徹底瘋了。不過，」老人微微一笑，「他焚燒穀殼，而我播下種子。」
"Off with you to Tarmitia, the land of unceasing warfare. A myriad of ages meld together there, and your success depends upon your ability to adapt."=ZH=「去 Tarmitia 吧，那片永無止境的征戰之地。無數紀元在那裡交織融合，你的成功取決於你適應變化的能力。」

His eyes lose their focus. "It is almost done, the circle is almost joined."=ZH=他的眼神失去焦距。「就快完成了，圓環即將閉合。」
The old man lies dead. His last request still plagues your memories. You must endure stench to get to Malefia and help Hawkslayer to defeat the Mad God Tarjan before he destroys all of reality.=ZH=老人倒地身亡。他最後的請求仍縈繞在你記憶中。你們必須忍受惡臭前往 Malefia，幫助 Hawkslayer 擊敗瘋狂之神 Tarjan，以免他毀滅一切現實。
"I know," the old man gurgles, "Werra lies dead."\n\nBlood bubbles up on his lips and joins the dark stains on the rest of his clothing. "I, too, have been slain by the Mad One."=ZH=「我知道，」老人咕噥道，「Werra 已死。」\n\n血沫從他唇邊湧出，與衣物上深色的血漬融為一體。「我也被那瘋狂者殺害了。」
"Gather up the prizes you have won, the special ones, those I requested. Hawkslayer has already ventured into Malefia, the land of EVIL."=ZH=「收集你們贏得的獎賞——那些特別的、我指定過的物品。Hawkslayer 已經深入 Malefia，那片邪惡之地。」

"Get yourselves hence and help him. Destroy the Mad God Tarjan before he destroys all reality!!"=ZH=「動身前往，幫助他。在瘋狂之神 Tarjan 毀滅一切現實之前消滅他！！」

Dragon Song=ZH=龍之歌
Dragon, dragon,\nWhy do you lair?\nUnfurl your wings, Take to the air!\nSoar high above, Far away fly,\nOr is that where, You wish to die?=ZH=巨龍，巨龍，\n為何蟄伏巢中？\n展開雙翼，翱翔蒼穹！\n高飛遠颺，遠離此地，\n抑或此處，便是你葬身之地？
Hawkslayer, Hawkslayer,\nWhy do you ask?\nKilling me is a difficult task.\nMy claws are sharp, my fangs sharper yet,\nAnd my breath flames, let us not forget.=ZH=屠鷹者，屠鷹者，\n為何發問？\n殺我豈是易事一樁。\n利爪鋒銳，尖牙更甚，\莫忘我吐息烈焰焚天。
Dragon, dragon,\nThou art quite strong.\nYour scales are bright and talons are long,\nBut a duty I have and it is clear:\nWhatever it takes, I will drive you from here.=ZH=巨龍啊，巨龍，\n你確實強悍無比。\n鱗片閃耀，利爪長長，\n但我肩負的職責再清楚不過：\n無論付出何種代價，我都要將你驅離此地。
Hawkslayer, Hawkslayer,\nSuch a brief life.\nHave you no wish to again see your wife?\nImagine her tears and grief and despair,\nWalk away from this fight, you have not a prayer.=ZH=弒鷹者，弒鷹者，\n生命如此短暫。\n難道你不想再見到你的妻子？\n想想她的淚水、悲傷與絕望，\n從這場戰鬥中退開吧，你連一絲勝算都沒有。
Dragon, dragon,\nHow wise thou art.\nA massive beast with such a kind heart.\nI do wish to kiss my wife and my heir,\nPray, fly to the mountains and await me there.=ZH=巨龍啊，巨龍，\n你何其睿智。\n一頭龐大的野獸，卻有如此仁慈的心。\n我確實渴望親吻我的妻子與子嗣，\拜託，飛向群山，在那裡等我。
Hawkslayer, Hawkslayer,\nI thought you were brave.\nHere I await you, deep in this cave.\nCome, little man, and I'll eat your brain,\nThen ravage Lucencia again and again.=ZH=弒鷹者，弒鷹者，\n我以為你很勇敢。\n我在這洞穴深處等你。\n來吧，小個子，我會吃掉你的腦子，\n然後一次又一次蹂躪盧森西亞。

"Talk aint cheap" the barkeep says.\nHow much will you tip him?=ZH=「說話可不便宜，」酒保說道。\n你打算給他多少小費？

What kind of tip is that? I just accept gold, bud!=ZH=那是什麼建議？我只收金幣，老兄！
The barkeep says, "Go down to the cellar and pick out a bottle."=ZH=酒保說：「下到地窖去，挑一瓶上來。」
"The guardians can be deadly", the barkeep smirks.=ZH=「守衛可是會要人命的，」酒保得意地笑著說。
"A taste of wine might turn to ready adventure", the barkeep chuckles.=ZH=「嚐一口美酒，或許就會轉為一場現成的冒險，」酒保輕聲笑道。
"Look for the Review Board on Trumpet Street", the barkeep whispers,=ZH=「去喇叭街找仲裁委員會，」酒保低聲說道。
"The gates cannot be scaled, but an entrance always exists", the barkeep stutters.=ZH=「城門無法攀越，但總有入口存在，」酒保結結巴巴地說。
"The Stone Golem has been spoken of twofold", the barkeep smiles.=ZH=「石魔像的傳聞有兩個版本，」酒保微笑著說。
"The Spectre Snare can draw in even the mightiest", the barkeep grumbles.=ZH=「幽魂陷阱連最強大的傢伙都能吸進去，」酒保咕噥道。
"Seek knowledge from the sage," the barkeep smirks.=ZH=「去向賢者求取知識，」酒保得意地笑著說。
"The Scarlet Bard likes his ale... Some more coin might help me remember his favorite tavern..." the barkeep chuckles.=ZH=「猩紅吟遊詩人喜歡他的麥酒……多給點金幣或許能幫我想起他最愛去哪家酒館……」酒保輕聲笑道。
"The Sage charges 500 in gold for information on magical items," the barkeep whispers.=ZH=「賢者對魔法物品的情報收費五百金幣，」酒保低聲說道。
"Seek the Masterkey to pass the gates," the barkeep stutters. "The crypt lies in the wilderness," the barkeep whispers.=ZH=「去找萬能鑰匙來通過城門，」酒保結結巴巴地說。「墓穴就在荒野之中，」酒保低聲說道。
"The Scarlet Bard's is often hanging around the Greysword Inn in the south end of Ephesus." the barkeep informs you.=ZH=「猩紅吟遊詩人經常待在以弗所南端的灰劍旅店附近。」酒保告訴你。
"The Dreamspell can be learned from the rock," the barkeep grumbles.=ZH=「夢之法術可以從岩石中學到，」酒保咕噥道。
"Every city has one dungeon within it," the barkeep grumbles.=ZH=「每座城市裡都有一個地下城，」酒保咕噥道。
"Traecham solved his task by chilling; he said 'F.....'," the barkeep mutters.=ZH=「崔查姆用冷凍的方式解決了他的任務；他說『該死的……』，」酒保喃喃自語。
"Oscon was sorcerer to Count Waldec... until he turned coat and slew him. Rumour is he’s working with Lagoth Zanta," the barkeep grumbles.=ZH=「奧斯康曾是瓦爾德克伯爵的術士……直到他叛變並殺死了伯爵。謠傳他現在和拉戈斯·贊塔合作，」酒保咕噥道。
"There’s a tower in Philippi... it just appeared one day, out of nothing... Some say it comes from another realm, whatever the heck that means," the barkeep whispers.=ZH=「腓立比有座塔……有一天就那麼憑空冒了出來……有人說它來自另一個領域，管他那是什麼意思，」酒保低聲說道。
"There's a building in Skara Brae where some of the survivors stashed their goods. It may be helpful," smiles the bartender.=ZH=「斯卡拉布雷有棟建築，一些倖存者把他們的東西藏在那裡。或許會有幫助，」酒保微笑著說。
"Be on the lookout for the magic gems, your spellcasters will need them"=ZH=「留意那些魔法寶石，你的施法者會需要它們。」
"There lies another bar in Celaria Bree, seek it. It exists only in the dimension called Lucencia."=ZH=「在瑟拉利亞·布里還有另一間酒吧，去找它。它只存在於名為盧森西亞的次元中。」
"Seek out the old man in the Review Board. He keeps watch over everything."=ZH=「去仲裁委員會找那位老人。他監視著一切。」
"Go to the bard's hall and listen to the songs they sing. They contain useful information."=ZH=「前往吟遊詩人大廳，聆聽他們吟唱的歌曲。其中包含有用的資訊。」
"There is said to be a key existing in the Violet Mountains, this will gain access to Cyanis's Tower"=ZH=「據說在紫羅蘭山脈中存在一把鑰匙，它能讓你進入賽亞尼斯之塔。」

Show Combat Effects=ZH=顯示戰鬥特效
The game will pause and bring up the Pause Menu whenever the game window loses focus.=ZH=當遊戲視窗失去焦點時，遊戲會暫停並顯示暫停選單。
Mouse look in the 3d view is reversed on the Y axis.=ZH=在 3D 視野中，滑鼠的 Y 軸方向會反轉。
This will use an alternative layout where the 3d view is much larger, but the player info and message scroll are smaller and use smaller text.\nNote: This is NOT supported on 4:3 aspect.=ZH=這將使用另一種版面配置，讓 3D 視野變得更大，但玩家資訊與訊息捲動區域會變小，並使用較小的文字。\n注意：此功能不支援 4:3 比例。
Combat actions that are not available will be transparent, rather than being removed. This way the options do not move on screen between characters and are easier to click on.=ZH=無法使用的戰鬥動作會以半透明顯示，而非直接移除。如此一來，不同角色之間的選項位置不會在畫面上移動，更容易點擊。
For AZERTY keyboard users, you can now navigate the game using ESDF keys, instead of the usual WASD keys.=ZH=對於使用 AZERTY 鍵盤的使用者，現在可以使用 ESDF 按鍵來操作遊戲，取代原本的 WASD 按鍵。
Do a visual effect whenever a monster or ally is attacked in combat. You may prefer to turn this off if you want a more passive experience.=ZH=每當戰鬥中怪物或盟友受到攻擊時，顯示視覺特效。如果您偏好較被動的體驗，可以關閉此選項。
Place a separator between character inventory and party inventory. This is more intuitive, though it means the UI must rebuild when items are equipped and unequipped.=ZH=在角色道具欄與隊伍道具欄之間放置分隔線。這會更直觀，但代表裝備或卸下物品時，UI 必須重新建構。
Toggles Fullscreen. Fullscreen maximises the game window to fit the screen.=ZH=切換全螢幕。全螢幕會將遊戲視窗最大化以填滿螢幕。
Toggles SSAO. SSAO (Screen Space Ambient Occlusion) creates advanced lighting effects for each point and how exposed it is to ambient lighting. Turning SSAO off may improve performance.=ZH=切換SSAO。SSAO（螢幕空間環境光遮蔽）能為每個點及其受環境光照射的程度建立進階光影效果。關閉SSAO可提升效能。
Toggles Shadows. Cast shadows from sunlight or moonlight.=ZH=切換陰影。投射來自陽光或月光的陰影。
Toggles Bloom. Bloom produces edges of light extending from the borders of bright areas, making lights brighter than what they actually appear.=ZH=切換泛光效果。泛光效果會讓明亮區域的邊緣產生光暈，使光線比實際看起來更亮。
Toggles Grass. Grass is nature's carpet.=ZH=切雜草。雜草是大自然的地毯。
Adjusts in game brightness levels.=ZH=調整遊戲內的亮度等級。
Text scroll speed changes the speed that scroll text in combat gets presented and shifted up. Scroll speed can also be adjusted by its <l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","shortcut keys"/>.=ZH=文字捲動速度會改變戰鬥中文字捲動與上移的速度。捲動速度也可透過其<l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","shortcut keys"/>調整。
Minimap alpha controls the visibility of the minimap in the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>.=ZH=小地圖透明度控制<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>中小地圖的可見度。

Bard's Tale Trilogy Remaster Manual=ZH=冰城傳奇三部曲重製版手冊
About=ZH=關於
The Bard’s Tale was originally written as trilogy of games. The release of the 3 titles was between 1985-1988.\n\n<color=@#Heading#>\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b>\n\n<b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>\n\n<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>\n\n</color>\n\nThis remaster combines the three games into a single narrative. Some features from The Destiny Knight and Thief of Fate have been taken through all three games to provide a consistent gameplay experience across the 3 volumes.=ZH=《冰城傳奇》最初是以三部曲的形式撰寫而成。這三部作品於 1985 年至 1988 年間發行。\n\n<color=@#Heading#>\n\n<b>《未知傳奇》（BT1）</b>\n\n<b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b>\n\n<b>《命運之賊》（BT3）</b>\n\n</color>\n\n本次重製將三部遊戲合併為一個連貫的故事。為了在三部曲中提供一致的遊戲體驗，我們從《天命勇士》和《命運之賊》中選取了部分特色，並將其貫穿於所有三部遊戲之中。

3D Game View=ZH=3D 遊戲視角
<pos=15%><sprite=51/></pos><line-height=1500%>\n\n</line-height>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>3D View</b></color>\n\nLocated in the center, framed by big stone blocks, this is the window which shows the 3D world that the party is exploring. All character, non-player-character cameos and visual effects will appear in this window. The cursor can be used inside of this window to <l="MAN_MOVEMENT","move"/> the party.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Magic Detection Skull</b></color>\n\nLocated in the middle of the top of the stone frame is the <l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","skull"/>. It changes its appearance to reflect the magic it is sensing. It helps track important information which is blessing or cursing the party.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Gold & Inventory</b></color>\n\nGold and Inventory space are displayed along the bottom of the 3D window frame.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Text Plate</b></color>\n\nAt the bottom of the 3D view is a black banner that details a number of things. Such as what location the party is currently in, what person or object is the focus and names of enemies and group size the party is facing. This text is changing quite frequently.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Minimap</b></color>\n\nThe minimap is located in the bottom right corner of the 3D view (<l="MAN_ACTIONS","when active"/>). The minimap is a small map that assists navigation, like the one found in the journal. The orientation of the minimap and the player is subject to change based on the active spells and effects.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Left Ribbon Action Buttons</b></color>\n\nThis ribbon in the top left holds the buttons for the alternative panels -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - Opens the Menu.\n\n\n\n<sprite=11/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - Opens this Manual.\n\n\n\n<sprite=12/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - Opens the Cloth Map.\n\n\n\n<sprite=13/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - Opens the Bestiary.\n\n\n\n<sprite=14/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - Opens Game Stats.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Right Ribbon Action Buttons</b></color>\n\nThis ribbon in the top right holds the buttons for actions -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=9/> - More Options.\n\n\n\n<sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#. \n\n\n\n<sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#.\n\n\n\n<sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#.=ZH=<pos=15%><sprite=51/></pos><line-height=1500%>\n\n</line-height>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>3D 視野</b></color>\n\n位於畫面中央，由大石塊框住，這是顯示隊伍正在探索的 3D 世界的視窗。所有角色、非玩家角色客串以及視覺效果都會出現在此視窗中。滑鼠游標可在這個視窗內使用，以 <l="MAN_MOVEMENT","move"/> 隊伍。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>魔法偵測骷髏</b></color>\n\n位於石框頂部中央的是 <l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","skull"/>。它會根據偵測到的魔法改變外觀。它有助於追蹤對隊伍有益或有害的重要資訊。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>金幣與庫存</b></color>\n\n金幣與庫存空間顯示在 3D 視窗框架的底部。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>文字面板</b></color>\n\n在 3D 視野的底部有一條黑色橫幅，詳細說明了許多事項。例如隊伍目前所在的位置、焦點人物或物體，以及隊伍面對的敵人名稱和群體大小。這段文字會相當頻繁地變更。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>小地圖</b></color>\n\n小地圖位於 3D 視野的右下角（<l="MAN_ACTIONS","when active"/>）。小地圖是一張協助導航的小型地圖，類似於日記中的地圖。小地圖和玩家的方位會根據當前啟用的法術和效果而改變。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>左側功能列動作按鈕</b></color>\n\n左上角的這個功能列包含了用於開啟其他面板的按鈕 -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - 開啟選單。\n\n<sprite=11/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - 開啟此手冊。\n\n<sprite=12/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - 開啟布質地圖。\n\n<sprite=13/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - 開啟怪物圖鑑。\n\n<sprite=14/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - 開啟遊戲統計。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>右側功能列動作按鈕</b></color>\n\n右上角的這個功能列包含了用於動作的按鈕 -<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=9/> - 更多選項。\n\n<sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#。\n\n<sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#。\n\n<sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#。\n\n<sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#。\n\n<sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#。\n\n<sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#。\n\n<sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#。\n\n<sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#。\n\n<sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#。\n\n<sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#。
Scroll=ZH=卷軸
<pos=50%><sprite=34/></pos><line-height=1250%>\n\n</line-height>This scroll contains essentially all of the changing text within the game, where majority of the in game information is read and most in game actions are decided. Information such as - Character information, Inventory and Items management, Combat text, Choices and Commands, Spell casting and even a text field for typing.\n\n\n\nAll of the options presented in the scroll can be clicked with mouse or the appropriate <color=@#InteractionCommand#>key</color> pressed on a keyboard.\n\n\n\n@#MOUSE_SCROLL_REVIEW#=ZH=<pos=50%><sprite=34/></pos><line-height=1250%>\n\n</line-height>此卷軸基本上包含了遊戲中所有會變動的文字，遊戲中的大部分資訊都在此閱讀，且大多數遊戲內動作都在此決定。例如：角色資訊、庫存與物品管理、戰鬥文字、選擇與指令、施法，甚至還有一個用於輸入的文字欄位。\n\n卷軸中呈現的所有選項都可以用滑鼠點擊，或在鍵盤上按下對應的 <color=@#InteractionCommand#>按鍵</color>。\n\n@#MOUSE_SCROLL_REVIEW#
Party Status=ZH=隊伍狀態
The party status contains all of the information about the party, this includes -\n\nParty slots and party slot numbers, all party members and their names, party member <l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS",@#AC#/>, <l="MAN_HIT_POINTS",@#HP#/>, <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS",@#SP#/>, <l="MAN_CLASS",@#class#/>, <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","conditions"/> and any active magical effect.\n\n<pos=15%><sprite=35/></pos></pos><pos=23%><sprite=37/></pos><pos=33%><sprite=38/></pos><pos=44%><sprite=39/></pos><pos=53%><sprite=40/></pos><pos=62%><sprite=42/></pos>\n\n<line-height=200%>\n\n</line-height><pos=40f%>@#AC#</pos><pos=55.5f%><color=#141414>@#HP#</color></pos><pos=78f%>@#SP#</pos><pos=95.5f%>@#CL#</pos>\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Brian light icons represent if the party has a light source to guide their way. Light icons can be two different shades. Lighter for light that <u>can not</u> detect secret doors, darker for light that <u>can</u> detect secret doors.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Levitation</b></color><pos=0%><sprite=45/></pos><line-height=125%>\n\n</line-height><margin-left=10fem>The levitation icon represents if the party has any form levitation in effect.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Sight</b></color><pos=0%><sprite=46/></pos><line-height=125%>\n\n</line-height><margin-left=10fem>The sight icon represents if an effect is active, assisting the parties navigation.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Shield</b></color><pos=0%><sprite=47/></pos><line-height=125%>\n\n</line-height><margin-left=10fem>The shield icon represents if the party has an effect that is improving their defensive capabilities.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Compass</b></color><pos=0%><sprite=48/></pos><pos=4f%><sprite=49/></pos><line-height=125%>\n\n</line-height><margin-left=10fem>The compass icons represent if the party has a bearing on direction. A compass with a highlighted arrow shows which world space direction the party is facing. A rotating compass with a question mark means the party has lost their bearings.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Bard party.</margin>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Tooltip</b></color>\n\nHovering the mouse cursor over any magical effect icon display a tooltip that details the source and the time remaining on the
