Settings=ZH=設定
Multiple adjustments can be made via the settings panel in the pause menu.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b><line-height=140%>\n\n</line-height><l="MAN_GRAPHICS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_AUDIO"/>\n\n<l="MAN_GAMEPLAY"/>=ZH=可透過暫停選單中的設定面板進行多項調整。<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b><line-height=140%>\n\n</line-height><l="MAN_GRAPHICS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_AUDIO"/>\n\n<l="MAN_GAMEPLAY"/>
Audio=ZH=音訊
Audio Settings.\n\n\n\n<sprite=57/> Adjust the volume of all music in the game.\n\n\n\n\n\n<sprite=58/> Adjust the volume of all sound effects (SFX) in the game.=ZH=音訊設定。\n\n\n\n<sprite=57/> 調整遊戲中所有音樂的音量。\n\n\n\n\n\n<sprite=58/> 調整遊戲中所有音效（SFX）的音量。
Controls=ZH=操作
The Bard's Tale Trilogy presents choices throughout the game. Choices are <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>highlighted</color></b> in yellow and defines what key to press to select that choice.\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=25/></pos>\n\n<margin=6.25fem>The cursor can also be used to click any of the same options and buttons throughout The Bard's Tale Trilogy.</margin>=ZH=《冰城傳奇三部曲》在遊戲過程中會提供各種選擇。選擇會以 <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>黃色高亮</color></b> 標示，並定義按下哪個按鍵來選取該選項。\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=25/></pos>\n\n<margin=6.25fem>你也可以使用游標點擊《冰城傳奇三部曲》中任何相同的選項和按鈕。</margin>
Movement=ZH=移動
<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Keyboard</b></color>\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>W</color></b> - move forward.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b> - turn to face the opposite direction.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>A</color></b> - turn left.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>D</color></b> - turn right.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Mouse</b></color>\n\nWhen the pointer is placed within the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>, it turns into a directional arrow. As the cursor is moved, the arrow points out the various directions of can travel. Ahead, around corners, into buildings, through doors. Click to go in the direction currently indicated by the arrow.\n\n\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=59/></pos>\n\n<pos=31%><sprite=60/></pos><pos=48%><sprite=61/></pos>\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=62/></pos><line-height=200%>\n\n</line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Up-Arrow</color></b> - move forward.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Down-Arrow</color></b> - turn around 180 degrees.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Left-Arrow</color></b> - turn left 90 degrees.\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Right-Arrow</color></b> - turn right 90 degrees.\n\nThe left mouse button can also be held down to continuously move.=ZH=<color=@#SubHeading#><b>鍵盤</b></color>\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>W</color></b> - 向前移動。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b> - 轉向面對相反方向。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>A</color></b> - 向左轉。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>D</color></b> - 向右轉。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>滑鼠</b></color>\n\n當指標置於 <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/> 內時，會變成方向箭頭。移動游標時，箭頭會指向可前進的各個方向：前方、轉角、進入建築物、穿過門。點擊即可朝箭頭當前指示的方向前進。\n\n\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=59/></pos>\n\n<pos=31%><sprite=60/></pos><pos=48%><sprite=61/></pos>\n\n<pos=40%><sprite=62/></pos><line-height=200%>\n\n</line-height><b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>上方向鍵</color></b> - 向前移動。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>下方向鍵</color></b> - 向後轉180度。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>左方向鍵</color></b> - 向左轉90度。\n\n<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>右方向鍵</color></b> - 向右轉90度。\n\n按住滑鼠左鍵也可持續移動。
Actions=ZH=動作
Clicking a character name plate in <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> or pressing (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) opens character information in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - Opens the Menu.\n\n\n\n<sprite=11/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - Opens this Manual.\n\n\n\n<sprite=12/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - Opens the Cloth Map.\n\n\n\n<sprite=13/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - Opens the Bestiary.\n\n\n\n<sprite=14/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - Opens Game Stats.\n\n\n\n<sprite=9/> - More Options.<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>When peacefully exploring streets, cities or navigating through dungeons the more options button can be pressed to use the following buttons -</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Learn the name of the location the party is in, and the time of day. If within a city this will also tell the street name.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Casts a spell from a selected mage. This can also be done by pressing a magic users party slot number (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) key, or click on a magic user's name in the party status and press (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b>) - to open the spell list.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Sing a Bard's tune.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#. \n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Use an equipped item.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Ascend <u>up</u> through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Descend <u>down</u> through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Assault another member of the party, including special members.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Set a new party order from (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>1</color></b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>). This can also be done by dragging and dropping character names in the party status.</size></indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Toggles the minimap in the 3d view.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Opens the journal.</indent>=ZH=在 <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> 中點擊角色名牌，或按下 (<b>XTAGX2<b>3XTAGX</b> - <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>7</color></b>) 可在 <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/> 中開啟角色資訊。<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=10/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F10</color></b> - 開啟選單。\n\n\n\n<sprite=11/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F1</color></b> - 開啟此手冊。\n\n\n\n<sprite=12/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F2</color></b> - 開啟布質地圖。\n\n\n\n<sprite=13/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F3</color></b> - 開啟怪物圖鑑。\n\n\n\n<sprite=14/>  <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>F4</color></b> - 開啟遊戲統計。\n\n\n\n<sprite=9/> - 更多選項。<line-height=130%>\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>在和平探索街道、城市或穿越地下城時，可按下更多選項按鈕來使用以下按鈕：</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=15/> @#BUTTON_WhereAmI#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>了解隊伍所在位置的名稱及當前時間。若在城市中，還會告知街道名稱。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=16/> @#BUTTON_CastSpell#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>從選定的法師施展法術。也可按下魔法使用者的隊伍欄位編號 (<b>XTAGX52<b>53XTAGX</b> - <b>XTAGX56</color>57XTAGX</b>) 鍵，或點擊隊伍狀態中魔法使用者的名字並按下 (<b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>S</color></b>) 來開啟法術列表。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=17/> @#BUTTON_PlaySong#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>吟唱吟遊詩人的曲調。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=18/> @#BUTTON_UseItem#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>使用已裝備的物品。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=19/> @#BUTTON_PortalUp#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>通過傳送門 <u>向上</u> 移動。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=20/> @#BUTTON_PortalDown#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>通過傳送門 <u>向下</u> 移動。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=21/> @#BUTTON_PartyAttack#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>攻擊隊伍中的另一名成員，包括特殊成員。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=22/> @#BUTTON_PartyOrder#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>設定新的隊伍順序 (<b>XTAGX98<b>99XTAGX</b> - <b>XTAGX102</color>103XTAGX</b>)。也可透過在隊伍狀態中拖放角色名稱來完成。</size></indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=23/> @#BUTTON_ToggleMinimap#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>切換3D視野中的小地圖。</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=24/> @#BUTTON_ToggleJournal#。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>開啟日誌。</indent>
Combat=ZH=戰鬥
During battle, the actions and options characters can take appear in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Advance ahead#</color> - Moves the party closer to the nearest group of enemies.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Fight bravely#</color> - Engages in combat.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Run away#</color> - Attempts to try and safely escape combat without having to engage.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Attack#</color> - Physically assaults foes any of the first 4 groups of monsters within 10'.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Defend#</color> - Do not interact this round - reduces chance of being hit.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Bard song#</color> - Plays a bard song to affect the party or groups of foes.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Hide in the shadows#</color> - Try to avoid combat by hiding. If successful, will not be a target of attack in the upcoming combat round.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Cast a spell#</color> - Casts a spell at enemies or the party.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Use an equipped item#</color> - Use an item or currently equipped item.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Ranged attack#</color> - Shoot or throw a projectile from a distance.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#Party attack#</color> - Assault another member of the party, including special members. \n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#EQUIP_OR_UNEQUIP#</color> - The party-member will equip or unequip a single item.\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#KICK_DEAD_MONSTERS#</color> - Free up character slots during combat by removing all existing dead monsters from the party.\n\n\n\n<sprite=26/> - increase message scrolling rate.\n\n<sprite=27/> - decrease message scrolling rate.=ZH=戰鬥期間，角色可採取的動作與選項會顯示在 <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/> 中。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#向前推進#</color> - 讓隊伍靠近最近的敵人群組。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#勇敢戰鬥#</color> - 進入戰鬥。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#逃跑#</color> - 嘗試安全逃離戰鬥，無需交戰。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#攻擊#</color> - 對10呎內前4組怪物進行物理攻擊。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#防禦#</color> - 此回合不行動，降低被擊中機率。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#吟遊詩人之歌#</color> - 演奏吟遊詩人之歌以影響隊伍或敵人群組。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#躲入陰影#</color> - 嘗試躲藏以避開戰鬥。若成功，在接下來的戰鬥回合中不會成為攻擊目標。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#施展法術#</color> - 對敵人或隊伍施展法術。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#使用已裝備物品#</color> - 使用物品或當前裝備的物品。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#遠程攻擊#</color> - 從遠處射擊或投擲投射物。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#攻擊隊友#</color> - 攻擊隊伍中的另一名成員，包括特殊成員。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#EQUIP_OR_UNEQUIP#</color> - 隊伍成員將裝備或卸下單一物品。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#>@#KICK_DEAD_MONSTERS#</color> - 在戰鬥中移除所有已死亡的怪物，以釋放角色欄位。\n\n\n\n<sprite=26/> - 增加訊息捲動速度。\n\n<sprite=27/> - 減少訊息捲動速度。
Character=ZH=角色
Characters can be created at the <b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b> in the\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b>, <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>, and in the <b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b> in the <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>.=ZH=角色可在 <b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b> 處創建，位於\n<b>《未知傳奇》（BT1）</b>、<b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b>，以及在 <b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b> 處創建，位於 <b>《命運之賊》（BT3）</b>。
Race=ZH=種族
Humans are not the only race in the world of The Bard's Tale. There is no single best race, each has its own strengths. Some are more magically inclined, some are stronger and some are smarter. There are 7 races:\n\n<b><l="MAN_HUMAN"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DWARF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HOBBIT"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFORC"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_GNOME"/></b>=ZH=人類並非《冰城傳奇》世界中唯一的種族。沒有哪個種族絕對最強，每個種族都有其優勢。有些種族天生擅長魔法，有些更強壯，有些則更聰明。共有 7 個種族：\n\n<b><l="MAN_HUMAN"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DWARF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HOBBIT"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFELF"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HALFORC"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_GNOME"/></b>
Human=ZH=人類
While possibly being of hardier stock, this character is nonetheless like you or me. The people of this race are known for their inventiveness, hardiness, aggressiveness, and downright stubbornness in the face of adversity.\n\n\n\nHuman capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>=ZH=儘管體格可能較為強健，但這個角色終究與你我無異。這個種族的人民以他們的創造力、堅韌、好鬥，以及在逆境中頑固不屈而聞名。\n\n人類可勝任的職業——\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>
Elf=ZH=精靈
These fair-haired, fair-skinned people closely resemble humans but are frequently taller, faster, and more agile due to their slim builds. The Elven people are highly skilled in the arts, especially magic and war.\n\n\n\nElf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=這些金髮白膚的種族與人類極為相似，但由於體型纖細，他們通常更高、更快、更敏捷。精靈族在藝術方面技藝高超，尤其擅長魔法與戰爭。\n\n精靈可勝任的職業——\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n無法勝任的職業——\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Dwarf=ZH=矮人
The Dwarven people are short and stout, extremely strong and healthy, but not amazingly intelligent. What they lack in intelligence, they more than make up for in their fighting abilities.\n\n\n\nDwarf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>=ZH=矮人族身材矮小粗壯，極其強壯健康，但智力並非特別出眾。他們在智力上的不足，完全由戰鬥能力所彌補。\n\n矮人可勝任的職業——\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n\n無法勝任的職業——\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>
Hobbit=ZH=哈比人
Hobbits are slightly smaller than Dwarves but are wise, nimble, dexterous and usually very patient. It is said, if need be, a hobbit can steal the gold-purse from a shadow. Just the right makeup for a rogue.\n\n\n\nHobbit capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=哈比人比矮人略為矮小，但他們睿智、靈巧、敏捷，而且通常非常有耐心。據說，如有必要，哈比人能從影子裡偷走錢袋。這正是成為盜賊的絕佳特質。\n\n\n哈比人可勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\n無法勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Half-Elf=ZH=半精靈
These people are living proof of the compatibility between humans and elves. They are fair-haired and light-skinned like elves, but gain some bulk and strength through their human ancestry.\n\n\n\nHalf-Elf capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>=ZH=這些人是人類與精靈能夠共存的活生生證明。他們像精靈一樣有著淺色頭髮和淺色皮膚，但透過人類的血統獲得了些許體格與力量。\n\n\n半精靈可勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\n無法勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>
Half-Orc=ZH=半獸人
Orcs are large, semi-intelligent, pig-like creatures that can usually be found working for evil wizards. Half-orcs, being half human, aren't quite as despicable as full orcs but you wouldn't want to date one.\n\n\n\nHalf-Orc capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>=ZH=獸人體型龐大、半具智慧、長得像豬的生物，通常會為邪惡巫師效力。半獸人因為有一半人類血統，不至於像純種獸人那樣卑鄙，但你絕對不會想跟他們約會。\n\n\n半獸人可勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\n無法勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>
Gnome=ZH=地侏
These people resemble dwarves, but usually have less hair, shorter tempers, and are always better at using magic.\n\n\n\nGnome capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\nNot capable classes -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>=ZH=這些種族長得像矮人，但通常毛髮較少、脾氣更暴躁，而且總是更擅長使用魔法。\n\n\n地侏可勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR"/>\n\n<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MONK"/>\n\n<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>\n\n\n\n無法勝任職業 -\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN"/>\n\n<l="MAN_BARD"/>
Class=ZH=職業
Classes represent a characters profession. They determine what gear they can were and use and what, if any, magic they can wield.=ZH=職業代表角色的專業。它們決定角色能穿戴與使用哪些裝備，以及能施展何種魔法（若有的話）。
Fighters=ZH=戰鬥者
Fighters are classes best suited to physical combat.=ZH=戰鬥者是專精於物理戰鬥的職業。
Warrior=ZH=戰士
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nWarriors are fighters. They can use nearly every weapon there is. For every 4 levels of experience after the first, warriors get an <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","extra attack"/> capability in combat.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>\n\n戰士就是戰鬥者。他們幾乎能使用所有武器。每當經驗等級超過第一級後，每提升四級，戰士就會在戰鬥中獲得一次<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","extra attack"/>能力。\n\n\n可晉升為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Paladin=ZH=聖騎士
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nPaladins are fighters who have sworn to fight all evil and uphold honor and purity in all places and situations. Paladins can use most weapons including many that other fighters can't. Paladins get <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/> at higher levels and also have greater resistance to evil magic.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>\n\n聖騎士是發誓對抗一切邪惡，並在任何地方與情況下維護榮譽與純潔的戰士。聖騎士能使用大多數武器，包括許多其他戰士無法使用的。聖騎士在高等級時會獲得<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>，並且對邪惡魔法有更強的抵抗力。\n\n\n\n能夠成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Rogue=ZH=盜賊
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nRogues are professional thieves with mediocre combat skills. However, rogues are experts at skulking in the shadows, as well as searching for <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/> and disarming them. Without a rogue in the ranks of the party, booty will come at an unusually high price.\n\n\n\nWhen rogues successfully slip into the shadows to avoid combat, they advance 10 feet. If they are already cloaked in the shadows, they can continue to advance further and further. An attack from the shadows have a good chance of getting a <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/> because they are striking the monster from behind.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>\n\n盜賊是戰鬥技巧平庸的專業竊賊。然而，盜賊是潛行陰影以及搜尋<l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>並解除它們的專家。冒險隊伍中若沒有盜賊，戰利品將付出異常高昂的代價。\n\n\n\n當盜賊成功潛入陰影以避免戰鬥時，他們會前進10英尺。如果他們已隱蔽在陰影中，則可以繼續前進得更遠。從陰影發動的攻擊有很高機率造成<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/>，因為他們是從怪物背後發動襲擊。
Bard=ZH=吟遊詩人
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color><sprite=4/>\n\n<width=65%>The bard is a wandering minstrel. He is always seen with a tankard of ale in front of him in even the less reputable of taverns. The rowdier the better.\n\n\n\nMost bards are warriors who have given up the art of war for the art of <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","magical music"/>. This doesn't mean that they have forgotten how to handle warrior weapons.</width>\n\nBut because they're not true warriors, bards don't get the advantage of extra attacks during combat. Their magic is so unique, it is almost impossible to survive without one.\n\n\n\nAny true bard has a variety of tunes on their lips, though to play them, they must have an instrument equipped and a healthy voice. When the going gets tough, the bard goes drinking.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color><sprite=4/>\n\n<width=65%>吟遊詩人是個流浪的樂師。即使在最不入流的酒館裡，也總能看到他面前擺著一杯麥芽酒。越喧鬧越好。\n\n\n\n大多數吟遊詩人是放棄了戰爭藝術，轉而投入<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","magical music"/>藝術的戰士。這並不代表他們忘記了如何使用戰士的武器。</width>\n\n但因為他們並非真正的戰士，吟遊詩人在戰鬥中無法獲得額外攻擊的優勢。他們的魔法如此獨特，以至於隊伍中幾乎不可能沒有吟遊詩人而存活。\n\n\n\n任何真正的吟遊詩人都會哼唱各種曲調，但要演奏它們，必須裝備樂器並擁有健康的嗓音。當情況變得艱難時，吟遊詩人就會去喝酒。\n\n\n\n能夠成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Hunter=ZH=獵人
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nThe Hunter is an assassin, a mercenary, but also a ninja. The hunter can use most weapons, and has the ability to do <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hits"/> in combat. As the hunter levels up so does the skill that increases critical hit chance.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>\n\n獵人是刺客、傭兵，同時也是忍者。獵人能使用大多數武器，並擁有在戰鬥中造成<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hits"/>的能力。隨著獵人升級，增加暴擊機率的技能也會提升。\n\n\n\n能夠成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Monk=ZH=武僧
<color=@#Fighter#>Fighter</color>\n\nThe monk is a martial artist. An almost inhuman fighting machine trained to fight without weapons or armor. The monk can use them, but, at higher levels particularly, often does better without. Monks do well in combat because of their ability to attack <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple times"/>.\n\n\n\nCapable of becoming a <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>.=ZH=<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>\n\n武僧是名武術家。一個幾乎非人的戰鬥機器，受訓成不靠武器或護甲戰鬥。武僧可以使用它們，但在高等級時，尤其是不使用時表現更佳。武僧在戰鬥中表現出色，因為他們有能力進行<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple times"/>攻擊。\n\n\n\n能夠成為<l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/>。
Mages=ZH=法師
Mages are the types of classes that are best suited for magic based combat by casting <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>.\n\n\n\nCasting spells uses <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/> and spells require different amounts spell points. Magic users qualify to learn spells in the next <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/> when they are at an eligible <l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>. It takes <l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/> to actually learn the new spells. Once a Magic User leaves a mage class, they can never return to it. Make sure to master each class before changing, to get full effectiveness out of a mage.\n\n\n\n<b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b> starts with the classes - <l="MAN_CONJURER"/>, <l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>, <l="MAN_SORCERER"/>, <l="MAN_WIZARD"/>.\n\n\n\n<b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b> introduces the - <l="MAN_ARCHMAGE"/> class.\n\n\n\n<b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b> adds the - <l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER"/> and <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/> classes.=ZH=法師是適合以施放<l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>進行魔法戰鬥的職業類型。\n\n施放法術會消耗<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>，且不同法術需要不同數量的法術點數。法師在達到符合資格的<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>時，便有資格學習下一個<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/>中的法術。實際學習新法術需要花費<l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/>。一旦法師離開某個法師職業，就永遠無法再回到該職業。請務必在轉職前精通每個職業，才能充分發揮法師的效能。\n\n<b>《未知傳奇》（BT1）</b> 起始職業為：<l="MAN_CONJURER"/>、<l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/>、<l="MAN_SORCERER"/>、<l="MAN_WIZARD"/>。\n\n<b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b> 引入了 <l="MAN_ARCHMAGE"/> 職業。\n\n<b>《命運之賊》（BT3）</b> 新增了 <l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER"/> 和 <l="MAN_GEOMANCER"/> 職業。
Conjurer=ZH=咒術師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nConjuring deals with healing the wounded, instantaneous creation of physical phenomena and effects, through the channeling of a mage's power. Conjurer spells are potent, but not unlimited in effect, since the exercise of creation takes a large amount of energy to produce even a moderate effect. A conjurer can also produce effects that distort non-solid things to produce new effects, like the distortion of space and time required to teleport human bodies to a new location.\n\n\n\n<sprite=0/>=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color>（<l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>）\n\n咒術涉及透過引導法師的力量來治療傷者、即時創造物理現象與效果。咒術師的法術威力強大，但效果並非無限，因為即使是創造中等效果也需要耗費大量能量。咒術師也能扭曲非實體事物以產生新效果，例如扭曲時空以將人體傳送至新地點。\n\n<sprite=0/>
Magician=ZH=幻術師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_MAGICIAN_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nMagic, the art practiced by a Magician, deals with bestowing magical effects on common objects. The items do not become magical, but radiate fields of power for the duration of a spell. The gist of Magic is that it can increase an item's capability, give it a totally new capability, or change its form to something totally different.\n\n\n\n<pos=2.5f%><sprite=1/></pos>=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color>（<l="MAN_MAGICIAN_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>）\n\n魔法，即幻術師所實踐的技藝，涉及將魔法效果賦予普通物品。這些物品並不會變成魔法物品，而是在法術持續期間散發出力量場域。魔法的精髓在於它能提升物品的能力、賦予其全新的能力，或將其形態改變成完全不同的東西。\n\n<pos=2.5f%><sprite=1/></pos>
Sorcerer=ZH=術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_SORCERER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nSorcery does not deal with a physical, manifestation, but rather the creation and manipulation of illusion and tapping into a heightened sense of awareness.\n\n<pos=70%><sprite=2/></pos>\n\n<width=67.5f%>An illusion is an image envisioned by the spell caster, which they then lay over the retinas of all who watch, augmenting this with appropriate stimulation of nerve endings to fool the other four senses. The human mind, believing an illusionary creation to be real, can be hurt or even killed by it, unless it is somehow disbelieved.<width=65%> A sorcerer, with the aid of their art, can also perceive certain special things hidden from view.</width>\n\n\n\nDue to the power of Sorcerer spells, this class is not available to newly created characters. To become a Sorcerer, the mage must first have achieved spell level 3 in one of the other magical arts.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color>（<l="MAN_SORCERER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>）\n\n術法並不涉及物理顯現，而是創造與操控幻象，並挖掘出更高層次的感知能力。\n\n<pos=70%><sprite=2/></pos>\n\n<width=67.5f%>幻象是施法者想像出的影像，他們將其投射在所有觀看者的視網膜上，並輔以適當的神經末梢刺激，以欺騙其他四種感官。人類的心靈若相信幻象創造物為真，便可能因此受傷甚至死亡，除非能以某種方式識破它。<width=65%>術士在其技藝的輔助下，也能感知某些隱藏於視線之外的特殊事物。</width>\n\n由於術士法術的強大威力，此職業不開放給新創建的角色。要成為術士，法師必須先在另一門魔法技藝中達到法術等級 3。
Wizard=ZH=巫師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_WIZARD_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nWizardry deals with the summoning and binding of various supernatural creatures. These creatures are not friendly to humankind and trying to control them is extremely hazardous.\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=3/></pos>\n\n<margin-left=25fem>The wizard has fewer spells than the other three classes, but theirs are by far the most potent. The creatures they can summon come from another plane of existence, called the negative plane. The summoned monsters will join party, and will fight until defeated. The wizard has some ability to trap and control normal monsters as well.</margin>\n\n\n\nDue to the power of Wizard spells, this class is not available to newly created characters. To become a Wizard, the mage must first have achieved spell level 3 in two other magical arts.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color>（<l="MAN_WIZARD_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>）\n\n巫術涉及召喚與束縛各種超自然生物。這些生物對人類並不友善，試圖控制它們極度危險。\n\n<pos=1%><sprite=3/></pos>\n\n<margin-left=25fem>巫師擁有的法術數量少於其他三個職業，但其法術威力遠勝於其他。他們能召喚的怪物來自另一個存在位面，稱為負能量位面。召喚出的怪物會加入隊伍，並戰鬥至被擊敗為止。巫師也具備一定能力來陷阱與控制普通怪物。</margin>\n\n由於巫師法術的強大威力，此職業不開放給新創建的角色。要成為巫師，法師必須先在另外兩門魔法技藝中達到法術等級 3。
Archmage=ZH=大法師
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_ARCHMAGE_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nArchmage's are masters of magic. They are the most powerful form of mage because an archmage can do almost anything. These masters of magic have risen through at least three <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/> for each of the previous four magic using classes - <b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>, <b><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/></b>, <b><l="MAN_SORCERER"/></b> and a <b><l="MAN_WIZARD"/></b>.\n\n\n\nArchmages possess the knowledge to cast all of the spells learnt from their previous classes.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color> (<l="MAN_ARCHMAGE_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\n大法師是魔法的宗師。他們是最強大的法師型態，因為大法師幾乎無所不能。這些魔法宗師至少在前四個魔法職業中，每個職業都達到了<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/>以上的等級——分別是<b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>、<b><l="MAN_MAGICIAN"/></b>、<b><l="MAN_SORCERER"/></b>，以及<b><l="MAN_WIZARD"/></b>。\n\n大法師擁有施展先前所有職業習得法術的知識。
Chronomancer=ZH=時空術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nChronomancers are the key to distant lands. Chronomancers are the travel experts of magic users. They help the party move from dimension to dimension with their special teleport spells. They also have a number of vicious offensive spells. Before becoming a chronomancer, a magic user must be an archmage and have a spell level of 3 or higher.\n\n\n\nWhen a mage becomes a chronomancer, they lose the power to use all the spells they had previously learnt. It may sound unfair, but the chronomancer needs to focus all of their attention on the important dimension teleport spells.\n\n\n\nIn <b>The Thief of Fate</b> a Chronomancer is <uppercase>necessary</uppercase> to get around.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color> (<l="MAN_CHRONOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\n時空術士是通往遙遠國度的關鍵。時空術士是魔法使用者中的旅行專家。他們憑藉特殊的傳送法術，幫助隊伍在次元之間移動。他們也擁有一些強大的攻擊法術。在成為時空術士之前，魔法使用者必須先是大法師，且法術等級達到3級或以上。\n\n當法師成為時空術士時，他們會失去所有先前習得法術的使用能力。這聽起來可能不公平，但時空術士需要將所有注意力集中在重要的次元傳送法術上。\n\n在<b>《命運之賊》</b>中，時空術士是<uppercase>必要</uppercase>的，才能四處移動。
Geomancer=ZH=大地術士
<color=@#MagicUser#>Magic User</color> (<l="MAN_GEOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\nThe most physically adept of all the spell casters, Geomancers can handle most weapons that fighters can. Their combative nature shows in their repertoire of predominantly offensive spells. Geomancers are fighters who want to convert to a life of magic. Once they choose to change, they can still use the armor, weapons, and magic items that the fighter classes use. Unfortunately, when a fighter becomes a geomancer, they lose some class specific traits.\n\n\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/> and <l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS","armor class"/> bonus.\n\n<l="MAN_BARD","Bards"/> lose their <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","songs"/>.\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/> lose their <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/> ability.\n\n\n\nIt is a part of the trade off. To become a geomancer, a special location must be found. It is the only place the rite of passage to become a Geomancer can be performed.=ZH=<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color> (<l="MAN_GEOMANCER_SPELLS","@#MAN_SPELLS#"/>)\n\n在所有施法者中體能最為出色的，大地術士能夠使用大多數戰士能用的武器。他們好戰的本性體現在以攻擊法術為主的法術庫中。大地術士是想要轉向魔法生活的戰士。一旦他們選擇轉變，仍然可以使用戰士職業能用的護甲、武器和魔法物品。不幸的是，當戰士成為大地術士時，他們會失去一些職業特有的特性。\n\n<l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/> 失去他們的 <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>。\n\n<l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/> 失去他們的 <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/>。\n\n<l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/> 失去他們的 <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","multiple attacks"/> 和 <l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS","armor class"/> 加成。\n\n<l="MAN_BARD","Bards"/> 失去他們的 <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","songs"/>。\n\n<l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/> 失去他們的 <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","critical hit"/> 能力。\n\n這是取捨的一部分。要成為大地術士，必須找到一個特殊地點。那是唯一能進行成為大地術士入門儀式的地方。
Attributes=ZH=屬性
Each character has five basic attributes which define their physical and mental prowess.\n\n<b><l="MAN_STRENGTH"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_INTELLIGENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DEXTERITY"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_CONSTITUTION"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_LUCK"/></b>\n\n\n\nEvery character also has other <b><l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/></b>.\n\n\n\nWhen a character is created, each attribute is randomly assigned a value from a <l="MAN_RACE","races'"/> base <l="MAN_BASE_RACIAL_ATTRIBUTES","racial attribute"/> value, to a maximum of 18. Higher numbers reflect higher capability. These stats can be re-rolled before confirming and applying these to the character.=ZH=每個角色都有五項基本屬性，定義了他們的身體與心智能力。\n\n<b><l="MAN_STRENGTH"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_INTELLIGENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_DEXTERITY"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_CONSTITUTION"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_LUCK"/></b>\n\n每個角色還有其他<b><l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/></b>。\n\n當角色創建時，每項屬性會從<l="MAN_RACE","races'"/>基礎<l="MAN_BASE_RACIAL_ATTRIBUTES","racial attribute"/>值開始隨機分配，最高可達18。數值越高代表能力越強。在確認並套用這些數值到角色之前，可以重新擲骰。
Base Racial Attributes=ZH=種族基礎屬性
Each <l="MAN_RACE","race"/> has base value <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>. These values act as a minimum value for an attribute roll and will never be less than the base value. Consider making characters races which have high base attributes that best benefit their <l="MAN_CLASS","class"/>.\n\n\n\n<size=125%><color=@#Heading#><align="center"><b>Base Strength=ZH=力量
Strength is physical power and chiefly affects the amount of damage a character can do to an opponent in <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","melee combat"/>. Make sure <color=@#Fighter#>fighters</color> are strong.=ZH=力量代表物理強度，主要影響角色在<l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","melee combat"/>中對敵人造成的傷害量。請確保<color=@#Fighter#>戰士</color>擁有足夠的力量。
Intelligence=ZH=智力
Intelligence is mental power. A high intelligence rating will enable <color=@#MagicUser#>magic users</color> to get bonus <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>.=ZH=智力代表心智力量。高智力值能讓<color=@#MagicUser#>法師</color>獲得額外的<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>。
Dexterity=ZH=敏捷
Dexterity measures agility and nimbleness. A high Dexterity makes characters harder to hit and helps them strike the first blow in <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>.=ZH=敏捷衡量靈巧與反應速度。高敏捷值能讓角色更難被擊中，並有助於他們在<l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>中搶先發動攻擊。
Constitution=ZH=體質
Constitution measures healthiness and survivability. It takes more damage to kill a character with a high constitution. Constitution is reflected with bonus <b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/></b>.=ZH=體質衡量健康與生存能力。體質高的角色需要承受更多傷害才會死亡。體質會反映在額外的<b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/></b>上。
Luck=ZH=幸運
Luck is ambiguous, unpredictable, and has a number of unforeseen effects on characters' lives. Lucky characters are more likely to resist evil magic and avoid <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/> and <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>.=ZH=幸運難以捉摸、無法預測，並對角色的命運有許多難以預見的影響。幸運值高的角色更容易抵抗邪惡魔法，並避開<l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>與<l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","chest traps"/>。
Statistics=ZH=數值
Each character also has statistics dependant on <b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b>.\n\n<b><l="MAN_LEVEL"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS"/></b>=ZH=每個角色還擁有取決於<b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b>的數值。\n\n<b><l="MAN_LEVEL"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_HIT_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE"/></b>\n\n<b><l="MAN_ARMOR_CLASS"/></b>
Level=ZH=等級
Level is a general measure of achievement within a character's class.\n\n\n\nThe <b><l="MAN_REVIEW_BOARD","Review board"/></b> will promote a character to higher levels based on their <b><l="MAN_EXPERIENCE","experience"/></b>, but only upon the character's in-person request. Advancement in levels is very important because it generally means an increase in <b><l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/></b>.=ZH=等級是衡量角色在其職業中成就的通用標準。\n\n公會長會根據角色的經驗值來晉升角色至更高等級，但必須由角色親自提出請求。等級的提升非常重要，因為這通常意味著生命值、法術點數和防禦等級的增加。
Experience (XP)=ZH=經驗值（XP）
Experience (XP) measures abilities gained by experience, the higher the number, the better the character. Characters are <l="MAN_CHESTS","awarded"/> XP for successful combat.\n\n\n\nXP is used to <b><l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/></b> characters up. When a character has enough XP to reach the next level, an arrow will appear on the left of a character's name in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>.\n\n\n\n<pos=40f%>@#AC#</pos><pos=55.5f%><color=#141414>@#HP#</color></pos><pos=78f%>@#SP#</pos><pos=95.5f%>@#CL#</pos>\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=40f%>-10</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><pos=95f%>@#WA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=40f%>-8</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><pos=95f%>@#PA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>3</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Markus#</pos><pos=40f%>-4</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>176/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>198</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><pos=95f%>@#BA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>4</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Sir Grady#</pos><pos=40f%>-5</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>178/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>215</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><pos=95f%>@#RO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=40f%>-1</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=95f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2%><sprite=63/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Omar#</pos><pos=40.5f%>2</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=95f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>7</pos>=ZH=經驗值（XP）衡量透過經驗獲得的能力，數值越高，角色越強。角色在成功戰鬥後會獲得經驗值。\n\n經驗值用於提升角色等級。當角色擁有足夠的經驗值達到下一級時，角色名稱左側會出現一個箭頭。\n\n1. Brian the Fist -10 108/258 WA\n2. El Cid -8 215/226 PA\n3. Markus -4 176/198 BA\n4. Sir Grady -5 178/215 RO\n5. Merlin -1 145/145 409/512 CO\n6. Omar 2 163/163 367/571 MA\n7.
Armor Class (AC)=ZH=防禦等級（AC）
Armor Class reflects the level of protection a character has from physical attacks. Armor class starts at 10, the lower the armor class the better.\n\n\n\nAs the character's protection improves through armor, spells, and other means, the armor class drops to a maximum of -50.=ZH=防禦等級反映角色對物理攻擊的防護程度。防禦等級從 10 開始，數值越低越好。\n\n隨著角色透過盔甲、法術和其他方式提升防護，防禦等級最多可降至 -50。
Hit Points (HP)=ZH=生命值（HP）
Hit Points (@#HP#) measure the damage a character can take before they are killed.\n\n\n\nThe @#HP# is represented by <color=#141414>(current health)/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>(maximum health)</color>\n\n<color=#141414>126/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color>\n\n\n\nIf a party members current @#HP# is 0 or less. They are <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","dead"/>.\n\n<color=#141414>0/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>dead</uppercase></color>\n\n\n\n@#HP# also shows any <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","condition"/> that the character has.\n\n\n\nCharacter's details in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> will cause the party slot line of the party member, to flash a color when something is affecting them.\n\n\n\nBeing hit or taking damage - flashes red.\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#A100003C>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#A100003C>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos></mark>\n\n\n\nRegenerating @#HP# or being healed - flashes yellow.\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#FFC91928>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>\n\n\n\nTaking damage from being <l="MAN_CHARACTER_CONDITIONS","poisoned"/> - flashes green.\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#00800064>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#00800064>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>\n\n\n\nDead party members can be revived at a <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/>, or by <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>.=ZH=生命值（HP）衡量角色在死亡前能承受的傷害。\n\nHP 以（目前生命值）/（最大生命值）表示，例如：126/258。\n\n如果隊伍成員的目前 HP 為 0 或更低，他們就會死亡，例如：0/0 dead。\n\nHP 也會顯示角色身上的任何狀態效果。\n\n角色詳情中的狀態會導致隊伍欄位在受到影響時閃爍顏色。\n\n受到攻擊或承受傷害——閃紅光。\n1.
