Omar 2 163/163 367/571 MA\n\n因中毒而承受傷害——閃綠光。\n2. El Cid -8 215/226 PA\n6. Omar 2 163/163 367/571 MA\n\n死亡的隊伍成員可以在神廟復活，或透過法術復活。
Spell Points (SP)=ZH=法術點數（SP）
Spell Points (@#SP#) are used to cast <l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>. @#SP# are used up with each cast of a magic spell. Spells have a required amount of @#SP# to be cast. If a magic users @#SP# do no meet the requirements of the spell, the <color=#991A1AFF>spell name will be red</color>, it will fizzle - and not be cast.\n\n\n\nSpell points are regenerated automatically when a magic user is in a city or out in a wilderness in the daytime. Spell points may also be regenerated in <l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/> or special <l="MAN_DUNGEON_REGENERATION","spell point regeneration"/> tiles in some <l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons"/>.\n\n\n\nThe @#SP# is represented by <color=#141414>(current spell points)/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>(maximum spell points)</color>\n\n<color=#141414>64/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>128</color>\n\n\n\nA character’s party slot will flash blue when spell points are regenerated.\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos></mark>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%><mark=#0069D22D>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos></mark>=ZH=法術點數（SP）用於施放法術。每次施放魔法法術都會消耗 SP。法術需要一定數量的 SP 才能施放。如果魔法使用者的 SP 未達到法術要求，法術名稱會顯示為紅色，法術會失效且無法施放。\n\n法術點數會在魔法使用者位於城市或白天身處野外時自動恢復。法術點數也可以在某些地下城中的旅館或特殊恢復格上恢復。\n\nSP 以（目前法術點數）/（最大法術點數）表示，例如：64/128。\n\n當法術點數恢復時，角色的隊伍欄位會閃藍光。\n5. Merlin -1 145/145 409/512 CO\n6. Omar 2 163/163 367/571 MA
Spell Level=ZH=法術等級
Spell level is the highest tier of magic spells a character can use in any given school of magic. There are 7 spell levels of magic for each <l="MAN_MAGES","mage class"/>. To be eligible to learn a new level of magic spells, a magic user must advance two <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>.<line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height><margin=15fem>Level<pos=42%>Spell Level</pos></margin>\n\n<margin=17em>1<pos=45.5%></pos>1\n\n2<pos=45.5%></pos>1\n\n3<pos=45.5%></pos>2<sprite=64/>\n\n4<pos=45.5%></pos>2\n\n5<pos=45.5%></pos>3<sprite=64/>\n\n6<pos=45.5%></pos>3\n\n7<pos=45.5%></pos>4<sprite=64/>\n\n8<pos=45.5%></pos>4\n\n9<pos=45.5%></pos>5<sprite=64/>\n\n10<pos=45.5%></pos>5 \n\n11<pos=45.5%></pos>6<sprite=64/>\n\n12<pos=45.5%></pos>6\n\n13+<pos=45.5%></pos>7<sprite=64/></margin><line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height>When a character is at a level where they are eligible to advance their spell level, a star will appear on the left of a character's name in the <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>.\n\n\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=40f%>-1</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=95f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Omar#</pos><pos=40.5f%>2</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=95f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n\n\nMages who have learnt at least 3 spell levels (<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/> 5+) in an art may elect to change to another mage class at any time. A mage changing class will have very basic knowledge of the class they are new to learning. However they retain all current <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>, <l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/> and all knowledge of spells they have practiced in all previous classes. \n\n\n\nOnce a magic user leaves a mage class, they can never return to it. Make sure to master each class before changing, to get full effectiveness out of a mage.=ZH=法術等級是角色在任何特定魔法學派中能使用的最高階法術層級。每個 <l="MAN_MAGES","mage class"/> 共有 7 個法術等級。要符合學習新法術等級的資格，魔法使用者必須提升兩級 <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>。<line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height><margin=15fem>等級<pos=42%>法術等級</pos></margin>\n\nXTAGX8<l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>9XTAGX</pos>1\n\n2<pos=45.5%></pos>1\n\n3<pos=45.5%>XTAGX14<line-height=120%>15XTAGX\n\n4<pos=45.5%></pos>2\n\n5<pos=45.5%>XTAGX19</line-height>20XTAGX\n\n6<pos=45.5%></pos>3\n\n7<pos=45.5%>XTAGX24<margin=15fem>25XTAGX\n\n8<pos=45.5%></pos>4\n\n9<pos=45.5%>XTAGX29<pos=42%>30XTAGX\n\n10<pos=45.5%></pos>5\n\n11<pos=45.5%>XTAGX34</pos>35XTAGX\n\n12<pos=45.5%></pos>6\n\n13+<pos=45.5%>XTAGX39</margin>40XTAGX</margin><line-height=120%>\n\n</line-height>當角色達到可提升法術等級的等級時，角色名稱左側會出現一顆星星，顯示於 <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>。\n\nXTAGX45<pos=42%>46XTAGX<pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=40f%>-1</pos><pos=53f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=95f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\nXTAGX68</pos>69XTAGX<pos=2%><sprite=64/></pos><pos=5.5f%>@#Omar#</pos>XTAGX75<line-height=120%>76XTAGX<pos=53f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=75f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=95f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n已學會至少 3 個法術等級（<l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/> 5+）的法師，可隨時選擇轉換為其他法師職業。轉換職業的法師對新職業僅有非常基礎的知識，但仍保留所有現有的 <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/>、<l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/>，以及先前所有職業中練習過的法術知識。\n\n一旦魔法使用者離開某個法師職業，便永遠無法回到該職業。請務必在轉換前精通每個職業，以充分發揮法師的效能。
Conditions=ZH=狀態
Conditions affect characters and monsters. Conditions hinder abilities in and out of battle. Conditions are inflicted by special attacks, spells, or <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>. Each condition has its own effect and usually lingers until they are cured at a <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> or by <l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/>. These conditions cover the maximum health text of a character on party roster, and are abbreviated to a code no longer than 5 letters. (<color=#141414>current health</color>)/(<color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>cond</uppercase></color>)\n\n\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Death</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DEAD</color>\n\nDead - not alive. This party member has had their health drop to 0. A dead party member can not act in any way. Resurrected by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Poison</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POIS</color>\n\nHit points are slowly drained. This includes while moving and after each round of combat. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Petrification</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STON</color>\n\nBeing turned to stone effectively kills the character twice - they will drop to the bottom of the roster, and after being un-petrified they will still need to be resurrected. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Insanity</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>NUTS</color>\n\nInsanity causes the loss of control of actions. Insane party members will perform random actions in and out of combat. It is common for the insane to attack other party members at any time. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Paralysis</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>PARA</color>\n\nImmobilized from performing actions, including combat. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Possession</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POSS</color>\n\nBeing possessed turns the party member against the party. In combat possessed party members will attack members within the party. When not in combat, they will initiate a party attack. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Drained</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DRAIN</color>\n\nThe character's level is drained, reducing their level by 1 and reducing their maximum @#HP#. Cured by - <u>Temple</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Withering</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>OLD</color>\n\nAll attributes are reduced to 1. Cured by - <u>Temple & Spells</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Stunned</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STUN</color>\n\nStunned party members miss a round in combat and perform no actions. Cured by - <u>Time</u>.\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Hidden in Shadows</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>HIDE</color>\n\nParty members hidden in shadows are cloaked and practically invisible, they can not be targeted for attack. Cancelled by - <u>Performing an attack</u>.=ZH=狀態會影響角色與怪物。狀態會妨礙戰鬥內外的能力。狀態由特殊攻擊、法術或 <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/> 造成。每種狀態都有其效果，通常會持續存在，直到在 <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> 或透過 <l="MAN_SPELLS","magic spells"/> 治癒。這些狀態會覆蓋隊伍名冊中角色的最大生命值文字，並以不超過 5 個字母的代碼縮寫表示。（<color=#141414>目前生命值</color>）/（<color=#F0F0F0FF><uppercase>狀態</uppercase></color>）\n\nXTAGX9<l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/>10XTAGX<pos=2.5f%>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos>\n\nXTAGX25<color=#F0F0F0FF>26XTAGX<pos=2.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n<smallcaps>死亡</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DEAD</color>\n\n死亡 - 不再存活。此隊伍成員的生命值降至 0。死亡的隊伍成員無法進行任何行動。復活方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>中毒</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POIS</color>\n\n生命值緩慢流失，包括移動時及每回合戰鬥後。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>石化</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STON</color>\n\n變成石頭實際上會讓角色死亡兩次——他們會掉到名冊底部，解除石化後仍需復活。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>瘋狂</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>NUTS</color>\n\n瘋狂會導致失去對行動的控制。瘋狂的隊伍成員會在戰鬥內外做出隨機行動。瘋狂者隨時可能攻擊其他隊伍成員。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>麻痺</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>PARA</color>\n\n無法進行任何行動，包括戰鬥。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>附身</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>POSS</color>\n\n被附身會使隊伍成員與隊伍為敵。戰鬥中，被附身的隊伍成員會攻擊隊伍內的成員。非戰鬥時，他們會發起隊伍攻擊。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>耗盡</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>DRAIN</color>\n\n角色的等級被吸取，降低 1 級並減少最大 @#HP#。治癒方式 - <u>神廟</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>衰老</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>OLD</color>\n\n所有屬性降至 1。治癒方式 - <u>神廟與法術</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>暈眩</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>STUN</color>\n\n暈眩的隊伍成員會在戰鬥中錯過一回合，無法進行任何行動。治癒方式 - <u>時間</u>。\n\n<smallcaps>隱身於暗影</smallcaps> - <color=#F0F0F0FF>HIDE</color>\n\n隱身於暗影的隊伍成員被遮蔽，幾乎隱形，無法成為攻擊目標。解除方式 - <u>發動攻擊</u>。
Spells=ZH=法術
Spells are used for a multitude of reasons, including - to damage, heal, summon, bolster, navigate, defend, explore and disarm. Spells are the primary way that <l="MAN_MAGES","mages"/> inflict damage in <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>. In order to advance in <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/>, the mage must be an appropriate <l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>.\n\n\n\nSpells can be searched for by a spell code, through filters, or navigating and selecting with the mouse.\n\n\n\nSpells have the ability to fizzle. Fizzling is when a spell is cast, the spell points are deducted from the mage, but the effect of the spell does not work. There are multiple reasons why a spell could fizzle.\n\n\n\nA spell consists of - a code, cost (<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>), target group, range, duration and effect.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>Code<pos=@#COL_COST#/>Cost<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>Target Group/Range<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>Duration</pos></color><line-height=130%>\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>@#CODE_ARFI#<pos=@#COL_COST#/>3 @#SP#<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>1 @#FOE# (10')<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>@#COMBAT#</pos></color>\n\n<b>@#SPELL_ArcFire#:</b> @#SPELL_ArcFire_DESC,1,4#=ZH=法術用於多種用途，包括：造成傷害、治療、召喚、強化、導航、防禦、探索與解除陷阱。法術是 <l="MAN_MAGES","mages"/> 在 <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/> 中造成傷害的主要方式。為了在 <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/> 中提升，法師必須達到適當的 <l="MAN_LEVEL","level"/>。\n\n法術可透過法術代碼、篩選條件，或使用滑鼠瀏覽與選取來搜尋。\n\n法術有可能失效。失效是指施放法術時，法術點數已從法師身上扣除，但法術效果並未發揮。法術失效的原因有多種。\n\n一個法術包含：代碼、消耗（<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>）、目標群組、範圍、持續時間與效果。\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>代碼<pos=@#COL_COST#/>消耗<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>目標群組/範圍<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>持續時間</pos></color><line-height=130%>\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#><pos=0%/>@#CODE_ARFI#<pos=@#COL_COST#/>3 @#SP#<pos=@#COL_RANGE#/>1 @#FOE# (10')<pos=@#COL_DURATION#/>@#COMBAT#</pos></color>\n\n<b>@#SPELL_ArcFire#:</b> @#SPELL_ArcFire_DESC,1,4#
Bard Songs=ZH=吟遊詩人之歌
The Bard makes magic by playing music. They need an equipped musical instrument, of course. They can play in combat or during exploration, with different <l="MAN_BARD_SONG_EFFECTS","effect"/>. A Bard can play as many tunes as they have experience <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>, before their throat gets dry. Then they need to get a drink from any nearby <l="MAN_TAVERN","tavern"/> to rejuvenate their voice. Tough duty, but someone has to do it.\n\n\n\nA song played as the party is exploring is long-lasting and continues into <b><l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/></b> and remains after the party returns, even if other songs were played during. After combat has ended, any songs and effects gained by playing during the battle, vanish.\n\n\n\nSongs played during combat have an abbreviated effect, creating different magical effects than the full non-combat versions. Some Bard songs can be played multiple times in combat - stacking the effect. Bard song strength varies with the bards level.\n\n\n\nOnly one Bard tune can be played at a time outside of combat. If a second one is played while the first is still playing (by the same or a different Bard), the first will end. \n\n\n\n@#BARD_STARTING_SONGS#=ZH=吟遊詩人透過演奏音樂來施展魔法。他們當然需要裝備一件樂器。他們可以在戰鬥中或探索時演奏，產生不同的 <l="MAN_BARD_SONG_EFFECTS","effect"/>。吟遊詩人可以在喉嚨乾燥前，演奏與其經驗 <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/> 相同數量的曲子。之後他們需要從附近的任何 <l="MAN_TAVERN","tavern"/> 喝點東西來恢復嗓音。任務艱鉅，但總得有人來做。\n\n當隊伍在探索時演奏的歌曲效果持久，會延續到 <b><l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/></b>，並在隊伍返回後仍然存在，即使期間演奏了其他歌曲。戰鬥結束後，戰鬥中演奏所獲得的任何歌曲與效果都會消失。\n\n戰鬥中演奏的歌曲效果會縮減，產生與非戰鬥完整版本不同的魔法效果。部分吟遊詩人之歌可在戰鬥中多次演奏，疊加效果。吟遊詩人之歌的強度隨吟遊詩人的等級而變化。\n\n非戰鬥時，一次只能演奏一首吟遊詩人之歌。如果在第一首仍在演奏時演奏第二首（由同一或不同吟遊詩人演奏），第一首將會結束。\n\n@#BARD_STARTING_SONGS#
Song Effects=ZH=歌曲效果
Inventory=ZH=道具欄
The party has a shared inventory space of <u>40</u>. When the inventory is full, no more items will be placed in it, until spaces are available. Visit <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/> to sell some wares, free up inventory space and make a little gold in the meanwhile.\n\n\n\nAn item which has been equipped is marked in white and is above the party inventory text. Items below the party inventory line are not equipped. Some items can only be used by specific characters. <l="MAN_ITEMS","Unidentified items"/> and an item which can never be used by a character is marked in red.<pos=75%><sprite=30/></pos>\n\n<color="white">Item</color> - Equipped Item.\n\n<color="black">Item</color> - Unequipped Item.\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Item</color> - Unidentified or class can not use.\n\n\n\n<color="white">Weapon</color>\n\n<color="white">Armor</color>\n\n<color="white">Gloves</color>\n\n<color=#664C33>@#PARTY_INVENTORY#</color>\n\n<color="black">Lamp (1)</color>\n\n<color="black">Lamp (1)</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Item</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Item</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified Weapon</color>=ZH=隊伍擁有共用的道具欄空間，共 <u>40</u> 格。當道具欄滿時，將無法再放入任何物品，直到有空位為止。前往 <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/> 出售一些貨物，清出道具欄空間，同時賺點金幣。\n\n已裝備的物品以白色標示，位於隊伍道具欄文字上方。隊伍道具欄線下方的物品則未裝備。部分物品僅能由特定角色使用。<l="MAN_ITEMS","Unidentified items"/> 而角色永遠無法使用的物品會以紅色標示。<pos=75%><sprite=30/></pos>\n\n<color="white">物品</color> - 已裝備物品。\n\n<color="black">物品</color> - 未裝備物品。\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>物品</color> - 未鑑定或職業無法使用。\n\n<color="white">武器</color>\n\n<color="white">護甲</color>\n\n<color="white">手套</color>\n\n<color=#664C33>@#PARTY_INVENTORY#</color>\n\n<color="black">燈具 (1)</color>\n\n<color="black">燈具 (1)</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>未鑑定物品</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>未鑑定物品</color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>未鑑定武器</color>
Items=ZH=物品
There are 11 different item types. Characters have 11 item slots. Only one item of each type can be equipped for use at any one time. Item types fall under 3 categories -\n\n\n\n<smallcaps>Offense<pos=20%>Defense</pos><pos=40%>Item</pos></smallcaps>\n\nWeapons<pos=20%>Armor</pos><pos=40%>Instrument</pos>\n\nOffhand<pos=20%>Helm</pos><pos=40%>General</pos>\n\nArrow<pos=20%>Gloves</pos><pos=40%>Figurine</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>Ring</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>Boots</pos>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Thief of Fate Specific</b></color>\n\nThere are two special item types which have special item slots in the the third of the trilogy -\n\nSuit\n\nContainer\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Unidentified Items & Specials</b></color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>Unidentified items</color> can be identified by a <l="MAN_ROGUE"/> or <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>. Items that have a special power can have it identified at <l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>.=ZH=共有11種不同的物品類型。角色擁有11個物品欄位。同一時間每種物品類型只能裝備一件使用。物品類型分為3個類別——\n\n<smallcaps>攻擊類<pos=20%>防禦類</pos><pos=40%>物品類</pos></smallcaps>\n\n武器<pos=20%>護甲</pos><pos=40%>樂器</pos>\n\n副手<pos=20%>頭盔</pos><pos=40%>通用</pos>\n\n箭矢<pos=20%>手套</pos><pos=40%>雕像</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>戒指</pos>\n\n<pos=20%>靴子</pos>\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>命運之賊專屬</b></color>\n\n在三部曲的第三部中，有兩種特殊的物品類型擁有專屬的物品欄位——\n\n套裝\n\n容器\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>未鑑定物品與特殊物品</b></color>\n\n<color=@#CantEquip#>未鑑定物品</color>可以透過<l="MAN_ROGUE"/>或<l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>來鑑定。具有特殊力量的物品可以在<l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>處鑑定其能力。
Gold=ZH=金幣
Gold is the currency used in all of the realms. It is primarily accumulated through <l="MAN_CHESTS","rewards"/> of successful combat. It can also be accumulated through selling wares at <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/> or gambling at the <l="MAN_CASINO","casino"/>. It can be stored at the <l="MAN_BANK","bank"/>, and spent at various <l="MAN_LOCATIONS","locations"/> throughout the realms.\n\n\n\nKeep a decent portion set aside for learning new <l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/> at the <l="MAN_REVIEW_BOARD","review board"/>, reviving and healing characters at the <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> and rejuvenating <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/> at <l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>.\n\n\n\nThe amount of gold that the party has can be seen at the bottom of the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>.<line-height=250%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=31/>=ZH=金幣是所有領域通用的貨幣。主要透過<l="MAN_CHESTS","rewards"/>成功的戰鬥來累積。也可以透過在<l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>販賣商品或在<l="MAN_CASINO","casino"/>賭博來獲得。金幣可以存放在<l="MAN_BANK","bank"/>，並在各領域的各種<l="MAN_LOCATIONS","locations"/>消費。\n\n\n\n記得保留相當一部分金幣，用於在<l="MAN_REVIEW_BOARD","review board"/>學習新的<l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/>、在<l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/>復活和治療角色，以及在<l="MAN_ROSCOES_ENERGY_EMPORIUM"/>恢復<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>。\n\n\n\n隊伍擁有的金幣數量可以在<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>底部看到。<line-height=250%>\n\n</line-height><sprite=31/>
Time=ZH=時間
Time waits for no one. Even without keystrokes, time passes, from day to night and back again. Night time is especially nasty. The really evil monsters hit the city streets and wilderness at night.\n\n\n\nIn addition, what's worse is that spell points get used up faster, because spell points only regenerate in daylight.\n\n\n\nTime is extremely important to keep in mind for <l="MAN_TIMED_TRAPS","timed traps"/>.=ZH=時間不等人。即使沒有按鍵輸入，時間依然流逝，從白天到夜晚，再回到白天。夜晚尤其危險。真正邪惡的怪物會在夜間出沒於城市街道和荒野。\n\n\n\n此外，更糟的是，法術點數消耗得更快，因為法術點數只在白天恢復。\n\n\n\n對於<l="MAN_TIMED_TRAPS","timed traps"/>來說，牢記時間極其重要。
Combat=ZH=戰鬥
Like a boxing match, combat is divided into a series of rounds. At the beginning of each round, a decision must be made for what action each character will perform in the upcoming fight. At the beginning of the round a menu of battle options will appear in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>, displaying the actions each character can perform the following round. There are several <b><l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","combat commands"/></b>, some are <b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b> specific.\n\n\n\nEnemies appear within a range of 10' to 90' feet from the party. Melee range is 10’ feet. Enemies 10' feet away are in melee range and can attack or be attacked with hand-held weapons. <l="MAN_ROGUE","Rogues"/> can choose to hide in shadows and, if successful, can increase their range by 10’ feet each combat round until they perform an attack.\n\n\n\nOnly the first 4 characters and the first 4 rows of monsters can be attacked physically, as long as they are within <l="MAN_MELEE_COMBAT","melee range"/>. The last 3 characters can only be struck by magical and ranged attacks, and can only attack back, with magic or ranged weapons. This simulates the first 4 party-members being up on the front line of attack, with the last 3 characters acting as backup in case one of the first 4 is killed. The first 4 groups of monsters are in a similar position, they are the only enemies who can attack or be attacked physically.\n\n\n\nOnce all the choices for characters have been entered, the round begins. The most <l="MAN_DEXTERITY","dexterous"/> characters or monsters usually make the first strike, but <l="MAN_LUCK","luck"/>, <l="MAN_LEVEL","character level"/> and <l="MAN_CLASS","character class"/> also affect this. In evenly matched fights, the outcome often depends on which side gets in the first blow.\n\n\n\nAt the end of a combat round, the monsters that are killed, are removed from the foe’s ranks so their bodies don’t pile up and interfere with battle. A fresh group of monsters may also advance into the first 4 ranks to assist their fellow monsters.\n\n\n\nIf party member dies in combat, they are shuffled to the bottom of the party list.\n\n\n\nWhen all of the enemies or party members in combat are dead (whichever comes first) - combat is over. Successfully defeating enemies in combat, awards each party member (characters only) <l="MAN_EXPERIENCE",@#XP#/>. The amount of XP awarded varies based on difficulty of the fight, the number of enemies defeated and the number of characters who survive. Surviving combat also offers the potential for the party to find a <l="MAN_CHESTS","chest"/>.\n\n\n\nCombat with enemies occurs both randomly and at set locations. Intra-party combat (Party Attack) can be initiated at almost any time, in case one of the members has become hostile.\n\n\n\nThe rate of scrolling of the combat messages can be <l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","sped up or slowed down"/>.=ZH=就像拳擊比賽一樣，戰鬥分為一系列回合。在每個回合開始時，必須決定每個角色在即將到來的戰鬥中將採取什麼行動。回合開始時，<l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>會出現一個戰鬥選項選單，顯示每個角色在下個回合可以執行的行動。有幾種<b><l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","combat commands"/></b>，有些是<b><l="MAN_CLASS","class"/></b>專屬的。\n\n\n\n敵人的出現範圍在距離隊伍10呎到90呎之間。近戰範圍是10呎。距離10呎的敵人處於近戰範圍內，可以使用手持武器攻擊或被攻擊。<l="MAN_ROGUE","Rogues"/>可以選擇隱入陰影，如果成功，每個戰鬥回合可以增加10呎的距離，直到他們發動攻擊。\n\n\n\n只有前4個角色和前4排的怪物可以在近戰範圍內受到物理攻擊。最後3個角色只能受到魔法和遠程攻擊，並且只能使用魔法或遠程武器反擊。這模擬了前4名隊員在前線攻擊，而最後3名角色則作為後備，以防前4名中的一人陣亡。前4組怪物也處於類似的位置，他們是唯一能夠進行物理攻擊或被物理攻擊的敵人。\n\n\n\n一旦所有角色的選擇都輸入完畢，回合就開始了。最<l="MAN_DEXTERITY","dexterous"/>的角色或怪物通常會先出手，但<l="MAN_LUCK","luck"/>、<l="MAN_LEVEL","character level"/>和<l="MAN_CLASS","character class"/>也會影響這一點。在勢均力敵的戰鬥中，結果往往取決於哪一方先出手。\n\n\n\n在戰鬥回合結束時，被殺死的怪物會從敵方隊伍中移除，這樣它們的屍體就不會堆積起來干擾戰鬥。新的一組怪物也可能會前進到前4排來協助它們的同類。\n\n\n\n如果隊伍成員在戰鬥中死亡，他們會被移到隊伍列表的底部。\n\n\n\n當戰鬥中的所有敵人或隊伍成員都死亡時（以先發生者為準）——戰鬥結束。成功擊敗戰鬥中的敵人，會獎勵每個隊伍成員（僅限角色）<l="MAN_EXPERIENCE",@#XP#/>。獲得的經驗值數量根據戰鬥的難度、擊敗的敵人數量和存活下來的角色數量而變化。在戰鬥中存活下來也提供了隊伍找到<l="MAN_CHESTS","chest"/>的可能性。\n\n\n\n與敵人的戰鬥既會隨機發生，也會在固定地點發生。隊伍內部戰鬥（隊伍攻擊）幾乎可以在任何時候發起，以防其中一名成員變得敵對。\n\n\n\n戰鬥訊息的滾動速度可以<l="MAN_COMBAT_COMMANDS","sped up or slowed down"/>。
Melee Combat=ZH=近戰
Melee combat is the act of physically assaulting enemies. Melee combat always takes place within 10' feet. Only the first 4 characters and the first 4 groups of monsters can be attacked physically. Melee damage can only be inflicted on enemies if they are in within melee range. Anything further than that, and the party members out of range will have to advance, throw a weapon or use an item to inflict damage. Weapons that have a range and the ability to be thrown, although they might not return. Weapons that can be thrown can still be used in melee combat (without throwing them).\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Critical Hit</b></color>\n\nCritical hits are an attack that hits a nerve center or other vital area which kills an opponent instantly. <l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/> and <l="MAN_ROGUE","Rogues"/> have the ability to perform critical hits. Rogues only have the ability to do so when hidden in shadows.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Multiple Attacks</b></color>\n\nMultiple attacks is the ability to attack the enemy with multiple hits within a single round of combat. <l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/>, <l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/> and <l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/> have the ability to perform multiple attacks.=ZH=近戰戰鬥是對敵人進行物理攻擊的行為。近戰戰鬥總是在10呎範圍內進行。只有前4個角色和前4組怪物可以受到物理攻擊。只有當敵人在近戰範圍內時，才能對他們造成近戰傷害。任何超出這個範圍的距離，隊伍成員就必須前進、投擲武器或使用物品來造成傷害。具有射程和可投擲能力的武器，雖然它們可能不會返回。可投擲的武器仍然可以在近戰戰鬥中使用（不投擲它們）。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>暴擊</b></color>\n\n暴擊是一種擊中神經中樞或其他要害部位的攻擊，能立即殺死對手。<l="MAN_HUNTER","Hunters"/>和<l="MAN_ROGUE","Rogues"/>有能力造成暴擊。盜賊只有在隱入陰影時才能做到這一點。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>多重攻擊</b></color>\n\n多重攻擊是指在單個戰鬥回合內對敵人進行多次攻擊的能力。<l="MAN_WARRIOR","Warriors"/>、<l="MAN_PALADIN","Paladins"/>和<l="MAN_MONK","Monks"/>有能力進行多重攻擊。
Ranged Combat=ZH=遠程戰鬥
Ranged combat is the act of attacking enemies from a distance.\n\nRange is represented by 10' - 90' feet. 20'+ is always ranged.\n\n\n\nWeapons that are thrown or shot are known as missile weapons and have a range in which they are effective. To use arrows, a bow must be equipped. Missile weapons are usually nonrecoverable - they can be used only once. There are, of course, exceptions to this rule. Some magical missile weapons such as Zen arrows can be reused, and at least one non magical missile weapon, the boomerang, can be reused because it returns to the one who threw it.\n\n\n\n<l="MAN_ITEMS","Weapons and items"/> will always display their maximum range.=ZH=遠程戰鬥是指從遠處攻擊敵人的作戰方式。\n\n距離以 10 呎至 90 呎表示。20 呎以上一律視為遠程。\n\n投擲或射出的武器稱為投射武器，並有其有效射程。要使用箭矢，必須裝備弓。投射武器通常無法回收——只能使用一次。當然，此規則也有例外。某些魔法投射武器（如禪箭）可以重複使用，而至少有一種非魔法投射武器——迴力鏢——因為會回到投擲者手中，所以也能重複使用。\n\n<l="MAN_ITEMS","Weapons and items"/> 永遠會顯示其最大射程。
Chests=ZH=寶箱
<pos=77.5f%><sprite=29/></pos><width=75%><pos=0.0%>After defeating a group of enemies in <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/> the party may find a chest. Chests can contain <l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/> and <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/>. A chest can also be armed with a <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","trap"/> and may need to be disarmed, in order to retrieve its glorious plunder.</width>\n\n\n\nMonitor how much free inventory space the party has. If the inventory is full, no items will be found in chests until there is space. Consider discarding unwanted items or selling them to <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>. It is wise to have a clean inventory before proceeding into a new dungeon.=ZH=<pos=77.5f%><sprite=29/></pos><width=75%><pos=0.0%>在 <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/> 擊敗一群敵人後，隊伍可能會找到一個寶箱。寶箱內可能含有 <l="MAN_GOLD","gold"/> 和 <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/>。寶箱也可能設有 <l="MAN_CHEST_TRAPS","trap"/>，需要先解除陷阱才能取得其中的輝煌戰利品。</width>\n\n請留意隊伍剩餘的道具欄空間。如果道具欄已滿，在清出空間之前，寶箱中將不會出現任何物品。考慮丟棄不需要的物品，或將它們賣給 <l="MAN_GARTHS_SHOPPE"/>。在進入新的地下城之前，保持道具欄整潔是明智之舉。
Chest Traps=ZH=寶箱陷阱
Traps in side chests as the same as regular <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/>. Chest traps can be disarmed by a skilled <l="MAN_ROGUE"/> as well as <l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","trap zapped"/> (<smallcaps><color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color></smallcaps>) by a <l="MAN_MAGES","mage"/>. The disarm chance of a rogue is dependant on their level and the difficulty of the traps within a dungeon. <color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color> has a <u>100%</u> chance of disarming a trap.\n\n\n\nIn the event that a trap has not been disarmed and the party continues to try and retrieve the contents within, the trap will unleash its trap on the party. If the character opening it is <l="MAN_LUCK","lucky"/> enough, they may avoid what the trap releases.\n\n\n\nBeware<size=100%>.<size=90%>.<size=80%>. <size=100%>traps can be extremely dangerous and potentially deadly. The party can always walk away from a chest. The loot retrieved may be less than what it costs to revive or heal those unfortunate enough to be subject to the traps effect.=ZH=側邊寶箱中的陷阱與一般 <l="MAN_TRAPS","traps"/> 相同。寶箱陷阱可由熟練的 <l="MAN_ROGUE"/> 解除，也可由 <l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","trap zapped"/>（<smallcaps><color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color></smallcaps>）透過 <l="MAN_MAGES","mage"/> 來解除。盜賊的解除陷阱機率取決於其等級以及地下城中陷阱的難度。<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color> 有 <u>100%</u> 的機率解除陷阱。\n\n如果陷阱未被解除，而隊伍仍試圖取出內容物，陷阱便會對隊伍發動攻擊。若開啟寶箱的角色夠 <l="MAN_LUCK","lucky"/>，或許能避開陷阱的影響。\n\n小心 <size=100%>。<size=90%>。<size=80%>。<size=100%>陷阱可能極度危險，甚至致命。隊伍隨時可以放棄寶箱。取得的戰利品，可能還比不上復活或治療那些不幸中招者的代價。
Locations=ZH=地點
There are many locations to explore within the world of 'The Bard's Tale' which all serve different purposes.=ZH=在《冰城傳奇》的世界中，有許多可供探索的地點，各自發揮不同的功用。
The Adventurer's Guild=ZH=冒險者公會
The Guild is like a union hall where adventurers hang out, hoping to join a party. This is where a party is formed and where all adventures begin.\n\n\n\nA party can have a total roster of 7 characters. Characters can also be added, removed, renamed, change their portrait, or permanently delete them. Entire parties can also be saved or deleted. There is a pre-built party - <b>The A-TEAM</b>, ready to jump straight into gameplay. They might not have the best <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/> or <l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/> but they get the job done.\n\n\n\nIn <b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b>, and <b> The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>, the game starts in the <b>Adventurer's Guild</b>. In <b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b>, the game starts in the <b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b>.\n\n\n\nThe Adventurer's Guild and the refugee camp are the <u>only</u> spots a party can be formed, characters created, and parties saved.\n\n\n\nIf an entire party roster dies while out and about, they will be transported, <u>still dead</u>, to the guild. By creating and adding a live character, the new very much alive character can then ferry the dead characters to a <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> to resurrect them, or just remove or delete them and create new characters.\n\n\n\nVisiting an Adventurer's Guild makes time seem like it's flying by. When exiting, it always turns night to day.\n\n\n\nThe Adventurer's Guild is <u>always open</u>.=ZH=公會就像冒險者聚集的工會大廳，他們在此等待加入隊伍。這裡是組成隊伍的地方，也是所有冒險的起點。\n\n一支隊伍最多可容納 7 名角色。角色可以新增、移除、重新命名、更換肖像，或永久刪除。整個隊伍也可以儲存或刪除。遊戲內建了一支預設隊伍——<b>A 小隊</b>，準備好直接投入遊戲。他們或許不是 <l="MAN_ATTRIBUTES","attributes"/> 或 <l="MAN_STATISTICS","statistics"/> 最強的，但足以完成任務。\n\n在 <b>《未知傳奇》（BT1）</b> 和 <b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b> 中，遊戲從 <b>冒險者公會</b> 開始。在 <b>《命運之賊》（BT3）</b> 中，遊戲則從 <b><l="MAN_REFUGEE_CAMP","refugee camp"/></b> 開始。\n\n冒險者公會和難民營是 <u>唯一</u> 可以組成隊伍、創建角色以及儲存隊伍的地點。\n\n如果整支隊伍在外出時全滅，他們會被傳送回公會，<u>仍然死亡</u>。透過創建並加入一名存活角色，這個活蹦亂跳的新角色就能將死者運送到 <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> 進行復活，或者直接移除或刪除他們，再創建新角色。\n\n造訪冒險者公會會讓時間飛逝。離開時，總是會從夜晚轉為白天。\n\n冒險者公會 <u>全天候開放</u>。
Refugee Camp=ZH=難民營
Once, there was a home to travellers called the <b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b>. Wayfarers stopped there when they wanted to slake a thirst, engage in merriment, or just rest their weary feet. Now that the plague has descended and Skara Brae has been shattered, the Refugee Camp serves as the gathering point for travellers.\n\n\n\nThe Refugee Camp is the same as the Adventurer's Guild, although lacking the niceties like tables, heating and vermin-free bedding, they do the same things.\n\n\n\nIf an entire party roster dies while out and about, they will be transported, <u>still dead</u>, to the camp. By creating and adding a live character, the new very much alive character can then ferry the dead characters to a <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> to resurrect them, or just remove or delete them and create new characters.\n\n\n\nVisiting the refugee camp, it will make time seem like it's flying by. When exiting, it always turns night to day.\n\n\n\nThe refugee camp is <u>always open</u>.=ZH=從前，有一個名為 <b><l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/></b> 的旅者之家。旅人們會在口渴時、想尋歡作樂時，或只是為了讓疲憊的雙腳休息時，在此停留。如今瘟疫降臨，斯卡拉布雷城已支離破碎，難民營成了旅人們的聚集點。\n\n難民營與冒險者公會功能相同，雖然缺乏桌子、暖氣和沒有蟲子的床鋪等舒適設施，但能做的事情都一樣。\n\n如果整支隊伍在外出時全滅，他們會被傳送回營地，<u>仍然死亡</u>。透過創建並加入一名存活角色，這個活蹦亂跳的新角色就能將死者運送到 <l="MAN_TEMPLE","temple"/> 進行復活，或者直接移除或刪除他們，再創建新角色。\n\n造訪難民營會讓時間飛逝。離開時，總是會從夜晚轉為白天。\n\n難民營 <u>全天候開放</u>。
Review Board=ZH=仲裁委員會
The Review Board is a group of high level representatives from all 13 different <b><l="MAN_CLASS","classes"/></b>.\n\n\n\nUpon an in person request, the board will determine whether a character has accumulated enough experience points for advancement to higher <l="MAN_LEVEL","levels"/>. The board teaches new <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/> to magic users who qualify for higher <b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell levels"/></b> too, but charges for this training.\n\n\n\nReview Boards are <u>closed</u> at night.=ZH=仲裁委員會是由全部13種不同職業的高階代表所組成的團體。\n\n親自提出請求後，委員會將判定一名角色是否累積了足夠的經驗值以晉升至更高的等級。委員會也會傳授新的法術給符合晉升條件的魔法使用者，但會為此收取訓練費用。\n\n仲裁委員會在夜間是關閉的。
Garth's Shoppe=ZH=加斯商店
Garth is one of yesterday's almost forgotten heroes, now retired from active adventuring to supply tomorrow's would-be greats. At Garth's shoppe characters can buy armor and weapons, sell items, have items identified, or pool all the party's gold for buying expensive items.\n\n\n\nDue to a deal with the city blacksmith, Garth has an infinite supply of basic armor and supplies. But unique Items found in labyrinths, even if sold to Garth, remain in his inventory only until they are sold. Occasionally an item won in combat will remain unidentified. This means that its general type might be apparent such as a shield or ring, but not which exact type, like a dragon shield or ring of power.\n\n\n\nGarth can identify items... for a price.\n\n\n\nGarth's shop is <u>closed</u> at night.=ZH=Garth是昨日幾乎被遺忘的英雄之一，如今已從活躍的冒險生涯退休，為明日未來的偉大人物提供補給。在Garth的商店裡，角色可以購買護甲與武器、販賣物品、鑑定物品，或集中隊伍的所有金幣來購買昂貴的物品。\n\n由於與城市鐵匠有協議，Garth擁有無限供應的基本護甲與補給品。但在地城中找到的獨特物品，即使賣給Garth，也只會留在他的庫存中直到售出為止。偶爾在戰鬥中獲得的物品會保持未鑑定狀態。這意味著其大致類型可能顯而易見，例如盾牌或戒指，但無法確定具體是哪一種，例如龍盾或力量戒指。\n\nGarth可以鑑定物品……但要收費。\n\nGarth的商店在夜間是關閉的。
Roscoe's Energy Emporium=ZH=羅斯柯能量商店
If spell points aren't re-charging fast enough, visit Roscoe's energy emporium. Roscoe will replenish a single or all magic users spell points for a fee. But be prepared for electrifyingly high fees. \n\n\n\nRoscoe can identify specials traits of weapons, armor and items... for a price.\n\n\n\nRoscoes is <u>open</u> at night.=ZH=如果法術點數恢復得不夠快，請造訪Roscoe的能量商店。Roscoe會收取費用，為單一或所有魔法使用者的法術點數進行補充。但請準備好面對高得驚人的費用。\n\nRoscoe可以鑑定武器、護甲與物品的特殊屬性……但要收費。\n\nRoscoe的商店在夜間是營業的。
Tavern=ZH=酒館
Taverns are favorite places to obtain refreshment and gossip. Watch <b><l="MAN_BARD","Bards"/></b> in taverns - they have a tendency to over tip the bartender and - tip over the barmaid.\n\n\n\nOrder a drink for a bard to refresh the amount <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","songs"/> they can sing.\n\n\n\nTaverns are <u>open</u> at night.=ZH=酒館是獲取飲料與八卦的最愛去處。在酒館裡要留意吟遊詩人——他們往往會給酒保太多小費，還會撞倒女侍者。\n\n為吟遊詩人點一杯飲料，可以恢復他們能夠吟唱的次數。\n\n酒館在夜間是營業的。
Temple=ZH=神廟
Temples are divine places of resurrection and complete healing.\n\n\n\nA resurrected character retains all the items, gold and experience points they had before dying, but comes back to life with only 1 hit point.\n\n\n\nWhen they finds out how much it cost to be resurrected, they may turn over their grave.\n\n\n\nTemples are <u>open</u> at night.=ZH=神廟是進行復活與完全治療的神聖場所。\n\n被復活的角色會保留死亡前擁有的所有物品、金幣與經驗值，但復活時僅有1點生命值。\n\n當他們發現復活要花多少錢時，可能會氣得在墳墓裡翻身。\n\n神廟在夜間是營業的。
Bank=ZH=銀行
<width=71f%>Bedder - an old half-elf who reportedly sold his mother to a band of lonely orcs in order to finance his first branch - will deposit gold for safekeeping at any of his bank's branches throughout the realm. Although stingy old Bedder doesn't pay interest on gold deposits, his bank is never robbed and gold can be withdrawn at any time, at any branch.</width><pos=72.5f%><sprite=28/></pos>\n\n\n\nBedder's Bank for the Bold can also safely store some items the party has accumulated through adventuring in the banks safety deposit slots. Unfortunately, Bedder charges a hefty fee to purchase these safety deposit slots. For 10,000 gold he will safely store 10 items. With another 50,000 gold Bedder's bank will store an additional 10 items.\n\n\n\n60,000 gold for 20 safety deposit boxes may not be the cheapest thing in the realm, but it is handy.\n\n\n\nBanks are <u>closed</u> at night.=ZH=<width=71f%>貝德——一名年邁的半精靈，據說為了籌措開設第一家分行，將自己的母親賣給了一群孤獨的獸人——他會在王國各地的銀行分行為冒險隊伍保管金幣。雖然吝嗇的老貝德不支付存款利息，但他的銀行從未被搶劫過，金幣也可隨時在任何分行提取。</width><pos=72.5f%><sprite=28/></pos>\n\n\n貝德勇者銀行還可以在銀行的安全保管箱中，安全存放冒險隊伍在冒險中累積的一些物品。不幸的是，貝德對購買這些安全保管箱收取高額費用。花費10,000金幣，他可以安全存放10件物品。再花費50,000金幣，貝德的銀行將額外存放10件物品。\n\n\n花費60,000金幣購買20個安全保管箱，可能不是王國中最便宜的選擇，但確實很方便。\n\n\n銀行在夜間<u>關閉</u>。
Casino=ZH=賭場
Casino gambling is a favorite way to relax after a hard day of battling orcs, and casinos are found in almost all of the Realm's cities. The casinos play a game whose closest equivalent is BlackJack.\n\n\n\nCasinos are <u>closed</u> at night.=ZH=賭場賭博是在與獸人激戰一天後最受歡迎的放鬆方式，王國中幾乎所有城市都有賭場。賭場玩一種最接近二十一點的遊戲。\n\n\n賭場在夜間<u>關閉</u>。
Unmarked Buildings=ZH=無標記建築
Most buildings are unmarked, but some of them are inhabited by corrupt creatures who have skulked in. Don't be afraid to kick in a few doors and find them. Remember that fighting is good for the party, it's the only way to build up enough experience to challenge the more and more difficult enemies.\n\n\n\nThere are also handy tips in a few of these.=ZH=大多數建築物都沒有標記，但其中一些被潛入的腐敗生物佔據。不要害怕踢開幾扇門去找出它們。記住，戰鬥對冒險隊伍有益，這是累積足夠經驗值以挑戰越來越強大敵人的唯一途徑。\n\n\n其中一些地方也藏有實用的小提示。
Dungeons & Towers=ZH=地下城與高塔
Dungeons take a variety of forms such as catacombs, tombs, underwater palaces and pits. Towers also take a variety of forms such as keeps, castles and towers. Both dungeons and towers can have a varied number of levels.\n\n\n\nLevels higher or lower than the parties current level, can be navigated to by using <b><l="MAN_STAIRS","stairways"/></b>, <b><l="MAN_PORTAL","portals"/></b>, or <l="MAN_TELEPORTATION","teleportation"/> - whichever is more convenient or readily available. \n\n\n\nAll noteworthy tiles are marked in the journal and have a respective icon.=ZH=地下城有多種形式，例如地下墓穴、陵墓、水下宮殿和深坑。高塔也有多種形式，例如堡壘、城堡和塔樓。地下城和高塔都可能擁有不同數量的樓層。\n\n\n可以使用<b><l="MAN_STAIRS","stairways"/></b>、<b><l="MAN_PORTAL","portals"/></b>或<l="MAN_TELEPORTATION","teleportation"/>——視哪一種更方便或更容易取得——來前往比冒險隊伍當前樓層更高或更低的樓層。\n\n\n所有值得注意的地磚都會標記在日誌中，並帶有相應的圖示。
Stairs=ZH=樓梯
Stairways are not visible from a distance. But when the party moves onto one, a prompt will appear in the <l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/> asking whether to ascend or descend.\n\n\n\nEach <u>succeeding</u> level goes <u><b><smallcaps>up</smallcaps></b></u> in a tower, and <u><b><smallcaps>down</smallcaps></b></u> in a dungeon.=ZH=樓梯在遠處無法看見。但當冒險隊伍移動到樓梯上時，<l="MAN_SCROLL","scroll"/>中會出現提示，詢問是要向上還是向下。\n\n\n在高塔中，每<u>往後</u>的樓層是<u><b><smallcaps>向上</smallcaps></b></u>；在地下城中，則是<u><b><smallcaps>向下</smallcaps></b></u>。
Portals=ZH=傳送門
Portals are holes in the floors and ceilings, and are visible from a distance. The party will not go through a portal merely by standing on it. They must first be instructed to do so. If they jump down through a portal, they will be damaged from the fall unless a levitation spell is active, or an item that conjures the magical equivalent. The only way to go <smallcaps><b><u>up</u></b></smallcaps> through a portal is by levitation.\n\n\n\nA basic levitation (<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>LEVI</color>) spell is learnt by a <b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b> with a <b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b> of 3 or higher.\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=19/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Q</color></b> - Portal Up.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Ascend up through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=20/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Z</color></b> - Portal Down.\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>Descend down through a portal.</indent>\n\n\n\nEach <u>succeeding</u> level goes <u><b><smallcaps>up</smallcaps></b></u> in a tower, and <u><b><smallcaps>down</smallcaps></b></u> in a dungeon.=ZH=傳送門是地板和天花板上的洞口，從遠處就能看見。冒險隊伍不會因為站在傳送門上就通過它。他們必須先下達指令才行。如果他們從傳送門跳下去，除非有漂浮法術生效，或是有能產生同等魔法效果的物品，否則會因墜落而受傷。唯一能<smallcaps><b><u>向上</u></b></smallcaps>通過傳送門的方法就是漂浮。\n\n一個基本的漂浮（<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>LEVI</color>）法術，可由一位<b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>且<b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b>達到3或以上者習得。\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=19/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Q</color></b> - 傳送門向上。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>向上穿過傳送門。</indent>\n\n<line-height=130%><sprite=20/> <b><color=@#InteractionCommand#>Z</color></b> - 傳送門向下。\n\n</line-height><indent=1.2f%>向下穿過傳送門。</indent>\n\n每一層<u>後續</u>的樓層，在塔中會<u><b><smallcaps>向上</smallcaps></b></u>，而在地下城中則會<u><b><smallcaps>向下</smallcaps></b></u>。
Portals=ZH=傳送門
Portals are holes in the floors and ceilings, and are visible from a distance. The party will not go through a portal merely by standing on it. They must first be instructed to do so. If they jump down through a portal, they will be damaged from the fall unless a levitation spell is active, or an item that conjures the magical equivalent. The only way to go <smallcaps><b><u>up</u></b></smallcaps> through a portal is by levitation.\n\n\n\nA basic levitation (<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>LEVI</color>) spell is learnt by a <b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b> with a <b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b> of 3 or higher.\n\n\n\nEach <u>succeeding</u> level goes <u><b><smallcaps>up</smallcaps></b></u> in a tower, and <u><b><smallcaps>down</smallcaps></b></u> in a dungeon.=ZH=傳送門是地板和天花板上的洞口，從遠處就能看見。冒險隊伍不會因為站在傳送門上就通過它。他們必須先下達指令才行。如果他們從傳送門跳下去，除非有漂浮法術生效，或是有能產生同等魔法效果的物品，否則會因墜落而受傷。唯一能<smallcaps><b><u>向上</u></b></smallcaps>通過傳送門的方法就是漂浮。\n\n一個基本的漂浮（<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>LEVI</color>）法術，可由一位<b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>且<b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b>達到3或以上者習得。\n\n每一層<u>後續</u>的樓層，在塔中會<u><b><smallcaps>向上</smallcaps></b></u>，而在地下城中則會<u><b><smallcaps>向下</smallcaps></b></u>。
Magic Detection Skull=ZH=魔法偵測頭骨
The skull at the top of the <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW,","3D game view"/>, shows information related to the current effects on the party within dungeons.\n\n\n\nWhen the skull's eyes are <u>not</u> glowing, the dungeon has no aura and the party is in a harm free space.\n\n\n\nWhen the skull's eyes <b>are</b> glowing, it is in a state where it has information about something in the dungeon that is causing an effect on the party.\n\n\n\n<color=#e50b0b>Red</color> - only a curse, active aura, event timer or general harm is affecting the party.\n\n<color=#0b7cef>Blue</color> - only a blessing is affecting the party.\n\n<color=#9d10e5>Purple</color> - a combination of blessings and curses.\n\n<pos=20%><sprite=52/></pos><pos=45%><sprite=54/></pos><pos=70%><sprite=53/></pos><line-height=350%>\n\n</line-height>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Curses</b></color>\n\nA curse is a magical effect that is inflicted upon the party to hinder their ability to navigate. Curses can impair vision, stop bard songs and even stop the use of magic. Curses are commonly documented as <l="MAN_DUNGEON_HAZARDS","hazardous"/> locations.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Blessings</b></color>\n\nBlessings are a magical effect that benefit party members. Blessings are commonly documented as locations of <l="MAN_DUNGEON_REGENERATION","regeneration"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Active Aura</b></color>\n\nAn aura is an invisible magical essence that lurks within the dungeon. The aura can also be built directly into the dungeons structure. Auras can cause anti-magic, anti-apport or anti-phasedoor.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Event Timers</b></color>\n\n<l="MAN_TIMED_TRAPS","Timed traps"/> are time based events. The magic detection skull keeps track of the time left in these events.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>General Harm</b></color>\n\nSometimes the party gets into a bit of trouble, the magic detection skull also shows if the party is drowning or freezing.=ZH=位於<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW,","3D game view"/>頂端的骷髏頭，會顯示與地下城中當前影響隊伍的效果相關的資訊。\n\n當骷髏頭的眼睛<u>沒有</u>發光時，表示地下城沒有靈氣，隊伍處於無害的空間。\n\n當骷髏頭的眼睛<b>正在</b>發光時，表示它處於一種狀態，顯示地下城中某物正對隊伍造成影響。\n\n<color=#e50b0b>紅色</color> - 僅有詛咒、活躍靈氣、事件計時器或一般傷害正在影響隊伍。\n\n<color=#0b7cef>藍色</color> - 僅有祝福正在影響隊伍。\n\n<color=#9d10e5>紫色</color> - 祝福與詛咒的組合。\n\n<pos=20%><sprite=52/></pos><pos=45%><sprite=54/></pos><pos=70%><sprite=53/></pos><line-height=350%>\n\n</line-height>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>詛咒</b></color>\n\n詛咒是一種施加在隊伍身上以妨礙其導航能力的魔法效果。詛咒會削弱視力、停止吟遊詩人之歌，甚至阻止魔法的使用。詛咒通常被記錄為<l="MAN_DUNGEON_HAZARDS","hazardous"/>地點。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>祝福</b></color>\n\n祝福是一種有益於隊伍成員的魔法效果。祝福通常被記錄為<l="MAN_DUNGEON_REGENERATION","regeneration"/>的地點。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>活躍靈氣</b></color>\n\n靈氣是一種潛伏在地下城中的無形魔法本質。靈氣也可能直接建構在地下城的結構中。靈氣可能造成反魔法、反傳送或反相位門。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>事件計時器</b></color>\n\n<l="MAN_TIMED_TRAPS","Timed traps"/>是基於時間的事件。魔法偵測骷髏頭會追蹤這些事件剩餘的時間。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>一般傷害</b></color>\n\n有時候隊伍會陷入一些麻煩，魔法偵測骷髏頭也會顯示隊伍是否正在溺水或凍僵。
Teleportation=ZH=傳送術
Teleportation requires the Apport Arcane (<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color>) spell.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color> can be cast from a magic user that has been, or is a <b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b> with a <b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b> of 7 or higher.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color> teleports from the parties <u><smallcaps>current</smallcaps></u> location. <color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color> always travels in the worlds North, East, South and West directions. Not the relative direction of the party.\n\nScry Sight (<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>SCSI</color>) is a helpful spell to identify which direction the party is facing. The direction is also pointed out in the journal, minimap and <l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/> compass if the appropriate spells are active.=ZH=傳送需要傳送奧術（<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color>）法術。\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color>可由一位曾經是或現在是<b><l="MAN_CONJURER"/></b>且<b><l="MAN_SPELL_LEVEL","spell level"/></b>達到7或以上的法師施放。\n\n<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color>會從隊伍<u><smallcaps>當前</smallcaps></u>的位置進行傳送。<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>APAR</color>總是沿著世界的北、東、南、西方向移動，而非隊伍的相對方向。\n\n千里眼（<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>SCSI</color>）是一個有助於辨識隊伍面向方向的法術。如果適當的法術已生效，該方向也會在日誌、小地圖和<l="MAN_PARTY_STATUS","party status"/>指南針中標示出來。
Hazards=ZH=危險
Hazards come in many shapes and forms. They are scattered throughout <l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons & towers"/> in 'The Bard’s Tale'. All of these tiles have a corresponding icon within the journal.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Darkness</smallcaps></color>\n\nCancels any light from the party. Including light or vision improving effect produced by <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>, <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/> and <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Trap</smallcaps></color>\n\nTrap tiles can contain any random <l="MAN_TRAPS","trap"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Encounter</smallcaps></color>\n\nForces the party into <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/> with a random group of enemies.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Spinner</smallcaps></color>\n\nSpins the party to face a random direction when landing on the trap, and after executing an action. Some <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/>, <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/> or <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/> may help reduce the possibility of being spun.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Anti-Magic</smallcaps></color>\n\nA field which causes <b><u>all</u></b> magical <l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/> being cast, to fizzle. <l="MAN_ITEMS"/> can still be used in this field.\n\n<pos=80%><sprite=33/></pos>\n\n<width=77.5f%><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Health Drain</smallcaps></color>\n\nDrains an amount of <l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/> (@#HP#) from every party member after a short period of time.</width>\n\n<pos=80%><sprite=32/></pos>\n\n<width=77.5f%><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Magic Drain</smallcaps></color>\n\nDrains an amount of <l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/> (@#SP#) from every party member after a short period of time.</width>\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Silence</smallcaps></color>\n\nStops all <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/> currently being played, and stops any bard songs from being sung.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Turncoat</smallcaps></color>\n\nTurns a random monster in the party against the team. Initiating intra-party <l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Smoke</smallcaps></color>\n\nReduces visibility of the in game <l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Explosive Gas</smallcaps></color>\n\nExplodes when coming into contact with any open flame, dealing damage to each party member, just like a <l="MAN_TRAPS","trap"/>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Anti-Apport Arcane</smallcaps></color>\n\nStops any attempt to <l="MAN_TELEPORTATION","teleport"/> to or from this tile.\n\n\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Magic Chains</smallcaps></color>\n\nTemporarily immobilizes the party from moving forward. Moving lots might break the effects of magic chains.=ZH=危險有許多不同的形狀和形式。它們散佈在《冰城傳奇》的<l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons & towers"/>各處。所有這些方格在日誌中都有對應的圖示。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>黑暗</smallcaps></color>\n\n取消隊伍的所有光源。包括由<l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>、<l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/>和<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>產生的光線或視覺強化效果。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>陷阱</smallcaps></color>\n\n陷阱方格可能包含任何隨機的<l="MAN_TRAPS","trap"/>。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>遭遇</smallcaps></color>\n\n強迫隊伍與一群隨機敵人進入<l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>迷向器</smallcaps></color>\n\n當踏上陷阱時，以及在執行一個動作後，會將隊伍旋轉至一個隨機方向。某些<l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/>、<l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>或<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>可能有助於降低被旋轉的可能性。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>反魔法</smallcaps></color>\n\n一個會導致<b><u>所有</u></b>正在施放的魔法<l="MAN_SPELLS","spells"/>失效的力場。在此力場中仍可使用<l="MAN_ITEMS"/>。\n\n<pos=80%><sprite=33/></pos>\n\n<width=77.5f%><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>生命值吸取</smallcaps></color>\n\n在一段短時間後，從每個隊伍成員身上吸取一定量的<l="MAN_HIT_POINTS","hit points"/>（@#HP#）。</width>\n\n<pos=80%><sprite=32/></pos>\n\n<width=77.5f%><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>法術點數吸取</smallcaps></color>\n\n在一段短時間後，從每個隊伍成員身上吸取一定量的<l="MAN_SPELL_POINTS","spell points"/>（@#SP#）。</width>\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>沉默</smallcaps></color>\n\n停止所有正在演奏的<l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>，並阻止任何吟遊詩人之歌被唱出。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>叛變</smallcaps></color>\n\n將隊伍中的一個隨機怪物轉而對抗團隊。引發隊伍內部的<l="MAN_COMBAT","combat"/>。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>煙霧</smallcaps></color>\n\n降低遊戲中<l="MAN_3D_GAME_VIEW","3D game view"/>的可見度。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>爆炸性氣體</smallcaps></color>\n\n接觸到任何明火時會爆炸，對每個隊伍成員造成傷害，就像<l="MAN_TRAPS","trap"/>一樣。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>反傳送奧術</smallcaps></color>\n\n阻止任何從此方格<l="MAN_TELEPORTATION","teleport"/>進出或傳送至該方格的嘗試。\n\n<color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>魔法鎖鏈</smallcaps></color>\n\n暫時使隊伍無法向前移動。大量移動或許能打破魔法鎖鏈的效果。
Traps=ZH=陷阱
Beware<size=100%>.<size=90%>.<size=80%>. <size=100%>traps can be extremely dangerous and potentially deadly. Traps can be disarmed by <l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","trap zapping"/> (<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color>) them. <color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color> has a <u>100%</u> chance of disarming a trap. If leading party member has a high level of <l="MAN_LUCK","luck"/>, they may cause the party to avoid what the trap releases.\n\n\n\nTraps can affect a <smallcaps>single party member</smallcaps> or the <smallcaps>entire party</smallcaps>. Traps can inflict <smallcaps>damage</smallcaps>, <smallcaps>conditions</smallcaps>, or both. Characters get a saving roll to avoid the trap.=ZH=請小心，<size=100%><size=90%><size=80%>。<size=100%>陷阱極度危險，可能致命。陷阱可由 <l="MAN_CONJURER_SPELLS","trap zapping"/>（<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color>）解除。<color=@#SPELL_HEADER_COL#>TRZP</color> 有 <u>100%</u> 的機率解除陷阱。若領隊角色擁有較高的 <l="MAN_LUCK","luck"/> 等級，可能使隊伍避開陷阱釋放的影響。\n\n陷阱可能影響 <smallcaps>單一隊員</smallcaps> 或 <smallcaps>整個隊伍</smallcaps>。陷阱可能造成 <smallcaps>傷害</smallcaps>、<smallcaps>狀態效果</smallcaps>，或兩者兼具。角色可進行豁免擲骰以避免陷阱。
Regeneration=ZH=再生
Scattered throughout <l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons & towers"/> are regeneration tiles. All of these tiles have a corresponding icon within the journal.\n\n<sprite=33/>\n\n<margin-left=16fem><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Health Regeneration</smallcaps></color>\n\nRegenerates an amount of Hit Points (@#HP#) to every party member after a short period of time. The amount of @#HP# regenerated will benefit from <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/> and <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>.</margin>\n\n<sprite=32/>\n\n<margin-left=16fem><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>Magic Regeneration</smallcaps></color>\n\nRegenerates an amount of Spell Points (@#SP#) to every party member after a short period of time. The amount of @#SP# regenerated will benefit from <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/> and <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>.</margin>=ZH=<l="MAN_DUNGEONS","dungeons & towers"/> 各處散落著再生地磚。所有此類地磚在日誌中都有對應的圖示。\n\n<sprite=33/>\n\n<margin-left=16fem><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>生命值再生</smallcaps></color>\n\n每隔一小段時間，為每位隊員恢復一定數量的生命值（@#HP#）。恢復的 @#HP# 量將受益於 <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/> 和 <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>。</margin>\n\n<sprite=32/>\n\n<margin-left=16fem><color=@#TrapHazard#><smallcaps>法術點數再生</smallcaps></color>\n\n每隔一小段時間，為每位隊員恢復一定數量的法術點數（@#SP#）。恢復的 @#SP# 量將受益於 <l="MAN_ITEMS","items"/> 和 <l="MAN_BARD_SONGS","Bard songs"/>。</margin>
Timed Traps=ZH=計時陷阱
Dungeons can contain timed traps which operate in real world seconds. Pausing the game will not stop these timers and the game can not be saved whilst in one.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Death Snare</b></color>\n\nSnares are special areas, that once entered - must have a puzzle solved within a time frame, in order to exit. Keep a close eye on the amount of time remaining to solve a Death Snare, dreadful things happen once it reaches zero. Death snares are specific to <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Tripwire</b></color>\n\nTripwires are special traps, that once triggered - is a race to get as far away as possible, as fast as possible. Disarming a tripwire requires the party to move onto a specific, but hidden location. Once the trap goes off, those standing near enough don't fare too well.\n\n\n\nTime remaining for death snares and tripwires can be monitored by the <l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","magic detection skull"/>.=ZH=地下城中可能含有以現實世界秒數計時的陷阱。暫停遊戲無法停止這些計時器，且身處其中時無法存檔。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>死亡陷阱</b></color>\n\n死亡陷阱是特殊的區域，一旦進入，就必須在時限內解開謎題才能離開。請密切注意剩餘時間，一旦歸零，可怕的事情就會發生。死亡陷阱是 <b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b> 特有的。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>絆索</b></color>\n\n絆索是特殊的陷阱，一旦觸發，就是一場盡快逃離的競賽。解除絆索需要隊伍移動到一個特定但隱藏的地點。一旦陷阱觸發，附近的人下場都不會太好。\n\n死亡陷阱和絆索的剩餘時間可透過 <l="MAN_MAGIC_DETECTION_SKULL","magic detection skull"/> 監控。
Legacy Mode=ZH=經典模式
Legacy Mode will revert new features in the remaster and try to behave more like the original 1980's titles. This includes things such as changing how features have been merged across the three games.\n\n\n\nLegacy settings can be toggled on and off to suit a variety of different play styles and desires. These settings are -\n\n<l="MAN_INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MISC_GAME_DIFFERENCES"/>\n\n<l="MAN_GAME_SPECIFIC_SONGS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_USE_UNEQUIPPED_ITEMS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_AUTOMAP_DISABLED"/>\n\n<l="MAN_FULL_XP_REQUIREMENTS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_SAVE_AT_GUILD_ONLY"/>\n\n<l="MAN_NO_ITEMS_IN_EMPTY_HOUSES"/>\n\n\n\nAny save game with a legacy option on, will have an icon in the load and save file menus.\n\n<sprite=65/>=ZH=經典模式將還原重製版中的新功能，並試圖更貼近 1980 年代原版遊戲的表現。這包括改變三款遊戲功能合併方式等內容。\n\n經典設定可自由開啟或關閉，以適應各種不同的遊玩風格與需求。這些設定包括——\n\n<l="MAN_INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY"/>\n\n<l="MAN_MISC_GAME_DIFFERENCES"/>\n\n<l="MAN_GAME_SPECIFIC_SONGS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_USE_UNEQUIPPED_ITEMS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_AUTOMAP_DISABLED"/>\n\n<l="MAN_FULL_XP_REQUIREMENTS"/>\n\n<l="MAN_SAVE_AT_GUILD_ONLY"/>\n\n<l="MAN_NO_ITEMS_IN_EMPTY_HOUSES"/>\n\n任何開啟經典選項的存檔，在讀取與存檔選單中都會顯示一個圖示。\n\n<sprite=65/>
Individual Inventory=ZH=個別道具欄
@#INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY_HINT#\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Gold</b></color>\n\nGold is displayed on each party members character information sheet, along with their used inventory space and inventory capacity. (Used space / Capacity)\n\nTo pool gold or trade gold, open a party members character information sheet.\n\nPool gold - All of the gold that each party member has gets given to this character.\n\nTrade gold - Give gold to a selected party member.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Items</b></color>\n\nTo trade items, open a characters inventory.\n\nTrade item to another party member - Sends the selected item to the chosen party member.=ZH=@#INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY_HINT#\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>金幣</b></color>\n\n每位隊員的角色資訊頁面上都會顯示金幣數量，以及已使用的道具欄空間與道具欄容量。（已使用空間 / 容量）\n\n若要集中金幣或交易金幣，請開啟隊員的角色資訊頁面。\n\n集中金幣——將所有隊員持有的金幣全部給予此角色。\n\n交易金幣——將金幣給予指定的隊員。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>物品</b></color>\n\n若要交易物品，請開啟角色的道具欄。\n\n將物品交易給其他隊員——將選中的物品發送給指定的隊員。
Misc Game Differences=ZH=其他遊戲差異
Misc Game Differences contains a number of differences between each game but apply all of the listed changes.\n\n\n\n<align="center"><color=@#Heading#><b>Differences - Summarized</b></color></align>\n\n<smallcaps><b>All Games</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- Items & spells adjusted to match each game.</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- Special slot for monsters and summoned creatures.\n\n- No archery.\n\n- No item charges. Instead items have a small chance to disappear on each use.\n\n- Individual inventory provides <u>8</u> inventory slots per character.</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- Individual inventory provides <u>10</u> inventory slots per character.</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- Exclusive wine skins.\n\n- Exclusive identifying items through Rogues.</size>\n\n\n\n<align="center"><color=@#Heading#><b>Differences - Detailed</b></align>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Adjusted Items & Spells</b></color>\n\nEach game within the trilogy has their own set of items and spells. In the remaster all generic (not specialised) items and spells have been merged to be found across all three volumes of the trilogy. This option reverts all items and spells to be specific to the games they were originally found in.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Special Slot</b></color>\n\nSpecial slot (S) is a slot in the party roster specifically for monsters and summoned creatures. No other party member or character can be in this slot. Having misc game differences toggled <b><u>on</u></b> will add a special slot to front the party roster leaving 6 spaces for characters - exclusively in Tales of the Unknown. Having this option turned off will maintain the party roster having 7 available spaces for characters, monsters and summoned creatures across all three games of the trilogy.\n\n\n\n<pos=0f%>S</pos><pos=2.5f%>Summoned Wolf</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=37.25f%><color=#141414>11/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>13</color></pos><pos=53f%></pos><color=#141414>?/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>?</color><pos=65f%>IL</pos>\n\n<pos=0.25f%>1</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Brian the Fist#</pos><pos=25f%>-10</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>108/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>258</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#WA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>2</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#El Cid#</pos><pos=25f%>-8</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>215/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>226</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#PA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>3</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Markus#</pos><pos=25f%>-4</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>176/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>198</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#BA#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>4</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Sir Grady#</pos><pos=25f%>-5</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>178/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>215</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#RO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>5</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\n<pos=0f%>6</pos><pos=2.5f%>@#Omar#</pos><pos=25.5f%>2</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>163</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n\n\nSpecials cannot be controlled in battle. They choose their own attack mode and generally go after the primary group of monsters the party is facing. Specials are removed from the S slot when killed. Illusionary specials (created by a <l="MAN_MAGES","mage"/>) are removed if any foe disbelieve them. If a non-illusionary special is attacked by another member of the party for any reason, it will turn hostile immediately and fight until defeated.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>No Archery</b></color>\n\nRemoves all forms of Archery and archery specific items that were not in the original Tales of the Unknown.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>No Item Charges</b></color>\n\nItems no longer have a specific amount of charges (uses) and are now reverted to being potentially infinite with a small chance to disappear on each use.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Different Game Sized Inventories</b></color>\n\nEach character has their own inventory and holds their own gold, the same as <l="MAN_INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY","individual inventory"/>. But each game has different sized inventories instead of the consistent 16 across all 3 of the trilogy.\n\n\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b></color> - Each member has <u>8</u> inventory slots.\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b></color> - Each member has <u>10</u> inventory slots.\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>Thief of Fate (BT3)</b></color> - Each member has <u>16</u> inventory slots.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Exclusive Wineskins</b></color>\n\nWine skins become exclusive again to the Thief of Fate within the trilogy, no longer being accessible from Tales of the Unknown or The Destiny Knight.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Exclusive Item Identification</b></color>\n\nItem identification through a Rogues abilities becomes restricted to the Thief of Fate and no longer available in Tales of the Unknown or The Destiny Knight.=ZH=雜項遊戲差異包含各遊戲之間的多項差異，但會套用所有列出的變更。\n\n\n\n<align="center"><color=@#Heading#><b>差異摘要</b></color></align>\n\n<smallcaps><b>所有遊戲</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- 物品與法術已調整以符合各遊戲。</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>未知傳奇（BT1）</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- 怪物與召喚生物專用特殊欄位。\n\n- 無弓箭。\n\n- 無物品使用次數。物品每次使用時有低機率消失。\n\n- 個人道具欄提供每位角色 XTAGX17</b>18XTAGX 格道具欄位。</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>天命勇士（BT2）</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- 個人道具欄提供每位角色 XTAGX25<size=85%>26XTAGX 格道具欄位。</size>\n\n<smallcaps><b>命運之賊（BT3）</b></smallcaps><size=85%>\n\n- 專屬酒囊。\n\n- 專屬透過盜賊鑑定物品。</size>\n\n\n\n<align="center"><color=@#Heading#><b>差異詳解</b></align>\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>調整物品與法術</b></color>\n\n三部曲中的每款遊戲都有其專屬的物品與法術。在重製版中，所有通用（非專屬）物品與法術已合併，可在三部曲的所有三卷中找到。此選項會將所有物品與法術還原為最初在各自遊戲中出現的專屬版本。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>特殊欄位</b></color>\n\n特殊欄位（S）是隊伍名冊中專門用於怪物與召喚生物的欄位。其他隊伍成員或角色不得佔用此欄位。啟用雜項遊戲差異 <b><u>開啟</u></b> 時，將在隊伍名冊前端新增一個特殊欄位，僅在《未知傳奇》中為角色留下 6 個空間。關閉此選項則會在三部曲的所有三款遊戲中，維持隊伍名冊有 7 個可用空間供角色、怪物與召喚生物使用。\n\n\n\n<pos=0f%>S</pos><pos=2.5f%>召喚狼</pos>XTAGX55<b>56XTAGX<pos=37.25f%><color=#141414>11/</color>XTAGX60<b>61XTAGX</pos><pos=53f%></pos><color=#141414>?/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>?</color><pos=65f%>IL</pos>\n\nXTAGX71<color=@#Heading#>72XTAGX<pos=2.5f%>@#Brian Grady#</pos><pos=25f%>-5</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>178/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>215</color></pos><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#RO#</pos>\n\nXTAGX135</align>136XTAGX<pos=2.5f%>@#Merlin#</pos><pos=25f%>-1</pos><pos=35f%><color=#141414>145/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>145</color><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>409/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>512</color><pos=50f%></pos><pos=65f%>@#CO#</pos>\n\nXTAGX156<smallcaps>157XTAGX<pos=2.5f%>@#Omar#</pos>XTAGX160<b>161XTAGX<pos=35f%><color=#141414>163/</color>XTAGX165<color=#141414>166XTAGX</pos><pos=50f%></pos><color=#141414>367/</color><color=#F0F0F0FF>571</color><pos=65f%>@#MA#</pos>\n\n\n\n特殊單位無法在戰鬥中控制。它們會自行選擇攻擊模式，通常會攻擊隊伍正在面對的主要怪物群。特殊單位被殺死時會從 S 欄位移除。幻象特殊單位（由 <l="MAN_MAGES","mage"/> 創造）若被任何敵人識破則會被移除。若非幻象的特殊單位因任何原因受到隊伍其他成員攻擊，它會立即轉為敵對並戰鬥至被擊敗。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>無弓箭</b></color>\n\n移除所有形式的弓箭以及原版《未知傳奇》中不存在的弓箭專用物品。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>無物品使用次數</b></color>\n\n物品不再有特定的使用次數，現在還原為可能無限使用，但每次使用時有低機率消失。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>不同遊戲尺寸的道具欄</b></color>\n\n每位角色擁有自己的道具欄並持有自己的金幣，與 <l="MAN_INDIVIDUAL_INVENTORY","individual inventory"/> 相同。但每款遊戲的道具欄尺寸不同，而非三部曲中統一的 16 格。\n\n\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>未知傳奇（BT1）</b></color> - 每位成員有 XTAGX194</b>195XTAGX 格道具欄位。\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>天命勇士（BT2）</b></color> - 每位成員有 XTAGX200<size=85%>201XTAGX 格道具欄位。\n\n<color=@#Heading#><b>命運之賊（BT3）</b></color> - 每位成員有 XTAGX206<size=85%>207XTAGX 格道具欄位。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>專屬酒囊</b></color>\n\n酒囊再次成為《命運之賊》在三部曲中的專屬物品，不再能從《未知傳奇》或《天命勇士》中取得。\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>專屬物品鑑定</b></color>\n\n透過盜賊能力鑑定物品的功能將限制於《命運之賊》，不再適用於《未知傳奇》或《天命勇士》。

Finishing A Volume=ZH=完成一部曲
When a volume of the trilogy has been completed and there is nothing of value left to do, head to any <l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/>. A new option will be present which will advance the game to the next volume in the trilogy. This option will only occur in <b>Tales of the Unknown (BT1)</b> and <b>The Destiny Knight (BT2)</b>.\n\n\n\nFinishing <b>Tales of the Unknown</b> will present -\n\nBegin Volume II - The Destiny Knight.\n\n\n\nFinishing <b>The Destiny Knight</b> will present -\n\nBegin Volume III - Thief of Fate.\n\n\n\nFinishing a volume does not require forming a new party, losing all gold and emptying all inventories. A <l="MAN_CHARACTER_TRANSFER","character transfer"/> option is available.\n\n\n\nCharacters can always be left behind at a guild and new ones created to take their place if the party needs a bit of a different composition. Alternatively - exit to the main menu and start an entirely fresh save for the new volume.\n\n\n\nIt is recommended to create a save file before transferring to a new volume.=ZH=當三部曲中的某一卷完成，且沒有其他有價值的事情可做時，前往任何一座 <l="MAN_ADVENTURERS_GUILD","Adventurer's Guild"/>。將會出現一個新選項，讓遊戲推進至三部曲的下一卷。此選項僅會出現在 <b>《未知傳奇》（BT1）</b> 與 <b>《天命勇士》（BT2）</b> 中。\n\n完成 <b>《未知傳奇》</b> 將會出現——\n\n開始第二卷——天命勇士。\n\n完成 <b>《天命勇士》</b> 將會出現——\n\n開始第三卷——命運之賊。\n\n完成一卷並不需要重新組建隊伍、損失所有金幣或清空所有道具欄。有 <l="MAN_CHARACTER_TRANSFER","character transfer"/> 選項可供使用。\n\n角色隨時可以留在公會中，並創建新角色來取代他們的位置，如果隊伍需要稍微不同的組成。或者，退出到主選單，為新卷從頭開始全新的存檔。\n\n建議在轉移至新卷之前建立一個存檔。
Character Transfer=ZH=角色轉移
When advancing to a new volume, there are 3 different options that allow characters to be transferred at different power levels and to retain different amounts of loot.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Full Power Transfer</b></color>\n\nBring over all characters exactly as they are with all experience and items. All inventories will be preserved.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Moderate Power Transfer</b></color>\n\nCharacters will be levelled down to a suitable level for the content. All inventories will be preserved.\n\n\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>Full Reset</b></color>\n\nAll characters will be reset to level 1. All inventories will be cleared.\n\n\n\nTransferring characters across volumes will also transfer characters in the guild roster.\n\n\n\nIt is recommended to create a save file before transferring to a new volume.=ZH=當推進至新卷時，有 3 種不同的選項，允許角色以不同的強度等級轉移，並保留不同數量的戰利品。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>完整強度轉移</b></color>\n\n將所有角色原封不動地帶過來，保留所有經驗值與物品。所有道具欄都將保留。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>中等強度轉移</b></color>\n\n角色將降級至適合該內容的等級。所有道具欄都將保留。\n\n<color=@#SubHeading#><b>完全重置</b></color>\n\n所有角色將重置為等級 1。所有道具欄都將清空。\n\n跨卷轉移角色也會轉移公會名冊中的角色。\n\n建議在轉移至新卷之前建立一個存檔。

The Destiny Knight is immune to all status conditions and cannot be turned to stoned or killed with a critical hit. Any damage to the Destiny Knight that would normally kill them, will instead reduce a small amount of their spell points. If they run out of magic, they can still die!=ZH=天命勇士免疫所有狀態異常，無法被石化，也無法被暴擊殺死。任何原本會殺死天命勇士的傷害，都只會減少其少量法術點數。如果他們耗盡魔力，仍然會死亡！
